Option Explicit

Const cstrModuleName As String = "ModTracks"

Public aValue As Single
Public bValue As Single
Public cValue As Single
Private mValue As Single
Public McValue As Single
Private LValue As Single
Public ResultArr(3) As Integer
Public ResultLagQTY(3) As Integer
Public ReqQty(1) As Integer
Private AvailShaftSpace As Single
Private couplerValue As Integer

Private Type RHM
    Sprg_wireSize As Single
    RHM As Single
End Type

Private Type LHM
    Sprg_wireSize As Single
    LHM As Single
End Type

Private Type MountData
    SB_LHM() As LHM
    SB_RHM() As RHM
End Type

Private SpringMountSide As MountData
Sub Calculate()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".Calculate()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim LenHT As Integer
    Dim VTrack As Boolean
    Dim VT As String
    Dim HT As String
    Dim LenDHT As Integer
      
    Select Case LiftType
       Case "Standard Lift"
           If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call SelectHardwareSize
           Else         'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               VT = "LowerTrack"
               Call TaperedADCA_VerticalTrack(VT)        ' Euorpean ADCA
           End If
           
           If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
               Call selectStdLiftOnePieceHT              ' For 1-Piece HT & also for European - 1-Piece HT
           Else
               Call selectCurvesHT
               LenHT = selectHTracks
               'add new schedule on May 16, 2003 'select Horizontal Track for sep. curve
               Call selectSepCurves(LenHT)
               Call selectHTCSplice
           End If
           
           If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount" Then
               Call GetBracketsStandard
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "ADCA" Then
               Call selectADCAPN
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call SelectTaperedADCAPN
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Clip angle" Then
               Call selectLowerWallAnglePN
           End If
                 
           'distance from face of jamb to external edge of track
           aValue = GetBracketMountA()
           
           'distance from face of jamb to external edge of track at splice of vt & HT curve
           bValue = GetBracketMountB()
           mValue = GetBracketMountM()
           
           'distance from end of HA to the first fastening loc. b/w HT & track
           McValue = GetBracketMountMc()
           
           selectFullHorzAngles
           LValue = DistanceLCalculation()
               
       Case "High Lift"
       
           If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call selectUpperTrack
           Else '            If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call TaperedADCA_UpperTrack
               Call TaperedADCA_VerticalTrack("LowerTrack")
           End If
           
           If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
               Call selectHighLiftOnePieceHT
           Else
               Call selectCurvesHT
               LenHT = selectHTracks
               Call selectSepCurves(LenHT)  'select Horizontal Track for sep. curve
               Call selectHTCSplice
           End If
           
            If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount" Then
                Call GetBracketsStandard
            ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "ADCA" Then
                Call selectADCAPN
            ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                Call SelectTaperedADCAPN
            ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Clip angle" Then
                Call selectLowerWallAnglePN
            End If
            
            aValue = GetBracketMountA()
            bValue = GetBracketMountB()
            cValue = GetBracketMountC()
            
            If varDoorData.Track.strLowerSplice = "w/ Upper Wall Angle" Then
                
                If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                    Call LowerSpliceBracket_UWA
                    Call selectUpperWallAngle

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                    Call selectUWATrackCips
''''''''''END Change in .NET
     
                Else                                    'For EU ADCA
                    Call selectEU_UpperWallAngle

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                    Call selectEU_UWATrackClips
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                      
                End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                Call selectUWATrackCips
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strLowerSplice = "w/ Upper Bracket Mount" Then
                Call selectUpperBrackets
                Call LowerSpliceBracket_UBrktMount
            End If
            
            Call UpperSplice_HL
            
            mValue = GetBracketMountM()
            McValue = GetBracketMountMc()
            
            selectFullHorzAngles
            LValue = DistanceLCalculation()
                
       Case "Vertical Lift"
       
           If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call SelectHardwareSize
               Call selectUpperTrack
           Else                 'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call TaperedADCA_UpperTrack
               Call TaperedADCA_VerticalTrack("LowerTrack")
           End If
           
           If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount" Then
               Call GetBracketsStandard
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "ADCA" Then
                Call selectADCAPN
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                Call SelectTaperedADCAPN
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Clip angle" Then
               Call selectLowerWallAnglePN
           End If
           
           aValue = GetBracketMountA()              'Jamb Weathering ADCA, Clip Angle
           bValue = GetBracketMountB()
           cValue = GetBracketMountC()
           
           If varDoorData.Track.strLowerSplice = "w/ Upper Wall Angle" Then
               If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                   Call LowerSpliceBracket_UWA      'only used in North American
                   
'                   Call selectUpperWallAngle   'being executed after drum selection

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                   Call selectUWATrackCips     'being executed after drum selection
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                   
                Else                'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
'                   Call selectEU_UpperWallAngle    'being executed after drum selection
                   
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                   Call selectEU_UWATrackClips     'being executed after drum selection
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                   
                End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                Call selectUWATrackCips
''''''''''END Change in .NET
               
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strLowerSplice = "w/ Upper Bracket Mount" Then
'               Call selectUpperBrackets
               'Call selectUpperBrackets_VL_VLHRVL  'being executed after drum selection
               Call LowerSpliceBracket_UBrktMount
           End If
          
           'Call VLTrackSupport now execute this routine after the drum selection
           
       Case "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
       
           If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call SelectHardwareSize
               Call selectUpperTrack
           Else                 'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call TaperedADCA_UpperTrack
               Call TaperedADCA_VerticalTrack("LowerTrack")
           End If
           
           If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount" Then
               Call GetBracketsStandard
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "ADCA" Then
               Call selectADCAPN
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                Call SelectTaperedADCAPN
           Else
               Call selectLowerWallAnglePN
           End If
                      
           aValue = GetBracketMountA()              ''''''Jamb Weathering ADCA, Clip Angle
           bValue = GetBracketMountB()
           cValue = GetBracketMountC()
           
           If varDoorData.Track.strLowerSplice = "w/ Upper Wall Angle" Then
               
               If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                   Call LowerSpliceBracket_UWA
                   Call selectUpperWallAngle

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                   Call selectUWATrackCips
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                   
               Else                    'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                   Call selectEU_UpperWallAngle

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                   Call selectEU_UWATrackClips
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                   
               End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Call selectUWATrackCips
''''''''''END Change in .NET
               
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strLowerSplice = "w/ Upper Bracket Mount" Then
               'Call selectUpperBrackets
               'Call selectUpperBrackets_VL_VLHRVL  'being executed after drum selection
               Call LowerSpliceBracket_UBrktMount
           End If
           
           'Call VLTrackSupport now execute this routine after the drum selection
           
       Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
       
           If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               Call SelectHardwareSize              'select NA vertical tracks
               Call selectHRZTracks_LHR             'select NA horizontal tracks
           Else                     'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
               VT = "LowerVT"
               Call TaperedADCA_VerticalTrack(VT)   'select EU Vertical tracks
               Call selectHRZTracks_LHR             'selects EU Horizontal tracks
           End If
           
           If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount" Then
               Call GetBracketsStandard
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "ADCA" Then
                Call selectADCAPN
           ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                Call SelectTaperedADCAPN         'European ADCA part no. is being selected
           Else    'Clip angle
               Call selectLowerWallAnglePN
           End If
           
           aValue = GetBracketMountA()
           bValue = GetBracketMountB()
           
           Call kit_wearhouse
    
        End Select
    
'    If varDoorData.Track.strJambType = "Wood" Then Call SelectLagBolts
        
    Select Case varDoorData.Track.strMountType
    Case "ADCA", "Clip angle"
        If varDoorData.Track.strWeatherStrip = "ADCA Mount Jamb Weather Seal" Then
            Call selectJambWeatheringStrip
        End If
        
    Case "EU ADCA"
        If Not LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
            If varDoorData.Track.strWeatherStrip = "EU Jamb Weather Seal" Then
                Call selectEUJambWeatherSeal

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
            ElseIf varDoorData.Track.strWeatherStrip = "ADCA Mount Jamb Weather Seal" Then
                Call selectJambWeatheringStrip
''''''''''END Change in .NET
            End If
        End If
    End Select
    If Not (LiftType = "Vertical Lift") And Not (LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift") Then Call SelectTrackPkgs

    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub

Private Function GetCouplerAmt() As Boolean
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetCouplerAmt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim WireType As String
    Dim NoSprg As Integer
    Dim lsngWireSize As Single
    Dim lsngActiveCoils As Single
    Dim lsngDeadcoils As Single
    Dim lsngsqfact As Single
    Dim lsngSprSpace As Single
    Dim AvailableShaft As Single
    Dim DrumW As Single
    Dim blTight As Boolean
    Dim BigQty As Integer
    Dim SmQty As Integer
    Dim BigWirS As Single
    Dim bigActiveCoils As Single
    Dim Deadcoils As Integer
    Dim Fact As Single
    Dim bigSprSpace As Single
    Dim smSprSpace As Single
    Dim lblTight As Boolean
    
    
    DrumW = GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping)
    AvailableShaft = GetAvailableShaft(DrumW)
    
    If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).blnMismatched = False Then
        
        NoSprg = UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring()) + 1
        
        lsngWireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize
        lsngActiveCoils = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).AC
        lsngDeadcoils = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringDC
        lsngsqfact = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize, _
                    varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
        
        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).strSpringWireType = "Round" Then
            lsngSprSpace = NoSprg * ((lsngActiveCoils + lsngDeadcoils + Winds) * lsngWireSize + 6) '(3 for plugs and centerplate, 3 for key)
        Else
            lsngSprSpace = NoSprg * ((lsngActiveCoils + lsngDeadcoils + Winds) * lsngWireSize * lsngsqfact + 6) '(3 for plugs and centerplate, 3 for key)
        End If
        
        If lsngSprSpace <= AvailableShaft - (CouplNum * 6) Then
            lblTight = True
            If lsngSprSpace <= AvailableShaft - (CouplNum * 8) Then lblTight = False
        End If
        
    Else    'mismatch
        SmQty = GetSelectedSprQtyPerGauge(SpringIndex, varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize)
        
        Dim smWirS As Single
        Dim smActiveCoils As Single
        
        smWirS = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize
        smActiveCoils = varDoorData.arrSpringSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).AC
        Deadcoils = varDoorData.arrSpringSolution(SpringIndex).DC
        Fact = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize, _
               varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
        
        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).strSpringWireType = "Round" Then
            smSprSpace = SmQty * ((smActiveCoils + Deadcoils + Winds) * smWirS + 6) '(3 for plugs and centerplate, 3 for key)
        Else
            smSprSpace = SmQty * ((smActiveCoils + Deadcoils + Winds) * smWirS * Fact + 6) '(3 for plugs and centerplate, 3 for key)
        End If
        
        Deadcoils = 0
        Fact = 0
        
        BigQty = GetSelectedSprQtyPerGauge(SpringIndex, varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize)
        BigWirS = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize
        bigActiveCoils = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).AC
        Deadcoils = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringDC
        Fact = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize, _
                varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
        
        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).strSpringWireType = "Round" Then
            bigSprSpace = BigQty * ((bigActiveCoils + Deadcoils + Winds) * BigWirS + 6) '(3 for plugs and centerplate, 3 for key)
        Else
            bigSprSpace = BigQty * ((bigActiveCoils + Deadcoils + Winds) * BigWirS * Fact + 6) '(3 for plugs and centerplate, 3 for key)
        End If
        
        If smSprSpace + bigSprSpace <= AvailableShaft - (CouplNum * 6) Then
            blTight = True
            If smSprSpace + bigSprSpace <= AvailableShaft - (CouplNum * 8) Then blTight = False 'if springs fit on the shaft
        End If
    End If
    
    GetCouplerAmt = blTight
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub GetHorizontalTrackLength(DrHgt As Single)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetHorizontalTrackLength()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim HorizontalTrackLength As Double
    Dim DHeightCheck As Single

    If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = False Then
        HorizontalTrackLength = DrHgt - 18
        'TotalTrackLength = HorizontalTrackLength + 15.75
    Else
        ' this will round the door height to even foot measurment
        ' and will add 3 0r 2 inches in order to get Horizontal track length
        
        DHeightCheck = DrHgt
            If DHeightCheck / 12 = Int(DHeightCheck / 12) Then
                DHeightCheck = DHeightCheck + 12
            Else
                DHeightCheck = Int(DHeightCheck \ 12 + 1) * 12
            End If
            
            If HWType = 2 Then
                HorizontalTrackLength = DHeightCheck + 3
            Else
                HorizontalTrackLength = DHeightCheck + 3
            End If
        'TotalTrackLength = HorizontalTrackLength + 15.75
    End If

    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub

' Implements New cutting instructions for :
' Lower Vertical Track for SL
' Upper & Lower Vertical Track for High and VL lift types

Function cutVerticalTrack(ByVal VerticalTrackLen As Integer, trkQTY As Integer, Optional PNTag As String) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".cutVerticalTrack()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim CutlenLowerTrack As Single
    Dim CutlenUpperTrack As Single
    Dim cutOff As Single
    Dim cutoffUVT As Single
    Dim cutoffLVT As String
    Dim UpperVTLen As Integer
    Dim cutTrack As String
    Dim TrackLen As String
    Dim lstrTrackType As String
    Dim cutInst As String
    Dim lintTop As Integer
    Dim lsngBtm As Single
    Dim cutFromTop As String
    Dim cutFromBtm As String
    Dim lsngCutOff As Single
    Dim lsngCutTo As Single
    
    lstrTrackType = "LowerVT"
    If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then   'ADCA
        Select Case LiftType
            Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"

                If PNTag = "LowerTrack" Then   '                If LiftType = "Standard Lift" Then
                    CutlenLowerTrack = DrHgt - 6        'cut to amount
                    If trkQTY = 1 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(CutlenLowerTrack - Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)) + Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)
'                            cutInst = "Cut(1) " & "to (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo) & " from each end"
                            cutInst = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
                         Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutlenLowerTrack
                            aCon.mmcalc
'                            cutInst = "Cut(1) " & "to (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm" & " from each end"
                            cutInst = "Cut (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                         End If
                    Else 'QTY = 2
                        cutOff = VerticalTrackLen - CutlenLowerTrack
                        If cutOff > 0 Then
                            ' calculates cut to's amount
                            If MeasU = 1 Then
                                lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(CutlenLowerTrack - Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)) + Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)
                                cutoffLVT = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
                            Else
                                Call ClearConObj
                                aCon.Inch = CutlenLowerTrack
                                aCon.mmcalc

                                ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                                cutoffLVT = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                cutoffLVT = "Cut (2) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                ''''''''''END Change in .NET
                            End If
                            
                            ' calculates cutoff amount
                            If cutOff <= 6 Or (cutOff > 6 And cutOff <= 6.5) Then 'cut bottom only
                                Call ClearConObj
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = cutOff
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(cutOff - Fix(cutOff)) + Fix(cutOff)
                                    TrackLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = TrackLen & " from bottom"
                                
                            ElseIf cutOff > 6 Then                     '(cutOff Mod 6) = 0 Then  'multiplie of 6"
                                Call ClearConObj
                                'cut from top
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = 5
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    TrackLen = GetImpString(5)
                                End If
                                cutFromTop = TrackLen & " from top"
                                
                                'cut from bottom
                                lsngBtm = cutOff - 5
                                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                        aCon.mmcalc
                                        TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                    Else
                                        lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                        TrackLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                    End If
                                    cutFromBtm = TrackLen & " from bottom"
                                End If
                            End If
                            If cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                                cutInst = "BOTH" & "~" & cutoffLVT & "~" & cutFromTop & "; " & cutFromBtm
                            ElseIf cutFromTop = "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                                cutInst = "BTM" & "~" & cutoffLVT & "~" & cutFromBtm
                            ElseIf cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm = "" Then
                                cutInst = "TOP" & "~" & cutoffLVT & "~" & cutFromTop
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If ' QTY
                Else        ' UpperTrack for HL, LHR-VL, VL
                    If LiftType = "High Lift" Then
                        CutlenUpperTrack = HILIFT - 9
                    ElseIf LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Or LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
                        CutlenUpperTrack = DrHgt + 18
                    End If
                    
                    'upper vertical track
                    'cut to's amount
                    
                    ' Qty = 1
                    If trkQTY = 1 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
'                            cutInst = "Cut(1) " & "to (2) " & GetImpString(CutlenUpperTrack) & " from each end"
                            cutInst = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(CutlenUpperTrack)
                         Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutlenUpperTrack
                            aCon.mmcalc
'                            cutInst = "Cut(1) " & " to (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm" & " from each end"
                            cutInst = "Cut (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                         End If
                            
                    Else  'Qty = 2
                        
                        cutOff = VerticalTrackLen - CutlenUpperTrack    'cut Upper Vertical Track
                        If cutOff > 0 Then
                            If MeasU = 1 Then
                                cutInst = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(CutlenUpperTrack) '& " ~ " & " Cut " & GetImpString(cutOff) & " from top"
                            Else
                                aCon.Inch = CutlenUpperTrack
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                cutInst = "Cut (2) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm" '& " ~ " & " Cut " & cutoffUVT & " from top"
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                
            Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    CutlenLowerTrack = DrHgt - 11
                 Else
                    CutlenLowerTrack = DrHgt - 14
                 End If
                
                If trkQTY = 1 Then
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(CutlenLowerTrack - Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)) + Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)
'                        cutInst = "Cut(1) " & "to (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo) & " from each end"
                        cutInst = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
                     Else
                        aCon.Inch = CutlenLowerTrack
                        aCon.mmcalc
'                        cutInst = "Cut(1) " & "to (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm" & " from each end"
                        cutInst = "Cut (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                     End If
                Else 'QTY = 2
                        
                    'rem cutoff amount
                    cutOff = VerticalTrackLen - CutlenLowerTrack
                    
                    If cutOff > 0 Then
                        ' calculates cut to's amount
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(CutlenLowerTrack - Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)) + Fix(CutlenLowerTrack)

                            ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                            cutoffLVT = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
                            cutoffLVT = "Cut (2) vertical track to " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
                            ''''''''''END Change in .NET
                        Else
                            Call ClearConObj
                            aCon.Inch = CutlenLowerTrack
                            aCon.mmcalc
                        
                            ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                            cutoffLVT = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            cutoffLVT = "Cut (2) vertical track to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            ''''''''''END Change in .NET
                        End If
                        
                        ' calculates cutoff amount
                        If cutOff <= 6 Or (cutOff > 6 And cutOff <= 6.5) Then  'cut bottom only
                            Call ClearConObj
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = cutOff
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(cutOff - Fix(cutOff)) + Fix(cutOff)
                                TrackLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                            End If
                            cutFromBtm = TrackLen & " from bottom"
                            
                        ElseIf cutOff > 6 Then
                            Call ClearConObj
                            'cut from top

                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 5
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                TrackLen = GetImpString(5)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = TrackLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from bottom
                            lsngBtm = cutOff - 5
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    TrackLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = TrackLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        End If
                        If cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                            cutInst = "BOTH" & "~" & cutoffLVT & "~" & cutFromTop & "; " & cutFromBtm
                        ElseIf cutFromTop = "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                            cutInst = "BTM" & "~" & cutoffLVT & "~" & cutFromBtm
                        ElseIf cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm = "" Then
                            cutInst = "TOP" & "~" & cutoffLVT & "~" & cutFromTop
                        ElseIf cutFromTop = "" And cutFromBtm = "" Then
                            cutInst = cutoffLVT
                        End If
                    End If
                End If  ' qty
           End Select
    
    Else     'European
        Select Case LiftType
        Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"    'for all hardware sizes
            If PNTag = "LowerTrack" Then
                cutOff = VerticalTrackLen + 6 - DrHgt
                If cutOff > 0 Then
                    Call ClearConObj
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = cutOff
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(cutOff - Fix(cutOff)) + Fix(cutOff)
                        TrackLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                    End If
                    cutInst = TrackLen & " from bottom"
                End If
            Else        ' UpperTrack for HL, LHR-VL, VL
                If LiftType = "High Lift" Then
                    CutlenUpperTrack = HILIFT - 9
                ElseIf LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Or LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
                     CutlenUpperTrack = DrHgt + 18   'cut Upper Vertical Track
                End If
                
'                cutOff = VerticalTrackLen - CutlenUpperTrack    'cut Upper Vertical Track
'                If cutOff > 0 Then
'                    Call ClearConObj
'                    If MeasU = 0 Then
'                        aCon.Inch = cutOff
'                        aCon.mmcalc
'                        TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
'                    Else
'                        lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(cutOff - Fix(cutOff)) + Fix(cutOff)
'                        TrackLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
'                    End If
'                    cutInst = TrackLen & " from top or from each end"
'                End If
                
                'NOTE: In 2.0.13 release, we are following the Bill's provided format of EU ADCA - Upper track cut instructions for these lift types.
                '       For LHR-F/R mount types, we are following the EU engineering drawings.
                
                ' Qty = 1
                If trkQTY = 1 Then
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        cutInst = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(CutlenUpperTrack)
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = CutlenUpperTrack
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutInst = "Cut (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                        
                Else  'Qty = 2
                    
                    cutOff = VerticalTrackLen - CutlenUpperTrack    'cut Upper Vertical Track
                    If cutOff > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            cutInst = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(CutlenUpperTrack) '& " ~ " & " Cut " & GetImpString(cutOff) & " from top"
                        Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutlenUpperTrack
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            cutInst = "Cut (2) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm" '& " ~ " & " Cut " & cutoffUVT & " from top"
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If

        Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"  'Need to revisit cut instructions after FP release
            cutOff = VerticalTrackLen + 6 - DrHgt
            Call ClearConObj
            If MeasU = 0 Then
                aCon.Inch = cutOff
                aCon.mmcalc
                TrackLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                If cutOff > 0 Then
                    If HWType = 2 Then
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "127 mm" & " from top" & _
                                    Chr$(32) & ";  " & TrackLen & " from bottom"
                    Else
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "203 mm" & " from top" & _
                                    Chr$(32) & ";  " & TrackLen & " from bottom"
                    End If
                Else
                    If HWType = 2 Then
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "127 mm " & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "203 mm " & " from top"
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                If cutOff > 0 Then
                    If HWType = 2 Then
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "5" & Chr$(34) & " from top" & _
                                Chr$(32) & ";  " & cutOff & Chr(34) & " from bottom"
                    Else
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "8" & Chr$(34) & " from top" & _
                                Chr$(32) & ";  " & cutOff & Chr(34) & " from bottom"
                    End If
                Else
                    If HWType = 2 Then
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "5" & Chr$(34) & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutInst = "vertical track " & "8" & Chr$(34) & " from top"
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End Select
   End If
   
   cutVerticalTrack = cutInst
    
   Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

Sub GetBracketsStandard()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBracketsStandard()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    ' this routine picks up Part no., QTY and SubGroup for Track Bracket according to the selected H/W Size, DH and DT criteria
    ' Array used to hold Part no, QTY, SubGroup value based on Track size, door height and door type

    Dim TrackBracketsPN1(3) As String
    Dim TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) As Integer
    Dim DoorType As String
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
    Dim FN As Integer
    
    If Left(DrType, 4) = "A175" Or DrType = "T175A" Or DrType = "T175-20" Or DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T45" Then
        DoorType = "T175"
    ElseIf Left(DrType, 2) = "SR" Then
        DoorType = "T200"
    ElseIf Left(DrType, 3) = "ADV" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Then
        DoorType = "T200"
    ElseIf DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
        DoorType = "T150"
    Else
        DoorType = DrType
    End If
    
    Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If DrHgt <= 96 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175", "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
'                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
'                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        If DoorType = "T35" Or DoorType = "T140" Or DoorType = "T138" Then
                            
                            TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 6
                            
                            TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "327-101"
                            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
                        
                        Else
                            TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                            
                        End If
                        
                        CB = 8
                End Select
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175", "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
'                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
'                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 10

                        If DoorType = "T35" Or DoorType = "T140" Or DoorType = "T138" Then

                            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                            
                            TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "327-101"
                            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
                        
                        Else
                        
                            TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 10
                        
                        End If
                        
                        CB = 10
                End Select
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175", "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
'                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
'                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 10

                            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 4

                        CB = 16
                        
                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"

                 Select Case DoorType

                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"

        Case 3, 3.25

                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        CB = 8
                End Select
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175", "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"

                        CB = 10

                End Select
                    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175"

                        CB = 12
                        
                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"

                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
                        
                        CB = 12
                        
                End Select
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "111-1470"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 4
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
                        
                        CB = 14
            
                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"

            ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175"

                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"

                        CB = 16
                        
                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"

                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 6
                        
                        CB = 16
                End Select
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "111-1470"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 4
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 6
                        
                        CB = 18
                        
                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "111-1470"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 8
                        
                        CB = 18
                End Select
                  
            ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "111-1470"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 4
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 8
                        
                        CB = 20
                        
                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "111-1470"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(3) = "111-1469"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
                        
                        CB = 20
                End Select
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 264 Then
                Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T35", "T138", "T140", "T165", "T150", "T175"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "111-1470"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 4
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(3) = "111-1469"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
                        
                        CB = 22
                       
                    Case "T200", "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
                        TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "111-1466A"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(1) = "111-1470"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(2) = "111-1468"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 8
                        
                        TrackBracketsPN1(3) = "111-1469"
                        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 4
                        
                        CB = 22
                End Select
            End If
    End Select
    
    If TrackBracketsPN1(0) = "" Then
        MsgBox "Cannot select Bracket Mount. " & vbCrLf & _
                "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot select Bracket Mount"
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 3
        If TrackBracketsPN1(i) <> "" And TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i) <> Empty Then
            FillSummaryGrid TrackBracketsPN1(i), TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(TrackBracketsPN1(i)), 0
        End If
    Next
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

'   "a" value-bottom for lower vertical track for all lift types
Public Function GetBracketMountA() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBracketMountA()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim JambWeatherSeal As String
    Dim DoorType As String
    Dim BracketMountA As Single

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''

'    If Left(DrType, 4) = "A175" Or DrType = "T175A" Then
    If Left(DrType, 4) = "A175" Or DrType = "T175A" Or DrType = "T175-20" Or _
        DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T45" Or DrType = "T35" Then
        DoorType = "T175"
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    ElseIf Left(DrType, 2) = "SR" Then
        DoorType = "T200"
    ElseIf Left(DrType, 3) = "ADV" Or DrType = "T200A" Then
        DoorType = "T200"
    ElseIf DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
        DoorType = "T150"
    ElseIf Left(DrType, 4) = "T300" Then  'For T300 aValue is  AdV200 + add 1"
        DoorType = "T300"
  
    Else
        DoorType = DrType
    End If
    
    ' same for standard & High Lift
    If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
        If HWType = 2 Then
            If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount" Then
                Select Case DoorType
                Case "T140", "T138"
                    BracketMountA = 0.9375  'T150 - .125
                Case "T150"
                    BracketMountA = 1.0625
                Case "T165"
                    BracketMountA = 1.1875  'T175 - .125
                Case "T175"
                    BracketMountA = 1.3125
                Case "T200"
                    BracketMountA = 1.5625
                Case "T300"
                    BracketMountA = 1.5625 + 1

                End Select
            Else ' adca or clip
                If varDoorData.Track.strJambType = "Steel" Or varDoorData.Track.strJambType = "Masonry" Then
                    If varDoorData.Track.blnWeatherStrip = False Then
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 1
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 1.125
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.25
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.375
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.625
                            
                        Case "T300"
                           BracketMountA = 1.625 + 1
                        End Select
                        
                    Else ' there is a jamb weathering
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 1.1875
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 1.3125
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.4375
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.5625
                        
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.8125
                        Case "T300"
                            BracketMountA = 1.8125 + 1
  
                            
                        End Select
                    End If
                Else ' wood jamb
                    If varDoorData.Track.blnWeatherStrip = False Then
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 0.9375
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 1.0625
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.1875
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.3125
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.5625
                        Case "T300"
                            BracketMountA = 1.5625 + 1
                            
                        End Select
                    Else ' there is a jamb weathering
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 1.125
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 1.25
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.375
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.5
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.75
                        Case "T300"
                           BracketMountA = 1.75 + 1
                            
                            
                        End Select
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Else    'HW = 3 or 3.25
            If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount" Then
                Select Case DoorType
                Case "T140", "T138"
                    BracketMountA = 0.8125
                Case "T150"
                    BracketMountA = 0.9375
                Case "T165"
                    BracketMountA = 1.0625
                Case "T175"
                    BracketMountA = 1.1875
                Case "T200"
                    BracketMountA = 1.4375
                Case "T300"
                    BracketMountA = 1.4375 + 1
                    
                End Select
            Else ' adca or clip
                If varDoorData.Track.strJambType = "Steel" Or varDoorData.Track.strJambType = "Masonry" Then
                    If varDoorData.Track.blnWeatherStrip = False Then
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 0.875
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 1
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.125
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.25
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.5
                        Case "T300"
                           BracketMountA = 1.5 + 1
                            
                        End Select
                    Else ' there is a jamb weathering
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 1.0625
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 1.1875
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.3125
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.4375
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.6875
                        Case "T300"
                           BracketMountA = 1.6875 + 1
                            
                        End Select
                    End If
                Else ' wood jamb
                    If varDoorData.Track.blnWeatherStrip = False Then
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 0.8125
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 0.9375
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.0625
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.1875
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.4375
                        Case "T300"
                           BracketMountA = 1.4375 + 1
                            
                            
                        End Select
                    Else ' there is a jamb weathering
                        Select Case DoorType
                        Case "T140", "T138"
                            BracketMountA = 1
                        Case "T150"
                            BracketMountA = 1.125
                        Case "T165"
                            BracketMountA = 1.25
                        Case "T175"
                            BracketMountA = 1.375
                        Case "T200"
                            BracketMountA = 1.625
                        Case "T300"
                           BracketMountA = 1.625 + 1
                            
                        End Select
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Else 'European ADCA
        If HWType = 2 Then
            'If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
                If varDoorData.Track.blnWeatherStrip = False Then
                    Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T140", "T138"
                        BracketMountA = 1.094
                    Case "T150"
                        BracketMountA = 1.219
                    Case "T165"
                        BracketMountA = 1.344
                    Case "T175"
                        BracketMountA = 1.469
                    Case "T200"
                        BracketMountA = 1.719
                    Case "T300"
                           BracketMountA = 1.719 + 1
                        
                    End Select
                    
                Else
                    Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T140", "T138"
                        BracketMountA = 1.313
                    Case "T150"
                        BracketMountA = 1.438
                    Case "T165"
                        BracketMountA = 1.563
                    Case "T175"
                        BracketMountA = 1.688
                    Case "T200"
                        BracketMountA = 1.938
                    Case "T300"
                         BracketMountA = 1.938 + 1
                        
                    End Select
                End If
            'End If
        Else
            If varDoorData.Track.blnWeatherStrip = False Then
                    Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T140", "T138"
                        BracketMountA = 0.969
                    Case "T150"
                        BracketMountA = 1.094
                    Case "T165"
                        BracketMountA = 1.219
                    Case "T175"
                        BracketMountA = 1.344
                    Case "T200"
                        BracketMountA = 1.594
                     Case "T300"
                         BracketMountA = 1.594 + 1
                        
                        
                    End Select
                    
                Else
                    Select Case DoorType
                    Case "T140", "T138"
                        BracketMountA = 1.188
                    Case "T150"
                        BracketMountA = 1.313
                    Case "T165"
                        BracketMountA = 1.438
                    Case "T175"
                        BracketMountA = 1.563
                    Case "T200"
                        BracketMountA = 1.813
                     Case "T300"
                         BracketMountA = 1.813 + 1
                        
                        
                    End Select
                End If
        End If
    End If
                        
    GetBracketMountA = BracketMountA
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

Public Function GetBracketMountM() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBracketMountM()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim BracketMountM As Single
    Dim noSection As Integer
    Dim i As Integer
    
    
'    If LiftType = "Standard Lift" Then
'    ' this is for standard lift
'        If HWType = 2 Then
'            If HRadius = 12 Then
'                If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
'                    BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1638) / 96
'                Else
'                    BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1914) / 96
'                End If
'            Else 'HRadius is 16"
'                If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
'                    BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1894) / 96
'                Else
'                    BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1914) / 96
'                End If
'            End If
'
'        ElseIf HWType = 3 Then
'            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1938) / 96
'            Else
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1914) / 96
'            End If
'        ElseIf HWType = 3.25 Then
'            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1938) / 96
'            Else
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + DrHgt + 1914) / 96
'            End If
'        End If
'
'    ' this is for hi lift
'    Else
'        If HWType = 2 Then
'            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + 10 * HILIFT + DrHgt + 1804) / 96
'            Else
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + 10 * HILIFT + DrHgt + 1823) / 96
'            End If
'        Else ' 3 and 3.25 "
'            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + 10 * HILIFT + DrHgt + 1847) / 96
'            Else
'                BracketMountM = (96 * aValue + 10 * HILIFT + DrHgt + 1823) / 96
'            End If
'        End If
'    End If

    noSection = (N21 + N24) '* 24 - 6
    If Not ((LiftType = "Vertical Lift") Or (LiftType = "Low Headroom Veritcal Lift")) Then
        If (HWType = 2) Then
            If (LiftType = "High Lift") Then
                BracketMountM = 20 + (HILIFT - 9) * 0.1051 + bValue          '(aValue + (noSection / 96) + 20 + ((HILIFT - 9) * 0.1051))
            Else
                If (LiftType = "Standard Lift") And (HRadius = 16) Then
                    BracketMountM = 20 + (noSection / 4) - (1 / 16) + aValue '(aValue + (noSection / 96) + 17)
                Else
                    BracketMountM = 17.0625 + (noSection / 4) - (1 / 16) + aValue                            '(aValue + (noSection / 96) + 20)
                End If
            End If
        Else
            BracketMountM = 20.375 + (noSection / 4) - (1 / 16) + aValue     ' aValue + (noSection / 96) + 20
            If (LiftType = "High Lift") Then
                BracketMountM = 20.375 + (HILIFT - 9) * 0.1051 + bValue       '(BracketMountM + ((HILIFT - 9) * 0.1051))
            End If
        End If
'        i = CInt(BracketMountM)                    Nov 14, 2003
'        BracketMountM = CSng(i - (i Mod 3))
    End If
    GetBracketMountM = BracketMountM

    Exit Function
    
Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

' "b"-value for the top of the lower vertical track
Public Function GetBracketMountB()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBracketMountB()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim BracketMountB As Single
    Dim lintNoSections As Integer
    
    lintNoSections = N21 + N24
    
    Select Case LiftType
        Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
            If HWType = 2 Then
                'BracketMountB = aValue + (DrHgt - 11) / 96
                BracketMountB = ((lintNoSections * 24) - 11) / 96 + aValue  'remains same
            ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
                BracketMountB = ((lintNoSections * 24) - 14) / 96 + aValue  'remains same
            End If

        Case Else
            'BracketMountB = aValue + (DrHgt - 6) / 96
            'BracketMountB = ((lintNoSections * 24) - 6) / 96 + aValue
            BracketMountB = (lintNoSections / 4) - (1 / 16) + aValue    'new b-value
    End Select

    GetBracketMountB = BracketMountB
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetBracketMountMc() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBracketMountMc()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim BracketMountMc As Single
    Dim i As Integer
    
'    BracketMountMc = CInt(mValue \ 3) * 3
'    GetBracketMountMc = BracketMountMc

    i = CInt(mValue)             'Nov 14, 2003
    GetBracketMountMc = CSng(i - (i Mod 3))

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource

Public Function DistanceLCalculation()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".DistanceLCalculation()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim DistanceL As Single

    If LiftType = "Standard Lift" Then
    ' this is for standard lift
        If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = True Then
            DistanceL = McValue + DrHgt
        Else
            DistanceL = McValue + DrHgt - 6
        End If
    Else ' hi lift
        DistanceL = McValue + (DrHgt - HILIFT)
    End If
    'BoltNCalculation BoltNOption1, DrHgt, LiftType, HWType, varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT
    DistanceLCalculation = DistanceL
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

' picks up Vertical Track Part no. and respective QTY attached to a particular Vertical Track Part no.
Public Sub SelectHardwareSize(Optional LenHT As Integer)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectHardwareSize()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim VerticalTrackLowerPN As String
    Dim VerticalTrackQTY As Integer
    Dim VerticalTrackLen As Integer
    Dim VTLen As String
    Dim lstrCutInstructions As Variant
    Dim lstrCutTo As String
    Dim lstrCutFrom As String
    Dim lstrCut As String
    Dim DoorHgt As Single
    Dim lblnTag As String
      
    DoorHgt = DrHgt

    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                If DoorHgt <= 45 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1506"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 78
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 1
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 45 And DoorHgt <= 51 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1507"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 90
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 1
                
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 51 And DoorHgt <= 57 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1508"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 102
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 1
                     
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 57 And DoorHgt <= 63 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1509"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 114
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 1
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 63 And DoorHgt <= 69 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1510"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 126
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 1
                     
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 69 And DoorHgt <= 75 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1511"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 138
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 1
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 75 And DoorHgt <= 84 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1506"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 78
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 84 And DoorHgt <= 96 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1507"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 90
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 96 And DoorHgt <= 108 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1508"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 102
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 108 And DoorHgt <= 120 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1509"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 114
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 120 And DoorHgt <= 132 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1510"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 126
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 132 And DoorHgt <= 144 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1511"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 138
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 144 And DoorHgt <= 156 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1512"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 150
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 156 And DoorHgt <= 168 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1513"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 162
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 168 And DoorHgt <= 180 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1514"
                    VerticalTrackLen = 174
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf DoorHgt > 180 And DoorHgt <= 192 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1515"
