'for SL we need to cut Lower vertical track only if required, and we need to cut upper/Lower vertical track for HL, LHR-VL, VL
    If VerticalTrackLowerPN = "" Then
        MsgBox "Cannot select Lower Vertical Track. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot select Lower Vertical Track. "
        Exit Sub
    Else
        VTLen = cutVerticalTrack(VerticalTrackLen, VerticalTrackQTY, "LowerTrack")
        lintcutValue = InStr(1, VTLen, "~")
        If lintcutValue > 0 Then
        
            lstrCutInstructions = Split(VTLen, "~")
            lstrCut = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(0))
                lstrCutTo = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(1))
                If lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                    lstrCutLoc = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutLoc
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                    lstrCutLoc = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutLoc
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                    lstrCutLoc = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    
                    lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutLoc
                End If
            'End If
            FillSummaryGrid VerticalTrackLowerPN, VerticalTrackQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(VerticalTrackLowerPN), 0, lstrCutAmt
        Else
            If VerticalTrackQTY = 1 Then
                lstrCutAmt = VTLen
                FillSummaryGrid VerticalTrackLowerPN, VerticalTrackQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(VerticalTrackLowerPN), 0, lstrCutAmt
            ElseIf VerticalTrackQTY = 2 And VTLen = "" Then
                FillSummaryGrid VerticalTrackLowerPN, VerticalTrackQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(VerticalTrackLowerPN), 0
'                    ElseIf VerticalTrackQTY = 2 And VTLen <> "" Then
'                        FillSummaryGrid VerticalTrackLowerPN, VerticalTrackQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(VerticalTrackLowerPN), 0, VTLen
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub


'cutting instructions for ADCA for all lift types

Public Function CutInstructionADCA(ByVal ADCALen As Variant, ByVal ADCAQTY As Integer) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".CutInstructionADCA()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim cutADCA As Single
    Dim selectLenADCA As Single
    Dim cutOffADCA As Single
    Dim cutLocation As String
    Dim ADCACutLen As String
    Dim cutInst As String
    Dim CutTo As String
    Dim lintTop As Integer
    Dim lsngBtm As Single
    Dim cutFromTop As String
    Dim cutFromBtm As String
    Dim lsngCutTo As Single
    Dim lsngCutOff As Single
    
    If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then 'ADCA
    
        Select Case LiftType
        Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
            If HWType = 2 Then
                If LiftType = "Standard Lift" And HRadius = 12 Then
                    cutADCA = DrHgt - 11  ' cut length of ADCA
                Else
                    cutADCA = DrHgt - 8
                End If
            Else
                cutADCA = DrHgt - 8
            End If
               
        Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
            If HWType = 2 Then
                cutADCA = DrHgt - 13
            Else
                cutADCA = DrHgt - 16
            End If
        End Select
        
        If ADCALen(1) <> "" Then     ' for only DH > 168 inches in 2" H/W
                                     ' use (2) ADCA per side w/ multiple part nos.
                                     ' bases of cutoff ranges, a part#  gets determine for a cut location (top / btm)
            If ADCAQTY = 4 Then
                selectLenADCA = ADCALen(0) + (ADCALen(1) * 2)
            Else
                selectLenADCA = ADCALen(0) + ADCALen(1)
            End If
                
            'calculate rem cutoff amount only !
            cutOffADCA = selectLenADCA - cutADCA
            If cutOffADCA > 0 Then
                If LiftType = "Standard Lift" And (HWType = 2 And HRadius = 12) Then
                        
                    If cutOffADCA <= 9 Then
                        Call ClearConObj
                            
                        'cut from top
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = 3
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            ADCACutLen = GetImpString(3)
                        End If
                        cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                        
                        'cut from btm
                        lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 3
                        If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                            End If
                            cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                        End If
                                                
                    ElseIf (cutOffADCA > 9 And cutOffADCA < 15) Then
                            
                            Call ClearConObj
                            'cut from top
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 8
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(8)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 8
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                    
                    ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 15 And cutOffADCA < 22 Then
                        'cut from top
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = 15
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            ADCACutLen = GetImpString(15)
                        End If
                        cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                                                                    
                        'cut from btm
                        lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 15
                        If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                            End If
                            cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                        End If
                    
                    ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 22 Then
                        'cut from top
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = 20
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            ADCACutLen = GetImpString(20)
                        End If
                        cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                                                                    
                        'cut from btm
                        lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 20
                        If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                            End If
                            cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                        End If
                    End If
                    
                Else 'SL w/16"Rad, HL, VL, LHR-VL
                
                    If cutOffADCA <= 6 Then  'cut from btm
                        Call ClearConObj
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = cutOffADCA
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(cutOffADCA - Fix(cutOffADCA)) + Fix(cutOffADCA)
                            ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        End If
                        cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                        
                    ElseIf (cutOffADCA > 6 And cutOffADCA < 12) Then
                            
                            Call ClearConObj
                            'cut from top
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 5
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(5)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 5
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                    
                    ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 12 And cutOffADCA < 19 Then
                        
                        'cut from top
                        Call ClearConObj
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = 12
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            ADCACutLen = GetImpString(12)
                        End If
                        cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                        
                        'cut from btm
                        lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 12
                        If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                            Call ClearConObj
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                            End If
                            cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                        End If
                    
                    ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 19 Then
                        'cut from top
                        Call ClearConObj
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = 17
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            ADCACutLen = GetImpString(17)
                        End If
                        cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                        
                        'cut from btm
                        lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 17
                        If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                            Call ClearConObj
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                            End If
                            cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                        End If
                    End If
                End If 'diff 12"/16" RAD
            End If
        
        Else                   ' for DH < 168 inches in 2" H/W
                               ' use (1) ADCA or (2) ADCA per side w/ a single part no.
                               ' calculate cut to's amount
                               ' if needed, calculate cutoff amount
            If ADCAQTY = 1 Then     'Qty = 1
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(cutADCA - Fix(cutADCA)) + Fix(cutADCA)
'                    cutLocation = "Cut (1) " & "to (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo) & " from each end"
                    cutLocation = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
                Else
                    aCon.Inch = cutADCA
                    aCon.mmcalc
'                    cutLocation = "Cut (1) " & "to (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm " & " from each end"
                    cutLocation = "Cut (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm "
                End If
               
            ElseIf ADCAQTY = 2 Then  'Qty = 2
                
                'rem cutoff amount
                cutOffADCA = ADCALen(0) - cutADCA
                If cutOffADCA > 0 Then
                    
                    'cut to's amount
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(cutADCA - Fix(cutADCA)) + Fix(cutADCA)
                        CutTo = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(cutADCA)
                    Else
                        Call ClearConObj
                        aCon.Inch = cutADCA
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        CutTo = "Cut (2) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                
                    If LiftType = "Standard Lift" And (HWType = 2 And HRadius = 12) Then
                            
                        'cut 3" from top, rem from btm
                        If cutOffADCA <= 9 Then
                            Call ClearConObj
                            'cut from top
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 3
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(3)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 3
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        
                        ElseIf (cutOffADCA > 9 And cutOffADCA < 15) Then
                            
                            Call ClearConObj
                            'cut from top
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 8
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(8)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 8
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        
                        ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 15 And cutOffADCA < 22 Then
                            
                            'cut from top
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 15
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(15)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                                                
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 15
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 22 Then
                            'cut from top
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 20
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(20)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                                                
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 20
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        End If
                        
                    Else                                'SL w/16"Rad, HL, VL, LHR-VL
                        If cutOffADCA <= 6 Then  'cut from btm
                            Call ClearConObj
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = cutOffADCA
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(cutOffADCA - Fix(cutOffADCA)) + Fix(cutOffADCA)
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                            End If

                            ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                            cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            ''''''''''END Change in .NET
                        
                        ElseIf (cutOffADCA > 6 And cutOffADCA < 12) Then
                            
                            Call ClearConObj
                            'cut from top
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 5
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(5)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 5
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        
                        ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 12 And cutOffADCA < 19 Then
                            
                            'cut from top

                            Call ClearConObj
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 12
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(12)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 12
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                Call ClearConObj
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        ElseIf cutOffADCA >= 19 Then
                            Call ClearConObj
                            If MeasU = 0 Then
                                aCon.Inch = 17
                                aCon.mmcalc
                                ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                            Else
                                ADCACutLen = GetImpString(17)
                            End If
                            cutFromTop = ADCACutLen & " from top"
                            
                            'cut from btm
                            lsngBtm = cutOffADCA - 17
                            If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                                Call ClearConObj
                                If MeasU = 0 Then
                                    aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                                    aCon.mmcalc
                                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                                Else
                                    lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                                    ADCACutLen = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                                End If
                                cutFromBtm = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If 'diff cut top/btm
                End If 'cutoff > 0
            End If 'QTY
        End If 'DH
        
        If ADCALen(1) <> "" Then
            If cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                cutLocation = "BOTH" & "~" & cutFromTop & "; " & cutFromBtm
            ElseIf cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm = "" Then
                cutLocation = "TOP" & "~" & cutFromTop
            ElseIf cutFromTop = "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                cutLocation = "BTM" & "~" & cutFromBtm
            End If
        Else
            If cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                cutLocation = "BOTH" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutFromTop & "; " & cutFromBtm
            ElseIf cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm = "" Then
                cutLocation = "TOP" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutFromTop
            ElseIf cutFromTop = "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
                cutLocation = "BTM" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutFromBtm
            End If
        End If
    Dim cut_B As Single
        
    Else    'European
        Select Case LiftType
            Case "Standard Lift"
                If HRadius = 12 Then        '12 RAD cut from both top & from bottom
                    'cut from both top & bottom
                    If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                        cut_B = 84 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 96 Then
                        cut_B = 96 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 120 Then
                        cut_B = 120 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 144 Then
                        cut_B = 144 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 168 Then
                        cut_B = 168 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 192 Then
                        cut_B = 192 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 216 Then
                        cut_B = 216 - DrHgt
                    End If
                        
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = cut_B
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        
                        If cut_B > 0 Then
                            cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                                  Chr$(32) & ";  " & 95 & " mm" & " from top"
                        Else
                            cutLocation = 95 & " mm" & " from top"
                        End If
                        
                    Else
                        ADCACutLen = cut_B & Chr(34)
                        If cut_B > 0 Then
                            cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                                  Chr$(32) & ";  " & 3.75 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                        Else
                            cutLocation = 3.75 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                        End If
                    End If
                Else    ' SL & 16 RAD, cut ADCA from bottom only
                  
                    If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 84 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 96 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 96 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 120 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 120 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 144 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 144 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 168 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 168 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 192 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 192 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 216 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 216 - DrHgt
                    ElseIf DrHgt <= 240 Then
                        cutOffADCA = 240 - DrHgt
                    End If
                        
                    If cutOffADCA > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = cutOffADCA
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            ADCACutLen = cutOffADCA & Chr(34)
                        End If
                        cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom"
                    End If
                End If
    
            Case "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
                'cut from both top & bottom
                If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                    cut_B = 84 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 96 Then
                    cut_B = 96 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 120 Then
                    cut_B = 120 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 144 Then
                    cut_B = 144 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 168 Then
                    cut_B = 168 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 192 Then
                    cut_B = 192 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 216 Then
                    cut_B = 216 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 240 Then
                    cut_B = 240 - DrHgt
                End If
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = cut_B
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    
                    If cut_B > 0 Then
                        cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                              Chr$(32) & ";  " & 270 & " mm" & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutLocation = 270 & " mm" & " from top"
                    End If
                Else
                    ADCACutLen = cut_B & Chr(34)
                    If cut_B > 0 Then
                        cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                              Chr$(32) & ";  " & 10.625 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutLocation = 10.625 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                    End If
                End If
                    
            Case "Low Headroom Front Mount"
                'cut from both top & bottom
                If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                    cut_B = 84 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 96 Then
                    cut_B = 96 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 120 Then
                    cut_B = 120 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 144 Then
                    cut_B = 144 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 168 Then
                    cut_B = 168 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 192 Then
                    cut_B = 192 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 216 Then
                    cut_B = 216 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 240 Then
                    cut_B = 240 - DrHgt
                End If
                    
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = cut_B
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    
                    If cut_B > 0 Then
                        cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                                  Chr$(32) & ";  " & 152 & " mm" & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutLocation = 152 & " mm" & " from top"
                    End If
                Else
                    ADCACutLen = cut_B & Chr(34)
                    If cut_B > 0 Then
                        cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                              Chr$(32) & ";  " & 6 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutLocation = 6 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                    End If
                End If
                
            Case "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
                'cut from both top & bottom
                If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                    cut_B = 84 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 96 Then
                    cut_B = 96 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 120 Then
                    cut_B = 120 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 144 Then
                    cut_B = 144 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 168 Then
                    cut_B = 168 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 192 Then
                    cut_B = 192 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 216 Then
                    cut_B = 216 - DrHgt
                ElseIf DrHgt <= 240 Then
                    cut_B = 240 - DrHgt
                End If

                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = cut_B
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    ADCACutLen = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    
                    If cut_B > 0 Then
                        cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                              Chr$(32) & ";  " & 184 & " mm" & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutLocation = 184 & " mm" & " from top"
                    End If
                    
                Else
                    ADCACutLen = cut_B & Chr(34)
                    
                    If cut_B > 0 Then
                        cutLocation = ADCACutLen & " from bottom" & _
                              Chr(32) & ";  " & 7.25 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                    Else
                        cutLocation = 7.25 & Chr(34) & " from top"
                    End If
                    
                End If

            End Select
    End If
    
    CutInstructionADCA = cutLocation
     
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

' select the ADCA Part No., QTY & Length

Public Sub selectADCAPN()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectADCAPN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim ADCAQTY(2) As Integer
    Dim ADCALen(2) As Variant
    Dim ADCAPN(2) As String
    Dim ADCACutLen As String
    Dim lstrcutoff As String
    Dim CB As Integer
    
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If LiftType = "Standard Lift" And HRadius = 12 Then
                If DrHgt <= 52 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0410"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                    ADCALen(0) = 88
                    
                    CB = 4
                    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 52 And DrHgt <= 64 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0411"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                    ADCALen(0) = 112
                    
                    CB = 6
    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 64 And DrHgt <= 75 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0412"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                    ADCALen(0) = 136
                    
                    CB = 6

            Else    ' 16 inches HRadius for SL, HL, VL, LHR-VL

            End If
        Case 3, 3.25    ' H/W for SL, HL, VL, LHR-VL

                ADCALen(0) = 136

            ElseIf DrHgt > 75 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0420"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 88
                
                CB = 8
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0421"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 112
                                
                CB = 10
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0422"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2

            ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then

                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 160
                
                CB = 14
     
            ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0421"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 112
                
                ADCAPN(1) = "425-0424"
                ADCAQTY(1) = 2
                ADCALen(1) = 72
                
                CB = 16
   
            ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0422"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 136
                
                ADCAPN(1) = "425-0424"
                ADCAQTY(1) = 2
                ADCALen(1) = 72
            
                CB = 18
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0423"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 160
                
                ADCAPN(1) = "425-0424"
                ADCAQTY(1) = 2
                ADCALen(1) = 72
                
                CB = 20
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 264 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0421"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 112
                
                ADCAPN(1) = "425-0424"
                ADCAQTY(1) = 4
                ADCALen(1) = 72
                
                CB = 22
                
            End If
            
        End Select
    
    Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
        Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If HRadius = 12 Then
                If DrHgt = 57 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0410"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                    ADCALen(0) = 88
                    
                    CB = 4
   
                ElseIf DrHgt > 57 And DrHgt <= 69 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0411"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                    ADCALen(0) = 112
                    
                    CB = 6
   
                ElseIf DrHgt > 69 And DrHgt <= 80 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0412"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                    ADCALen(0) = 136
                    
                    CB = 6
    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 80 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0410"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                    ADCALen(0) = 88

                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0413"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                    ADCALen(0) = 160

                ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then

                    ADCAPN(1) = "425-0414"
                    ADCAQTY(1) = 2
                    ADCALen(1) = 72
                    
                    CB = 16

                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0412"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                    ADCALen(0) = 136
                    
                    ADCAPN(1) = "425-0414"
                    ADCAQTY(1) = 2
                    ADCALen(1) = 72
                    
                    CB = 18
    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                    ADCAPN(0) = "425-0413"
                    ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                    ADCALen(0) = 160
                    
                    ADCAPN(1) = "425-0414"
                    ADCAQTY(1) = 2
                    ADCALen(1) = 72
                    
                    CB = 20
                 
                End If
            End If
       Case 3, 3.25  ' for low headroom front / rear H/W sizes
            If DrHgt <= 60 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0420"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                ADCALen(0) = 88
                
                CB = 4
          
            ElseIf DrHgt > 60 And DrHgt <= 72 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0421"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                ADCALen(0) = 112
                
                CB = 6
    
            ElseIf DrHgt > 72 And DrHgt <= 83 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0422"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 1
                ADCALen(0) = 136
                                
                CB = 6
                   
            ElseIf DrHgt > 83 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0420"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 88

            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0421"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 112
                
                CB = 10
              
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0422"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
                ADCALen(0) = 136
                
                CB = 12
            
            ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0423"
                ADCAQTY(0) = 2

                ADCALen(1) = 72
                
                CB = 16

            ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 264 Then

                ADCAPN(1) = "425-0424"

                ADCALen(1) = 72

'                ADCAPN(0) = "425-0423"
'                ADCAQTY(0) = 2
'                ADCALen(0) = 160
'
'                ADCAPN(1) = "425-0424"
'                ADCAQTY(1) = 4
'                ADCALen(1) = 72
'
'                CB = 20

    Dim adca_subCat As String
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim lstrCutInstructions As Variant
    Dim lstrCut As String
    Dim lstrCutFrom As String
    
    If ADCAPN(0) = "" Then
        MsgBox "Cannot select ADCA. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select ADCA."
        Exit Sub
    Else
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        
        If DrHgt > 248 And DrHgt <= 272 Then
            ADCACutLen = CutInstructionADCA(ADCALen(), ADCAQTY(1))
        Else
            ADCACutLen = CutInstructionADCA(ADCALen(), ADCAQTY(0))
        End If
        
        Dim lintcutValue As Integer
        Dim lstrCutTo As String
        Dim lstrCutAmt As String
        Dim lintFound As Integer
        Dim lstrcutAmt1 As String
        Dim lstrcutAmt2 As String
        
        lintcutValue = InStr(1, ADCACutLen, "~")
        If lintcutValue > 0 Then
        
            lstrCutInstructions = Split(ADCACutLen, "~")    ' CutTo carry ' Tag ' for DH > 14' Top/Btm/Both
            lstrCut = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(0))          ' CutLocation carry cutoff amount
            If DrHgt <= 168 Then
                lstrCutTo = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(1))
                If lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutFrom
                
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutFrom
                    
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lintFound = InStr(1, lstrCutFrom, ";")
                    If lintFound > 0 Then
                        lstrcutAmt1 = Mid(lstrCutFrom, 1, lintFound - 1)
                        lstrcutAmt2 = Mid(lstrCutFrom, lintFound + 1, Len(lstrCutFrom))
                        lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrcutAmt1 & "; " & lstrcutAmt2
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(1))
                If lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                    lstrCutAmt = "Cut " & ADCAPN(1) & " (2) " & lstrCutFrom
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                    lstrCutAmt = "Cut " & ADCAPN(0) & " (2) " & lstrCutFrom
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                    lintFound = InStr(1, lstrCutFrom, ";")
                    If lintFound > 0 Then
                        lstrcutAmt1 = "Cut (2) " & Mid(lstrCutFrom, 1, lintFound - 1)
                        lstrcutAmt2 = "Cut (2) " & Mid(lstrCutFrom, lintFound + 1, Len(lstrCutFrom))
'                        lstrcutAmt = "Cut " & ADCAPN(1) & " (2) " & lstrcutAmt1 & vbNewLine & "Cut " & ADCAPN(0) & " (2) " & lstrcutAmt2
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Else
            lstrCutAmt = ADCACutLen  'qty = 1
        End If
        
        For i = 0 To 1
            If ADCAPN(i) <> "" And ADCAQTY(i) <> Empty Then
                adca_subCat = ADCAPN(i)
'                If ADCACutLen <> "" Then
                
                    If lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                        If ADCAPN(1) <> "" Then
                            If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
                            If i = 1 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0
                        Else
                            If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
                        End If
                        
                    ElseIf lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                        If ADCAPN(1) <> "" Then
                            If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0
                            If i = 1 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
                        Else
                            If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
                        End If
                        
                    ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                        If ADCAPN(1) <> "" Then
                            If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrcutAmt2 <> "", lstrcutAmt2, "") 'btm  PN = 2
                            If i = 1 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrcutAmt1 <> "", lstrcutAmt1, "") 'top
                        Else
                            If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "") 'both when PN = 1
                        End If
                    Else
                        FillSummaryGrid ADCAPN(i), ADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(adca_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
                    End If
'                End If
            
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub

'LowerWallAngles for all 2", 3" and super 3.25 inches H/W sizes and for all lift types
Public Sub selectLowerWallAnglePN()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectLowerWallAnglePN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim LWAPN As String
    Dim LWAQTY As Integer
    Dim LWALen As Integer
    Dim cutLWA As String
    Dim lstrCut As String
    Dim lstrcutoff As String
    Dim lstrCutToAmt As String
    Dim lstrCutOffAmt As String
    Dim lstrCutInstructions As Variant
        
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                If DrHgt > 44 And DrHgt <= 52 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0310"
                    LWAQTY = 1
                    LWALen = 88
                                    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 52 And DrHgt <= 64 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0311"
                    LWAQTY = 1
                    LWALen = 112
                    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 64 And DrHgt <= 75 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0312"
                    LWAQTY = 1
                    LWALen = 136
                            
                ElseIf DrHgt > 75 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0310"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 88
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0311"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 112
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0312"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 136
                    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0313"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 160
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0314"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 184
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0315"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 208
                End If
            
        Case 3, 3.25    ' 3 & 3.25 inches for SL, High, VL, LHR-VL
        
            If DrHgt > 44 And DrHgt <= 52 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0320"
                LWAQTY = 1
                LWALen = 88
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 52 And DrHgt <= 64 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0321"
                LWAQTY = 1
                LWALen = 112
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 64 And DrHgt <= 75 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0322"
                LWAQTY = 1
                LWALen = 136
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 75 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0320"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 88
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0321"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 112
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0322"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 136
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0323"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 160
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0324"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 184
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0325"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 208
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 248 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0326"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 232
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 264 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0327"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 256
                
            End If
        End Select  ' H/W : High, VL, LHR-VL, SL
        
    Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
        Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If HRadius = 12 Then
               If DrHgt > 44 And DrHgt <= 52 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0310"
                    LWAQTY = 1
                    LWALen = 88
                                    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 52 And DrHgt <= 64 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0311"
                    LWAQTY = 1
                    LWALen = 112
                    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 64 And DrHgt <= 80 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0312"
                    LWAQTY = 1
                    LWALen = 136
                            
                ElseIf DrHgt > 80 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0310"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 88
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0311"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 112
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0312"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 136
                    
                ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0313"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 160
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0314"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 184
                        
                ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                    LWAPN = "425-0315"
                    LWAQTY = 2
                    LWALen = 208
                End If
            End If
            
        Case 3, 3.25     ' H/W Low headroom front / rear
        
            If DrHgt > 44 And DrHgt <= 52 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0320"
                LWAQTY = 1
                LWALen = 88
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 52 And DrHgt <= 64 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0321"
                LWAQTY = 1
                LWALen = 112
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 64 And DrHgt <= 83 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0322"
                LWAQTY = 1
                LWALen = 136
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 83 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0320"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 88
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0321"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 112
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0322"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 136
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0323"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 160
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0324"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 184
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0325"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 208
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 248 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0326"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 232
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 264 Then
                LWAPN = "425-0327"
                LWAQTY = 2
                LWALen = 256
                
            End If
        End Select  ' H/W : Low Headroom
    End Select     ' LWA : LiftType
    
    Dim lstrCutTo As String
    Dim lstrCutLoc As String
    Dim lstrCutAmt As String
    Dim lintcutValue As Integer
    
    If LWAPN <> "" Then
        cutLWA = CutInstructionLWA(LWALen, LWAQTY)
        lintcutValue = InStr(1, cutLWA, "~")
        If lintcutValue > 0 Then
            lstrCutInstructions = Split(cutLWA, "~")
            lstrCut = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(0))
            lstrCutTo = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(1))
            If lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                lstrCutLoc = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutLoc
            ElseIf lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                lstrCutLoc = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutLoc
            ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                lstrCutLoc = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutLoc
            End If
            FillSummaryGrid LWAPN, LWAQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LWAPN), 0, lstrCutAmt
        Else
            FillSummaryGrid LWAPN, LWAQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LWAPN), 0, IIf(cutLWA <> "", cutLWA, "")
        End If
        Call selectLWATrackClip
    Else
        MsgBox "Cannot select Lower Wall Angle. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Lower Wall Angle. "
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub

'this routine will select upper vertical tracks, QTY for High lift and vertical lift

Public Sub selectUpperTrack()

        MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Vertical Track. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Vertical Track. "

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
        If LiftType = "High Lift" Then Call SelectHardwareSize 'Lower Vertical Track
''''''''''END Change in .NET
        
        Exit Sub
    Else
        VTLen = cutVerticalTrack(UpperVTLen, UpperVTQTY, "UpperTrack")
        If VTLen <> "" Then
            FillSummaryGrid UpperVTPN, UpperVTQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(UpperVTPN), 0, VTLen

'            FillSummaryGrid UpperVTPN, UpperVTQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(UpperVTPN), 0, lstrCut & "; " & lstrCutFrom
'        End If

        If LiftType = "High Lift" Then Call SelectHardwareSize 'Lower Vertical Track

        
    End If
    
    Exit Sub
