    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
End Sub

Public Sub selectLWATrackClip()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectLWATrackClip()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim LWATrackClipPN(5) As String
    Dim LWATrackClipQTY(5) As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
    
    Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If DrHgt >= 47 And DrHgt < 72 Then
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                                               
                CB = 4
                KN = 4

            ElseIf DrHgt >= 72 And DrHgt < 96 Then
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
               
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 2
               
                CB = 6
                KN = 6

            ElseIf DrHgt >= 96 And DrHgt < 120 Then
           
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
               
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
               
                CB = 8
                KN = 8
                               
           ElseIf DrHgt >= 120 And DrHgt < 144 Then
           
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
               
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
               
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 2
                               
                CB = 10
                KN = 10

            ElseIf DrHgt >= 144 And DrHgt < 168 Then
               
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
               
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
               
                CB = 12
                KN = 12
    
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 168 And DrHgt < 192 Then
           
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 2
               
                CB = 14
                KN = 14
            
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 192 And DrHgt < 216 Then
           
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 4
                
                CB = 16
                KN = 16
               
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 216 And DrHgt < 240 Then
           
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(4) = "426-1015"
                LWATrackClipQTY(4) = 2
                
                CB = 18
                KN = 18
            End If
          
        Case 3, 3.25    ' 3 & 3.25 inches for SL, High, VL, LHR-VL
        
            If DrHgt >= 47 And DrHgt < 72 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                
                CB = 4
                KN = 4
 
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 72 And DrHgt < 96 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 2
                
                CB = 6
                KN = 6
     
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 96 And DrHgt < 120 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                CB = 8
                KN = 8
                    
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 120 And DrHgt < 144 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 2
                
                CB = 10
                KN = 10
       
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 144 And DrHgt < 168 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                CB = 12
                KN = 12
        
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 168 And DrHgt < 192 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 2
                
                CB = 14
                KN = 14
      
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 192 And DrHgt < 216 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 4
                
                CB = 16
                KN = 16
     
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 216 And DrHgt < 240 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(4) = "426-1015"
                LWATrackClipQTY(4) = 2
                
                CB = 18
                KN = 18
               
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 240 And DrHgt < 264 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(4) = "426-1015"
                LWATrackClipQTY(4) = 4
                
                CB = 20
                KN = 20
            
            ElseIf DrHgt >= 264 And DrHgt < 288 Then
            
                LWATrackClipPN(0) = "426-1011"
                LWATrackClipQTY(0) = 4
                    
                LWATrackClipPN(1) = "426-1012"
                LWATrackClipQTY(1) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(2) = "426-1013"
                LWATrackClipQTY(2) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(3) = "426-1014"
                LWATrackClipQTY(3) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(4) = "426-1015"
                LWATrackClipQTY(4) = 4
                
                LWATrackClipPN(5) = "426-1016"
                LWATrackClipQTY(5) = 2
                
                CB = 22
                KN = 22
            End If
        End Select  ' H/W
    
    If LWATrackClipPN(0) <> "" Then
    
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
        
        Dim i As Integer
        For i = 0 To 5
            If LWATrackClipPN(i) <> "" And LWATrackClipQTY(i) <> Empty Then
                FillSummaryGrid LWATrackClipPN(i), LWATrackClipQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(LWATrackClipPN(i)), 0
            Else
                Exit Sub
            End If
        Next
    Else
         MsgBox "Cannot select LWA track clips. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select LWA track clips. "
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub

Function CutInstructionLWA(ByVal LWALen As Integer, ByVal LWAQTY As Integer) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".CutInstructionLWA()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim cutLWA As Single
    Dim cutOffLWA As Single
    Dim cutLoc As String
    Dim cutLoc1 As String
    Dim cutLoc2 As String
    Dim LWACutLen As String
    Dim CutTo As String
    Dim CutTop As Integer
    Dim CutBtm As Single
    Dim lstrCutFromTop As String
    Dim lstrCutFromBtm As String
    Dim lsngCutTo As Single
    Dim lsngCutOff As Single
    
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If LiftType = "Standard Lift" And HRadius = 12 Then
                cutLWA = DrHgt - 11
            Else
                cutLWA = DrHgt - 8
            End If
        Case 3, 3.25
            cutLWA = DrHgt - 8
        End Select
        
    Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
        Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If HRadius = 12 Then
                cutLWA = DrHgt - 11
            End If
        Case 3, 3.25
            cutLWA = DrHgt - 16
        End Select
    End Select
    
    If LWAQTY = 1 Then
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(cutLWA - Fix(cutLWA)) + Fix(cutLWA)
'            cutLoc = "Cut (1) " & "to (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo) & " from each ends"
            cutLoc = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
        Else
            aCon.Inch = cutLWA
            aCon.mmcalc
'            cutLoc = "Cut (1) " & "to (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm " & " from each end"
            cutLoc = "Cut (2) " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm "
        End If
    Else ' QTY = 2
         ' rem cutoff amount
        cutOffLWA = LWALen - cutLWA
        
        If cutOffLWA > 0 Then
        ' calculates cut to's amount
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(cutLWA - Fix(cutLWA)) + Fix(cutLWA)
                CutTo = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
            Else
                Call ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = cutLWA
                aCon.mmcalc
                CutTo = "Cut (2) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
            End If
            
            If LiftType = "Standard Lift" And (HWType = 2 And HRadius = 12) Then
                ' calculates cutoff amount
                    
                'cut 3" from top and balance from btm
                If cutOffLWA <= 9 Then
                    
                    'cut 3" from top
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lstrCutFromTop = GetImpString(3)
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = 3
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromTop = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromTop & " from top"
                    
                    'cut from bottom
                    CutBtm = cutOffLWA - 3
                    If CutBtm > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(CutBtm - Fix(CutBtm)) + Fix(CutBtm)
                            lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutBtm
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        End If
                        cutLoc2 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
                    End If
                    
                ElseIf (cutOffLWA > 9 And cutOffLWA < 15) Then
                            
                    Call ClearConObj
                    'cut from top
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = 8
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromTop = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lstrCutFromTop = GetImpString(8)
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromTop & " from top"
                    
                    'cut from btm
                    CutBtm = cutOffLWA - 8
                    If CutBtm > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = CutBtm
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(CutBtm - Fix(CutBtm)) + Fix(CutBtm)
                            lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        End If
                        cutLoc2 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
                    End If
                
                ElseIf cutOffLWA >= 15 And cutOffLWA < 22 Then
                    'cut from top
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lstrCutFromTop = GetImpString(15)
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = 15
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromTop = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromTop & " from top"
                    
                    'cut from btm
                    CutBtm = cutOffLWA - 15
                    If CutBtm > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(CutBtm - Fix(CutBtm)) + Fix(CutBtm)
                            lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutBtm
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        End If
                        cutLoc2 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
                    End If
                
                ElseIf cutOffLWA >= 22 Then
                    'cut from top
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lstrCutFromTop = GetImpString(20)
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = 20
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromTop = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromTop & " from top"
                    
                    'cut from btm
                    CutBtm = cutOffLWA - 20
                    If CutBtm > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(CutBtm - Fix(CutBtm)) + Fix(CutBtm)
                            lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutBtm
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        End If
                        cutLoc2 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
                    End If
                End If
            Else                                'all lift conditions
                ' calculates cutoff amount
                If cutOffLWA <= 6 Then           'cut from bottom
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(cutOffLWA - Fix(cutOffLWA)) + Fix(cutOffLWA)
                        lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(cutOffLWA)
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = cutOffLWA
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
     
                ElseIf (cutOffLWA > 6 And cutOffLWA < 12) Then
                            
                    Call ClearConObj
                    'cut from top
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = 5
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromTop = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lstrCutFromTop = GetImpString(5)
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromTop & " from top"
                    
                    'cut from btm
                    CutBtm = cutOffLWA - 5
                    If CutBtm > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = CutBtm
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        Else
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(CutBtm - Fix(CutBtm)) + Fix(CutBtm)
                            lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        End If
                        cutLoc2 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
                    End If
            
                ElseIf cutOffLWA >= 12 And cutOffLWA < 19 Then
                    'cut from top
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lstrCutFromTop = GetImpString(12)
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = 12
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromTop = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromTop & " from top"
                    
                    'cut from btm
                    CutBtm = cutOffLWA - 12
                    If CutBtm > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(CutBtm - Fix(CutBtm)) + Fix(CutBtm)
                            lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutBtm
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        End If
                        cutLoc2 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
                    End If
                    
                ElseIf cutOffLWA >= 19 Then
                    'cut from top
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        lstrCutFromTop = GetImpString(17)
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = 17
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lstrCutFromTop = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    End If
                    cutLoc1 = lstrCutFromTop & " from top"
                    
                    'cut from btm
                    CutBtm = cutOffLWA - 17
                    If CutBtm > 0 Then
                        If MeasU = 1 Then
                            lsngCutOff = RoundFraction(CutBtm - Fix(CutBtm)) + Fix(CutBtm)
                            lstrCutFromBtm = GetImpString(lsngCutOff)
                        Else
                            aCon.Inch = CutBtm
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            lstrCutFromBtm = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                        End If
                        cutLoc2 = lstrCutFromBtm & " from bottom"
                    End If
                End If  'cutofflwa
            End If  '12/16 radius
        End If
    End If 'QTY = 2
    
    If LWAQTY > 1 Then
        If cutLoc1 <> "" And cutLoc2 <> "" Then
            CutInstructionLWA = "BOTH" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutLoc1 & " balance " & cutLoc2
        ElseIf cutLoc1 <> "" And cutLoc2 = "" Then
            CutInstructionLWA = "TOP" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutLoc1
        ElseIf cutLoc2 <> "" And cutLoc1 = "" Then
            CutInstructionLWA = "BTM" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutLoc2
        End If
    Else
        CutInstructionLWA = cutLoc
    End If
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

'this routine pick Lower Splice Bracket Part no., QTY for 2", 3" or 3 1/4" HL, VL
'with upper wall angle on the basis of 'b'-value

Public Sub LowerSpliceBracket_UWA()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".LowerSpliceBracket_UWA()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim LowerSplice1PN As String
    Dim LowerSplice1QTY As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''

    'Bill Leather approved - Updated lower range for min B-value: before b-value >= 2.75 <= 4
'    If LiftType = "High Lift" Or LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Or LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
'        If bValue <= 4 Then
'            LowerSplice1PN = "426-1040"
'            LowerSplice1QTY = 2
'
'        ElseIf bValue > 4 And bValue <= 5.75 Then
'            LowerSplice1PN = "426-1041"
'            LowerSplice1QTY = 2
'        End If
'
'        Select Case HWType
'        Case 2
'            CB = 4
'            KN = 4
'
'        Case 3, 3.25
'            CB = 8
'            KN = 4
'        End Select
'    End If
    

    If LiftType = "High Lift" Or LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Or LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
        Select Case HWType
        
        Case 2
            CB = 4
            KN = 4

        Case 3, 3.25
            CB = 8
            KN = 4
        End Select
    End If
    
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        If HWType = 2 Then
            If bValue <= 3.75 Then  'see jakub post dated April 22, 2010  - "B" value for LS1 Lower Splice Bracket
                LowerSplice1PN = "426-1040"
                LowerSplice1QTY = 2
            ElseIf bValue > 3.875 And bValue <= 5.375 Then
                LowerSplice1PN = "426-1041"
                LowerSplice1QTY = 2
            End If
        
        ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
            If bValue <= 3 Then     'see jakub post dated April 22, 2010  - "B" value for LS1 Lower Splice Bracket
                LowerSplice1PN = "426-1040"
                LowerSplice1QTY = 2
            ElseIf bValue > 3 And bValue <= 4.5 Then
                LowerSplice1PN = "426-1041"
                LowerSplice1QTY = 2
            End If
        End If
    End Select
           
''''''''''END Change in .NET

    If LowerSplice1PN <> "" Then
        FillSummaryGrid LowerSplice1PN, LowerSplice1QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LowerSplice1PN), 0
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
    Else
        MsgBox "Cannot select Lower Splice Bracket. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Lower Splice Bracket. "
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

'this routine pick Lower Splice Bracket Part no., QTY for 2", 3" or 3 1/4" HL, VL
'with upper bracket mount on the basis of 'b'-value

Public Sub LowerSpliceBracket_UBrktMount()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".LowerSpliceBracket_UBrktMount()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim LowerSplice1PN_LH As String
    Dim LowerSplice1PN_RH As String
    Dim LowerSplice1QTY_LH As Integer
    Dim LowerSplice1QTY_RH As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    

    If LiftType = "High Lift" Or LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Or LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
        Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If bValue <= 5.773 Then '>= 1.148 And bValue <= 5.773
                LowerSplice1PN_LH = "436-0076"
                LowerSplice1QTY_LH = 1
                
                CB = 2
            
            End If
    
            If bValue <= 5.773 Then '>= 1.148 And bValue <= 5.773
                LowerSplice1PN_RH = "436-0077"
                LowerSplice1QTY_RH = 1
                
                CB = 2
 
            End If
                
        Case 3, 3.25
            If bValue <= 4.822 Then '>= 1.447 And bValue <= 4.822
                LowerSplice1PN_LH = "436-0076"
                LowerSplice1QTY_LH = 1
                
                CB = 2
    
            End If
    
            If bValue <= 4.822 Then '>= 1.447 And bValue <= 4.822
                LowerSplice1PN_RH = "436-0077"
                LowerSplice1QTY_RH = 1
                
                CB = 2
 
            End If
        End Select
    End If
    
    If LowerSplice1PN_LH <> "" Then
    
        FillSummaryGrid LowerSplice1PN_LH, LowerSplice1QTY_LH, "Track", GetSubCategory(LowerSplice1PN_LH), 0
        FillSummaryGrid LowerSplice1PN_RH, LowerSplice1QTY_RH, "Track", GetSubCategory(LowerSplice1PN_RH), 0
        
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
    Else
        MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Bracket mount. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Bracket mount. "
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub kit_wearhouse()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".kit_wearhouse()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim LHFM_PN As String, LHRM_PN As String
    Dim LHFM_QTY As Integer, LHRM_QTY As Integer
    
    Select Case LiftType
        Case "Low Headroom Front Mount"
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                LHFM_PN = "7-4011"
                LHFM_QTY = 1
             Case 3
                LHFM_PN = "7-4013"
                LHFM_QTY = 1
             Case 3.25
                LHFM_PN = "7-4015"
                LHFM_QTY = 1
             End Select
             FillSummaryGrid LHFM_PN, LHFM_QTY, "Track", "LOW HDRM KIT FRONT MOUNT", 0
        
        Case "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                LHRM_PN = "7-4010"
                LHRM_QTY = 1
             Case 3
                LHRM_PN = "7-4012"
                LHRM_QTY = 1
             Case 3.25
                LHRM_PN = "7-4014"
                LHRM_QTY = 1
             End Select
             FillSummaryGrid LHRM_PN, LHRM_QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LHRM_PN), 0
        End Select
            
        Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
End Sub

Public Sub VLTrackSupport()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".VLTrackSupport()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim lstrVLTrackSupport_PN As String
    Dim lintVLTrackSupport_QTY As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
    
    If LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Then
        Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                If cValue <= 20.8125 Then   'mim c-value approved by Bill L on March 30, 2004
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2014"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 6
                    KN = 2
                       
                ElseIf cValue > 20.8125 And cValue <= 29.59375 Then
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 6
                    KN = 2
                
                ElseIf cValue > 29.59375 Then
                    
                    cValue = 29.59375   'new c-value if c max out the upper limit then re-set to the upper limit pick pn
                    
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 6
                    KN = 2
                
                 
                End If
            Case 3, 3.25
                If cValue <= 21.0625 Then   'mim c-value approved by Bill L on March 30, 2004
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2014"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    KN = 2

                ElseIf cValue > 21.0626 And cValue <= 28.625 Then
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    KN = 2
                    
                ElseIf cValue > 28.625 Then
                    
                    cValue = 28.625     'new c-value if c max out the upper limit then re-set to the upper limit & pick pn
                    
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    KN = 2
                    

                End If
                
        End Select
        
    ElseIf LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
        Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                If cValue <= 20.875 Then    'mim c-value approved by Bill L on March 30, 2004
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2014"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 6
                    KN = 2
                  
                    
                ElseIf cValue > 20.875 And cValue <= 29.65625 Then
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 6
                    KN = 2
                    
                ElseIf cValue > 29.65625 Then
                        
                    cValue = 29.65625       'new c-value if c max out the upper limit then re-set to the upper limit & pick pn
                    
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 6
                    KN = 2
                    

                End If
                
            Case 3, 3.25
                If cValue <= 20 Then    'mim c-value approved by Bill L on March 30, 2004
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2014"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    KN = 2
 
                ElseIf cValue > 20 And cValue <= 28.75 Then
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    KN = 2
                    
                ElseIf cValue > 28.75 Then
                
                    cValue = 28.75      'new c-value if c max out the upper limit then re-set to the upper limit & pick pn
                    
                    lstrVLTrackSupport_PN = "425-2015"
                    lintVLTrackSupport_QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    KN = 2
                
                End If
        End Select
    End If
    
    If lstrVLTrackSupport_PN <> "" Then
        FillSummaryGrid lstrVLTrackSupport_PN, lintVLTrackSupport_QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(lstrVLTrackSupport_PN), 0
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
    Else
'        MsgBox "Cannot select Track Support. " & vbCrLf & _
'        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Track Support."
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub

Public Function GetBracketMountC() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBracketMountC()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim BracketMountC As Single
    
    Select Case LiftType
        Case "High Lift"
            BracketMountC = (HILIFT - 9) * 0.1051 + bValue
             
        Case "Vertical Lift"
            BracketMountC = (DrHgt + 17) * 0.1051 + bValue
            Select Case HWType
                Case 2
'                    If DrHgt <= 80.25 And DrType = "T150" Then 'Don and Bill decided on Nov 13, 2003
                        If BracketMountC < 12 Then BracketMountC = 12
'                    End If
                    
                Case 3, 4
'                    If DrHgt <= 72.25 And DrType = "T150" Then 'Don and Bill decided on Nov 13, 2003
                        If BracketMountC < 11.0625 Then BracketMountC = 11.0625
'                    End If
            End Select
            
        Case "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
            BracketMountC = (DrHgt + 5) * 0.1051 + bValue
            Select Case HWType
                Case 2
'                    If DrHgt <= 81.5 And DrType = "T150" Then  'Don and Bill decided on Nov 13, 2003
                        If BracketMountC < 12.125 Then BracketMountC = 12.125
'                    End If
                    
                Case 3, 3.25
'                    If DrHgt <= 73.5 And DrType = "T150" Then  ''Don and Bill decided on Nov 13, 2003
                        If BracketMountC < 11.21875 Then BracketMountC = 11.21875
'                    End If
            End Select
    End Select
    
    GetBracketMountC = BracketMountC

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

Public Sub UpperSplice_HL()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".UpperSplice_HL()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim UpperSplice1PN As String
    Dim UpperSplice1QTY As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
    
    'Use c-value instead of HILIFT range
    'Bill Leather approved - min C-value
    If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
        If LiftType = "High Lift" Then
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
               If cValue >= 5.375 And cValue <= 12.5 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1042"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                
                ElseIf cValue >= 12.5001 And cValue <= 18.5 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1043"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    
                ElseIf cValue >= 18.5001 And cValue <= 24.59375 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1044"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 8
                    
                End If
                    
            Case 3, 3.25
                If cValue >= 5 And cValue <= 12.75 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1042"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 12
                
                ElseIf cValue >= 12.7501 And cValue <= 18.75 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1043"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 12
                    
                ElseIf cValue >= 18.7501 And cValue <= 24.1875 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1044"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 12
                   
                End If
            End Select
        End If
    Else   'European
        If LiftType = "High Lift" Then
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2, 3, 3.25
                If cValue >= 5.375 And cValue <= 12.5 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1042"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 4
                    KN = 4
                
                ElseIf cValue > 12.5 And cValue <= 18.5 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1043"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 4
                    KN = 4
                    
                ElseIf cValue > 18.5 And cValue <= 24.59375 Then
                    UpperSplice1PN = "426-1044"
                    UpperSplice1QTY = 2
                    
                    CB = 4
                    KN = 4
                    
                End If
            End Select
        End If
    End If
    
    If UpperSplice1PN <> "" Then
        FillSummaryGrid UpperSplice1PN, UpperSplice1QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(UpperSplice1PN), 0
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
    Else
        If LiftType = "High Lift" Then
            If (HWType = 2 And cValue < 5.375) Or _
                (HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 And cValue < 5) Then
            
            'DO NOTHING!!!
            Else
                MsgBox "Cannot select HL Upper Splice. " & vbCrLf & _
                "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select HL Upper Splice."
            End If
         End If
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub

Public Sub SelectTrackPkgs()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectTrackPkgs()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This routine will select the track packages for a door bill-of-materials
    'UTBktFlag : Used to determine the type of upper track
    '            mount type (Angle vs bracket)
    'Array used to hold nuts and bold quantities based on door height and others
    
    Dim HorTrackSet() As Integer    'Add on for horizontal track and hardware type
    Dim S As String
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim j As Integer
    Dim CBFN As String
    Dim KNFN As String
    Dim QTY As Integer
    ReDim HorTrackSet(3) As Integer
    
    'changed on Nov 26, 2003: to correct the values from 1.2.4
    HorTrackSet(0) = 6      '10
    HorTrackSet(1) = 8      '18
    HorTrackSet(2) = 10     '8
    HorTrackSet(3) = 12     '12
    
    'Fastener for headplates
'    ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + 8  'no need to add headplate fastener ! already been picked in headplate fastener pkg. (4-0071 / 4-0072)
'    ReqQty(1) = 0
    
    'Fastener for Upper splice
'    If (LiftType = "High Lift") Then
'        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + 12
'    End If
    
    'Fastener for horizontal support - no need to add
    
'    If (LiftType = "Standard Lift") Or (LiftType = "High Lift") Then
'        If (HWType = 2) Then
'            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = False Then
'                ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + HorTrackSet(1)  'separate
'            Else
'                ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + HorTrackSet(0)  ' 1 piece
'            End If
'        Else
'            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = False Then
'                ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + HorTrackSet(3)
'            Else
'                ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + HorTrackSet(2)
'            End If
'        End If
'    End If
    
    'extra Fasteners to be added for both CB & KN
    
    
    'new CB
    If ReqQty(0) > 0 Then
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + 4
    End If
    
    'new KN
    If ReqQty(1) > 0 Then
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + 2
    End If
    
    
    
    'Fasteners required for upper clips
'    If (LiftType = "Vertical Lift") Or (LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift") Or (LiftType = "High Lift") Then
'        If (LiftType = "Vertical Lift") Or (LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift") Then
'            J = Int(DrHgt)
'            If (DrHgt > J) Then
'                J = J + 1
'            End If
'        Else
'            J = Int(HILIFT)
'            If (HILIFT > J) Then
'                J = J + 1
'            End If
'        End If
'
'        i = NumOfUpperTrackClips()
'
'        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + (i * 2)
'        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + (i * 2)
'
'        If (varDoorData.Track.strLowerSplice = "w/ Upper Bracket Mount") Then
'            '# of Cariage bolts = (total number of clips * 2)
'            '                     (     ( I * 2 )        * 2)
'            ReqQty(0) = (ReqQty(0) + (i * 4))
'        End If
'    End If
    
    'Fasteners for Lower Splice
'    ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + 8
'    ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + 2
    
    'Fasteners for Upper track support brackets
'    If (LiftType = "Vertical Lift") Or (LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift") Then
'        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + 8
'        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + 2
'    End If
    
    'Carmen - 14.01.00 - Calculation for right number of KNFN bolts
'    If (LiftType = "High Lift") And (varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Clip angle") Then
'        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + 2
'    End If
    
    'Fastener for angles/brackets
'    If (varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Bracket Mount") Then
'        i = (CInt(DrHgt) \ 24) * 2
'        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + i
'    Else
'        'I = ((CInt(DoorHeight) - 72) \ 24) + 2
'        'If (((CInt(DoorHeight) - 84) Mod 24) = 0) Then
'    'Carmen - 13.01.00 - Change made per discussion with Ray and Bill
'                        ' New formula for no. of bolts
'        i = Int((DrHgt - 21) / 24)
'        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + (i * 2)
'        If (varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "Clip angle") Then
'              ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + (i * 2)
'        End If
'    End If
    
    'Add fasteners for angle mounted weather seal
'    If varDoorData.Track.strWeatherStrip = "ADCA mount Jamb Weather Seal" Then
'        i = ((CInt(DrHgt) - 10) \ 24) + 1
'        i = (i * 2)
'        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + i
'    End If
    
    If (ReqQty(0) > 0) Or (ReqQty(1) > 0) Then
        ReDim HorTrackSet(3)
        For i = 0 To 3
            HorTrackSet(i) = 0
        Next i
    
        'Get number of Carriage bolts required
       If (ReqQty(0) > 0) Then
            i = ReqQty(0)
    
            'Call a routine to determine how many packages to pick from available
            'quantity configurations
           
            Call PickTrackPkgQty(i)
            
            For j = 0 To 3
                If (ResultArr(j) > 0) Then
    
                    If (j = 0) Then
                        CBFN = "4-0090"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j))
                        FillSummaryGrid CBFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(CBFN), 0
                    ElseIf (j = 1) Then
                        CBFN = "4-0091"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j))
                        FillSummaryGrid CBFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(CBFN), 0
                    ElseIf (j = 2) Then
                        CBFN = "4-0092"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j))
                        FillSummaryGrid CBFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(CBFN), 0
                    Else
                        CBFN = "4-0093"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j))
                        FillSummaryGrid CBFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(CBFN), 0
                    End If
                End If
            Next j
        End If
    
        'Get number of Knurl bolts required
        
        If (ReqQty(1) > 0) Then
            i = ReqQty(1)
            
            'Call a routine to determine how many packages to pick from available
            'quantity configurations
            
            Call PickTrackPkgQty(i)
            
            For j = 0 To 3
                If (ResultArr(j) > 0) Then
                    If (j = 0) Then         'KNFN 6 are selected
                        KNFN = "4-0100"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j)) '+ 3     Need confirmation: add 3 more, customer recomendation
                        FillSummaryGrid KNFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(KNFN), 0
                    ElseIf (j = 1) Then
                        KNFN = "4-0101"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j))
                        FillSummaryGrid KNFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(KNFN), 0
                    ElseIf (j = 2) Then
                        KNFN = "4-0102"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j))
                        FillSummaryGrid KNFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(KNFN), 0
                    Else
                        KNFN = "4-0103"
                        QTY = CSng(ResultArr(j))
                        FillSummaryGrid KNFN, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(KNFN), 0
                    End If
                
'''''                Else    'When NO KNFN 6 selected then Add (1) 4-0100 KNFN 6
'''''                    If j = 0 And ResultArr(0) = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid "4-0100", 1, "Track", GetSubCategory("4-0100"), 0
                End If
            Next j
        End If
    End If
    
    For i = 0 To 1
        ReqQty(i) = 0
    Next i
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
End Sub

Public Sub PickTrackPkgQty(QTY As Integer)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".PickTrackPkgQty()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This routine will try to find a combination of packages of size
    '6, 8, 10 and 12 to make up a selection for given quantity Qty.
    
    Dim D1 As Integer, T1 As Integer, M As Integer
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Dim i As Integer
    
    For i = 0 To 3
        ResultArr(i) = 0
    Next i
    
    T1 = QTY \ 2
    D1 = 6
    
    Flag = False
    Do While Not Flag
        M = T1 Mod D1
        Select Case M
        Case 0, 3, 4, 5
            If M = 0 Then
                If D1 = 6 Then
                    ResultArr(3) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 5) Then
                    ResultArr(2) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 4) Then
                    ResultArr(1) = T1 \ D1
                Else
                    ResultArr(0) = T1 \ D1
                End If
            Else
                If D1 = 6 Then
                    ResultArr(3) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 5) Then
                    ResultArr(2) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 4) Then
                    ResultArr(1) = T1 \ D1
                Else
                    ResultArr(0) = T1 \ D1
                End If
                    
                If (M = 3) Then
                    ResultArr(0) = (T1 Mod D1) \ M
                ElseIf (M = 4) Then
                    ResultArr(1) = (T1 Mod D1) \ M
                Else
                    ResultArr(2) = (T1 Mod D1) \ M
                End If
            End If
            Flag = True
        Case Else
            D1 = D1 - 1
            If D1 < 3 Then
                If (T1 - 8) > 0 Then
                    T1 = T1 - 6
                    ResultArr(3) = ResultArr(3) + 1
                ElseIf (T1 - 7) > 0 Then
                    T1 = T1 - 5
                    ResultArr(2) = ResultArr(2) + 1
                ElseIf (T1 - 6) > 0 Then
                    T1 = T1 - 4
                    ResultArr(1) = ResultArr(1) + 1
                Else
                    ResultArr(0) = ResultArr(0) + 1
                    Flag = True
                End If
                If Not Flag Then
                    D1 = 6
                End If
            End If
        End Select
    Loop
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
    End Sub

Public Function NumOfUpperTrackClips() As Integer

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".NumOfUpperTrackClips()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'This funtion will calculate the number of clips needed per upper track for
    'Hi-Lift and Vertical Lift
    '
    'Parameters:
    '   DH      : Door height (Hifh lift distance if it is a Hi-Lift door)
    '   LType   : Lift type
    Dim i As Single
    i = 0!
    If (LiftType = "High Lift") Then
        i = ((HILIFT - 9) / 30) - 1!
    ElseIf (LiftType = "Vertical Lift") Then
        i = ((DrHgt + 18) / 30) - 1!
    ElseIf (LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift") Then
        i = ((DrHgt + 6) / 30) - 1!
    End If
    If (i <= 0!) Then
        i = 0!
    Else
        If (i > 7!) Then
            i = 7!
        Else
            If ((i - Fix(i)) >= 0.5) Then
                i = Fix(i) + 1!
            Else
                i = Fix(i) + 0!
            End If
        End If
    End If
    NumOfUpperTrackClips = CInt(i)
      
   Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
 End Function

Public Sub SelectTaperedADCAPN()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectTaperedADCAPN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim TADCAQTY(4) As Integer
    Dim TADCAPN(4) As String
    Dim TADCALen(4) As Variant
    Dim TADCACutLen As String
    Dim CB As Integer
    
    Select Case HWType
    Case 2
        Select Case LiftType
        Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift", _
             "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
            'Tapered TADCA for Vertical Lift goes up to 18' or (216")DH
            If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0458"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 95.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0459"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 16
               
            ElseIf DrHgt > 84 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0460"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 107.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0461"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 16
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0462"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 131.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0463"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 18
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0464"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 155.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0465"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 20
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0466"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 179.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0467"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 22
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0468"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 203.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0469"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 24
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0470"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 227.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0471"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 26
                
            Else
                If Not (LiftType = "Vertical Lift") Or Not (LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift") Then
                    If DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                        TADCAPN(0) = "425-0472"
                        TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                        TADCALen(0) = 201
                        
                        TADCAPN(1) = "425-0473"
                        TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                        
                        TADCAPN(2) = "425-0474"
                        TADCAQTY(2) = 1
                        TADCALen(2) = 50.625
                        
                        TADCAPN(3) = "425-0475"
                        TADCAQTY(3) = 1
                        
                        CB = 36
                        
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End Select
    Case 3, 3.25
        Select Case LiftType
        Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift", "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift", _
             "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
            'Tapered TADCA for Vertical Lift goes up to 18' or (216")DH
            If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0658"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 95.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0659"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 16 + 1 'use 2" EU ADCA + (4)
               
            ElseIf DrHgt > 84 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0660"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 107.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0661"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 16 + 1
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0662"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 131.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0663"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 18 + 1
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0664"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 155.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0665"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 20 + 1
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0666"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 179.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0667"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 22 + 1
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0668"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 203.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0669"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 24 + 1
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                TADCAPN(0) = "425-0670"
                TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                TADCALen(0) = 227.625
                
                TADCAPN(1) = "425-0671"
                TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                CB = 26 + 1
                
            Else
                If Not (LiftType = "Vertical Lift") Or Not (LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift") Then
                    If DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                        TADCAPN(0) = "425-0672"
                        TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                        TADCALen(0) = 201
                        
                        TADCAPN(1) = "425-0673"
                        TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                        
                        TADCAPN(2) = "425-0674"
                        TADCAQTY(2) = 1
                        TADCALen(2) = 50.625
                        
                        TADCAPN(3) = "425-0675"
                        TADCAQTY(3) = 1
                        
                        CB = 36 + 1
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 264 Then    'for 3", use 2" ADCA 20' high part numbers
                        TADCAPN(0) = "425-0672"
                        TADCAQTY(0) = 1
                        TADCALen(0) = 201
                        
                        TADCAPN(1) = "425-0673"
                        TADCAQTY(1) = 1
                
                        TADCAPN(2) = "425-0676"
                        TADCAQTY(2) = 1
                        TADCALen(2) = 50.625
                        
                        TADCAPN(3) = "425-0677"
                        TADCAQTY(3) = 1
                        
                        CB = 36 + 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End Select
    End Select
    
    Dim tadcaSubCat As String
    Dim i As Integer
    
    If TADCAPN(0) = "" Then
        MsgBox "Cannot select EU ADCA. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select EU ADCA."
        Exit Sub
    Else
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        TADCACutLen = CutInstructionADCA(TADCALen(), TADCAQTY(0))
        For i = 0 To 3
            If TADCALen(i) = Empty Then
                If TADCACutLen <> "" Then
                    tadcaSubCat = TADCAPN(i)
                    FillSummaryGrid TADCAPN(i), TADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(tadcaSubCat), 0, "Cut (2) " & TADCACutLen
                    If TADCAPN(2) = "" Then Exit Sub
                Else
                    If TADCAPN(i) <> "" Then FillSummaryGrid TADCAPN(i), TADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(tadcaSubCat), 0
                End If
                
            ElseIf TADCAPN(i) <> "" And TADCAQTY(i) <> Empty Then
                tadcaSubCat = TADCAPN(i)
                FillSummaryGrid TADCAPN(i), TADCAQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(tadcaSubCat), 0
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub TaperedADCA_VerticalTrack(Optional TrackType As String)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".TaperedADCA_VerticalTrack()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim VerticalTrackLowerPN As String
    Dim VerticalTrackQTY As Integer
    Dim VerticalTrackLen As Integer
    Dim VTLen As String
     
    Select Case HWType
    Case 2
        If DrHgt <= 84 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1506"
            VerticalTrackLen = 78 ' inches equal to 6'-6"
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 84 And DrHgt <= 96 Then

            VerticalTrackQTY = 2

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1509"
            VerticalTrackLen = 114 ' inches equal to 9'-6"
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1510"
            VerticalTrackLen = 126 ' inches equal to 10'-6"
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2

            VerticalTrackLen = 150 ' inches equal to 12'-6"
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1517"
            VerticalTrackLen = 210 ' inches equal to 17'-6"
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1518"

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1610"

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1611"
            VerticalTrackLen = 138
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 156 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1612"

            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1616"

            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
             
        ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1617"
            VerticalTrackLen = 210

             VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1618"

        ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 156 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1712"

            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 156 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1713"

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1714"
            VerticalTrackLen = 174
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2

            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1715"
            VerticalTrackLen = 186
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1716"
            VerticalTrackLen = 198
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1717"
            VerticalTrackLen = 210
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 228 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1718"
            VerticalTrackLen = 222
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 228 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1719"
            VerticalTrackLen = 234
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 252 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1720"
            VerticalTrackLen = 246
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
        
        ElseIf DrHgt > 252 And DrHgt <= 264 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1721"
            VerticalTrackLen = 258
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 264 And DrHgt <= 276 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1722"
            VerticalTrackLen = 270
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 276 And DrHgt <= 288 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1723"
            VerticalTrackLen = 282
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
            
        ElseIf DrHgt > 288 And DrHgt <= 300 Then
            VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1724"
            VerticalTrackLen = 294
            VerticalTrackQTY = 2
        End If
    End Select

    'for all lift types
    If VerticalTrackLowerPN = "" Then
        MsgBox "Cannot select Lower Vertical Track. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Lower Vertical Track."
        Exit Sub
    Else
        VTLen = cutVerticalTrack(VerticalTrackLen, VerticalTrackQTY, TrackType)
        If VTLen <> "" Then
            FillSummaryGrid VerticalTrackLowerPN, VerticalTrackQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(VerticalTrackLowerPN), 0, "Cut (2) " & VTLen
            Exit Sub
        Else

            Exit Sub
        End If
        
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)

Public Sub TaperedADCA_UpperTrack()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".TaperedADCA_UpperTrack()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim EUUpperVTPN As String
    Dim EUUpperVTQTY As Integer
    Dim EUUpperVTLen As Integer
    Dim VTLen As String
 
 ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''

'                TUpperVTQTY = 2

'                TTUpperVTPN = "431-1519"
'                TUpperVTLen = 234
'                TUpperVTQTY = 2
'             End If
'        End Select

    Select Case LiftType
    Case "High Lift"
        Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            If HILIFT >= 17 And HILIFT <= 48 Then
                EUUpperVTPN = "431-1506"
                EUUpperVTLen = 78
                EUUpperVTQTY = 1
                
            ElseIf HILIFT > 48 And HILIFT <= 54 Then
                EUUpperVTPN = "431-1507"
                EUUpperVTLen = 90
                EUUpperVTQTY = 1
    
            ElseIf HILIFT > 54 And HILIFT <= 60 Then
                EUUpperVTPN = "431-1508"
                EUUpperVTLen = 102
                EUUpperVTQTY = 1
                
            ElseIf HILIFT > 60 And HILIFT <= 66 Then
                EUUpperVTPN = "431-1509"
                EUUpperVTLen = 114
                EUUpperVTQTY = 1
                
            ElseIf HILIFT > 66 And HILIFT <= 72 Then
                EUUpperVTPN = "431-1510"
                EUUpperVTLen = 126
                EUUpperVTQTY = 1
                
            ElseIf HILIFT > 72 And HILIFT <= 78 Then
                EUUpperVTPN = "431-1511"
                EUUpperVTLen = 138
                EUUpperVTQTY = 1
                
            ElseIf HILIFT > 78 And HILIFT <= 87 Then
                EUUpperVTPN = "431-1506"
                EUUpperVTLen = 78
                EUUpperVTQTY = 2
                
            ElseIf HILIFT > 87 And HILIFT <= 99 Then
