End Sub

'This routine selects fastening packages for woodjamb doors
Public Sub SelectLagBolts()
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectLagBolts()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

'    Dim X As Integer
'    Dim Y As Integer
'    Dim Z As Integer
'    Dim OffSet As Integer
    
'    If (HWType = 2) Then
'        OffSet = 97
'        Z = 169
'    Else
'        OffSet = 73
'        Z = 145
'    End If
'
'    X = CInt(DrHgt)
'    Y = 1
'    Do While X > Z
'        X = X - 96
'        Y = Y + 1
'    Loop
'
'    If (X <= OffSet) Then
'        FillSummaryGrid "4-0045", 1, "Track", GetSubCategory("4-0045"), 0
'    Else
'        If (X <= (OffSet + 24)) Then
'            FillSummaryGrid "4-0046", 1, "Track", GetSubCategory("4-0046"), 0
'        ElseIf (X <= (OffSet + 48)) Then
'            FillSummaryGrid "4-0047", 1, "Track", GetSubCategory("4-0047"), 0
'        Else
'            FillSummaryGrid "4-0048", 1, "Track", GetSubCategory("4-0048"), 0
'        End If
'    End If
    Dim lintQty As Integer
    Dim LagBLT As String
    Dim j As Integer
    Dim QTY As Integer
        
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift", "Low Headroom Rear Mount", "Low Headroom Front Mount"
        lintQty = RoundUp(DrHgt / 24) * 2         'for track
    Case "High Lift"
        lintQty = RoundUp((DrHgt + HILIFT) / 24) * 2
    Case "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        lintQty = RoundUp((DrHgt + DrHgt) / 24) * 2
    End Select
        
    If HWType = 2 Then
        lintQty = lintQty + 6                       'for head plate
    Else
        lintQty = lintQty + 8
    End If
    
    lintQty = lintQty + (varDoorData.intCenterPlates * 2) + 2     'for center plate & extra
    Call PicklagPkg(lintQty)
    
    For j = 0 To 3
        If (ResultLagQTY(j) > 0) Then

            If (j = 0) Then
                LagBLT = "4-0045"
                QTY = CSng(ResultLagQTY(j))
                FillSummaryGrid LagBLT, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LagBLT), 0
            ElseIf (j = 1) Then
                LagBLT = "4-0046"
                QTY = CSng(ResultLagQTY(j))
                FillSummaryGrid LagBLT, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LagBLT), 0
            ElseIf (j = 2) Then
                LagBLT = "4-0047"
                QTY = CSng(ResultLagQTY(j))
                FillSummaryGrid LagBLT, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LagBLT), 0
            Else
                LagBLT = "4-0048"
                QTY = CSng(ResultLagQTY(j))
                FillSummaryGrid LagBLT, QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(LagBLT), 0
            End If
        End If
    Next j
    
   lintQty = 0
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub
Public Sub selectJambWeatheringStrip()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectJambWeatheringStrip()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim i As Integer
    Dim H As Integer
    Dim DH As Integer
    Dim P1 As String
    Dim P2 As String
    Dim lintcutValue As Integer
    Dim lstrCutTo As String
    Dim lstrCutAmt As String
    Dim lintFound As Integer
    Dim lstrcutAmt1 As String
    Dim lstrcutAmt2 As String
    Dim JWCutLen As String
    Dim lstrCutInstructions As Variant
    Dim lstrCut As String
    Dim lstrCutFrom As String
    Dim JW_subCat As String
    
    'for New Jamb W.S.
    
    Dim JWeatherQTY(2) As Integer
    Dim JWeatherLen(2) As Variant
    Dim JWeatherPN(2) As String
    
    Dim CB As Integer
        
    If (DrHgt <= 96) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-330"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 108
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        CB = 10
      
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 96) And (DrHgt <= 120) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-331"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 132
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        CB = 12
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 120) And (DrHgt <= 144) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-332"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 156
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        CB = 14
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 144) And (DrHgt <= 168) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-333"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 180
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        CB = 16
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 168) And (DrHgt <= 192) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-334"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 204
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        CB = 18
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 192) And (DrHgt <= 216) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-335"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 228
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        CB = 20
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 216) And (DrHgt <= 228) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-330"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 108
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        JWeatherPN(1) = "152-331"
        JWeatherLen(1) = 132
        JWeatherQTY(1) = 2
        
        CB = 22
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 228) And (DrHgt <= 252) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-331"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 132
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 4
        
        CB = 24
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 252) And (DrHgt <= 276) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-331"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 132
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 2
        
        JWeatherPN(1) = "152-332"
        JWeatherLen(1) = 156
        JWeatherQTY(1) = 2
        
        CB = 26
        
    ElseIf (DrHgt > 276) And (DrHgt <= 300) Then
        JWeatherPN(0) = "152-332"
        JWeatherLen(0) = 156
        JWeatherQTY(0) = 4
        
        CB = 28
        
    End If
    
    If JWeatherPN(0) <> "" Then
    
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        
        JWCutLen = cutInstructionJWeather(JWeatherLen())
        lintcutValue = InStr(1, JWCutLen, "~")
        If lintcutValue > 0 Then
            
            lstrCutInstructions = Split(JWCutLen, "~")    ' CutTo carry ' Tag ' for DH > 14' Top/Btm/Both
            lstrCut = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(0))          ' CutLocation carry cutoff amount
            If DrHgt < 223 Then
                lstrCutTo = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(1))
                If lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutFrom
                
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrCutFrom
                    
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(2))
                    lintFound = InStr(1, lstrCutFrom, ";")
                    If lintFound > 0 Then
                        lstrcutAmt1 = Mid(lstrCutFrom, 1, lintFound - 1)
                        lstrcutAmt2 = Mid(lstrCutFrom, lintFound + 1, Len(lstrCutFrom))
                        lstrCutAmt = lstrCutTo & "; " & lstrcutAmt1 & "; " & lstrcutAmt2
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                If JWeatherQTY(0) = 4 Then
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(1))
                    If lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                        lstrCutAmt = "Cut " & JWeatherPN(0) & " (2) " & lstrCutFrom
                    ElseIf lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                        lstrCutAmt = "Cut " & JWeatherPN(0) & " (2) " & lstrCutFrom
                    ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                        lintFound = InStr(1, lstrCutFrom, ";")
                        If lintFound > 0 Then
                            lstrcutAmt1 = Mid(lstrCutFrom, 1, lintFound - 1)
                            lstrcutAmt2 = Mid(lstrCutFrom, lintFound + 1, Len(lstrCutFrom))
                            lstrCutAmt = "Cut (4) " & lstrcutAmt1 & "; " & lstrcutAmt2
    
                        End If
                    End If
                Else    'DH > 223 and PN = 2
                    lstrCutFrom = CStr(lstrCutInstructions(1))
                    If lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                        lstrCutAmt = "Cut " & JWeatherPN(0) & " (2) " & lstrCutFrom
                    ElseIf lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                        lstrCutAmt = "Cut " & JWeatherPN(1) & " (2) " & lstrCutFrom
                    ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                        lintFound = InStr(1, lstrCutFrom, ";")
                        If lintFound > 0 Then
                            lstrcutAmt1 = "Cut (2) " & Mid(lstrCutFrom, 1, lintFound - 1)
                            lstrcutAmt2 = "Cut (2) " & Mid(lstrCutFrom, lintFound + 1, Len(lstrCutFrom))
                            
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Else
            lstrCutAmt = JWCutLen  'qty = 1
        End If
        
         For i = 0 To 1
            If JWeatherPN(i) <> "" And JWeatherQTY(i) <> Empty Then
                JW_subCat = JWeatherPN(i)
                
                If lstrCut = "BTM" Then
                    If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(JWeatherQTY(0) = 4, lstrCutAmt, "") 'only in case of QTY = 4
                    If i = 1 Then FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
    
                ElseIf lstrCut = "TOP" Then
                    If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
                    If i = 1 Then FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(JWeatherQTY(0) = 4, lstrCutAmt, "") 'only in case of QTY = 4
    
                ElseIf lstrCut = "BOTH" Then
                    If JWeatherPN(1) <> "" Then
                        If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrcutAmt2 <> "", lstrcutAmt2, "") 'btm  PN = 2
                        If i = 1 Then FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrcutAmt1 <> "", lstrcutAmt1, "") 'top
                    Else
                        If i = 0 Then FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "") 'both when PN = 1
                    End If
                Else
                    FillSummaryGrid JWeatherPN(i), JWeatherQTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(JW_subCat), 0, IIf(lstrCutAmt <> "", lstrCutAmt, "")
                End If
            End If
        Next i
'    Else
'       MsgBox "Cannot select Jamb Weather Seal. " & vbCrLf & _
'        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Jamb Weather Seal."
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim HTPN_RH As String
    Dim HTPN_LH As String
    
    Select Case HWType
        Case 2

                            HTPN_LH = "431-2001"
                        Case 16

                            HTPN_RH = "431-2010"
                            HTPN_LH = "431-2011"
                        Case 16
                            HTPN_RH = "431-2040"
                            HTPN_LH = "431-2041"
                        End Select
                Case 144 To 156
                    Select Case HRadius
                        Case 12
                            HTPN_RH = "431-2012"
                            HTPN_LH = "431-2013"
                        Case 16
                            HTPN_RH = "431-2042"
                            HTPN_LH = "431-2043"
                        End Select
                Case 156 To 168

'                            If TADCA = 0 Then       'only in European ADCA(1-Piece HT goes up to 14' DH in 12" RAD
                                                    'Other than European ADCA, (1-Piece HT in SL lift with RAD 12" goes upto 13' DH

                                HTPN_LH = "431-2015"
'                            End If

                Case 168 To 180
                    Select Case HRadius
                        Case 16

                            HTPN_LH = "431-2047"
                        End Select
                Case 180 To 192
                    Select Case HRadius
                        Case 16
                            HTPN_RH = "431-2048"
                            HTPN_LH = "431-2049"
                        End Select
                End Select
        Case 3
            Select Case DrHgt
                 Case Is <= 84
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3006"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3007"
                 Case 84 To 96
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3008"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3009"
                 Case 96 To 108
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3010"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3011"

                     HTPN_LH = "431-3013"
                 Case 120 To 132
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3014"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3015"
                 Case 132 To 144
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3016"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3017"
                 Case 144 To 156
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3018"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3019"
                 Case 156 To 168
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3020"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3021"
                 Case 168 To 180
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3022"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3023"
                 Case 180 To 192
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3024"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3025"
                 Case 192 To 204
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3026"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3027"
                 Case 204 To 216
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3028"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3029"
                 Case 216 To 228
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3030"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3031"
                 Case 228 To 240
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3032"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3033"
                 Case 240 To 252
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3034"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3035"
                 Case 252 To 264
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3036"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3037"
                 Case 264 To 276
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3038"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3039"
                 Case 276 To 288
                      HTPN_RH = "431-3040"
                      HTPN_LH = "431-3041"
                 End Select
            
        Case 3.25
            Select Case DrHgt
                Case Is <= 84
                    HTPN_RH = "431-3056"
                    HTPN_LH = "431-3057"
                Case 84 To 96
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3058"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3059"
                Case 96 To 108
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3060"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3061"
                Case 108 To 120
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3062"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3063"
                Case 120 To 132
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3064"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3065"
                Case 132 To 144
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3066"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3067"
                Case 144 To 156
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3068"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3069"
                Case 156 To 168
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3070"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3071"
                Case 168 To 180
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3072"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3073"
                Case 180 To 192
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3074"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3075"
                Case 192 To 204
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3076"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3077"
                Case 204 To 216
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3078"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3079"
                Case 216 To 228
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3080"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3081"
                Case 228 To 240
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3082"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3083"
                Case 240 To 252
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3084"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3085"
                Case 252 To 264
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3086"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3087"
                Case 264 To 276
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3088"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3089"
                Case 276 To 288
                     HTPN_RH = "431-3090"
                     HTPN_LH = "431-3091"
                End Select
        End Select
            
        If HTPN_RH = "" Or HTPN_LH = "" Then
            MsgBox "Cannot select 1-Piece Hrz Tracks for standard Lift." & vbCrLf & _
                    "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select 1-Piece Hrz Tracks for standard Lift."
            Exit Sub
        Else
            'cut instruction for sloped Horizontal
            Dim lsngCutSlopHA As Single

            If SlopHAng > 0 And SlopHAng > 3.5 Then
                lsngCutSlopHA = 90 - SlopHAng
            End If
                
            If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then

            End If
        End If
    Exit Sub

    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub SelectBearings()
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectBearings()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim blnFlag As Boolean
    
    blnFlag = False
    If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
         
        If varDoorData.FlangeBearing = True Or DrWgt > 1000 Then
            FillSummaryGrid "411-189", 2, "Track", "Flange Bearings", 0
        Else
            If Not (LiftType = "Low Headroom Front Mount") Or Not (LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount") Then
                If (varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-384" And LiftType = "Standard Lift" And HWType = 3) _
                    Or (varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D5750-120" And LiftType = "High Lift" And HWType = 3) Or _
                        varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "1350-336" Then
                        FillSummaryGrid "411-189", 2, "Track", "Flange Bearings", 0
                Else
                    FillSummaryGrid "411-226", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("411-226"), 0
                End If
            End If
        End If
    ElseIf varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then
        If Not (LiftType = "Low Headroom Front Mount") Or Not (LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount") Then
            FillSummaryGrid "411-227", 2, "Track", GetSubCategory("411-227"), 0
        End If
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    Call DisplayError(llngErrNo, IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub selectCurvesHT()
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectCurvesHT()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This sub-routine will pick up curve tracks for standard lift and high lift.
    'For 2-inch hardware, curves picked up depend on Radius Type.

    Dim P1 As String
    Dim P2 As String
    Dim DH As Integer
    
    DH = CInt(DrHgt)
    Select Case LiftType
        Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift"
            Select Case HWType
                Case 2
                    If HRadius = 12 Then
                        P1 = "431-0050"
                        P2 = "431-0051"
                    Else
                        P1 = "431-0052"
                        P2 = "431-0053"
                    End If
                Case 3
                    P1 = "431-0060"
                    P2 = "431-0061"
                Case 3.25
                    P1 = "431-0070"
                    P2 = "431-0071"
                End Select
        End Select
        
        FillSummaryGrid P1, 1, "Track", GetSubCategory(P1), 0
        FillSummaryGrid P2, 1, "Track", GetSubCategory(P2), 0
        
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub selectHTCSplice()
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectHTCSplice()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This sub-routine will select Horizontal Track and Curve Splices.
    'These brackets are available for standard lift and hi-lift only.
    '2" hardware: 426-1001; Qty = 2
    '3" hardware: 426-1002; Qty = 2
    'Super 3" hardware: 426-1002; Qty = 2
    
    Dim DH As Integer
    Dim Partno As String
    Dim CB As Integer
    
    
    DH = CInt(DrHgt)
    If (LiftType = "Standard Lift") Or (LiftType = "High Lift") Then
        Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                Partno = "426-1001"
                CB = 4
            Case Else
                Partno = "426-1002"
                CB = 8
            End Select
    End If
    
    FillSummaryGrid Partno, 2, "Track", GetSubCategory(Partno), 0
    ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB

    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function selectHTracks() As Integer

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectHTracks()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim LenHT As Integer
    
    'only for sep. curve, select horizontal track based on LenHT
    Select Case LiftType
        Case "Standard Lift"
            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = False Then LenHT = DrHgt - 18  'From Bill Leather, changed on May 16, 2003 : L = DH - 12
        Case "High Lift"
            If varDoorData.Track.blnOnePieceHT = False Then LenHT = DrHgt - (HILIFT - 18)   'L = DH - (HILIFT + 9)
        End Select
        
    selectHTracks = LenHT
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub selectHighLiftOnePieceHT()
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectHighLiftOnePieceHT()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'This sub-routine will pick up 1 Piece Horizontal Tracks for high lift.

    Dim P1 As String
    Dim P2 As String
    Dim DH As Integer
    Dim L As Integer
    Dim HL As Integer
    
    DH = CInt(DrHgt)
    HL = CInt(HILIFT)
    L = 0
    
    'Calculate stock length for horizontal tracks
    'L = DH + 39 - HILIFT    'Ray and Len discussed and changed to this on Oct 27,97.
                        'DH + 3'3" - HL New disciplines from LEN on Oct 27, 97.
      
    L = DH - HL         ' Bill Leather provided new length on May 16, 2003.
      
    Select Case HWType
    Case 2
    
        If (L <= 60) Then       '90
            P1 = "431-2026"
            P2 = "431-2027"
        ElseIf (L >= 61) And (L <= 72) Then     '102
            P1 = "431-2028"
            P2 = "431-2029"
        ElseIf (L >= 73) And (L <= 84) Then    '114
            P1 = "431-2030"
            P2 = "431-2031"
        ElseIf (L >= 85) And (L <= 96) Then    '126
            P1 = "431-2032"
            P2 = "431-2033"
        ElseIf (L >= 97) And (L <= 108) Then    '138
            P1 = "431-2034"
            P2 = "431-2035"
        ElseIf (L >= 109) And (L <= 120) Then    '150
            P1 = "431-2036"
            P2 = "431-2037"
        ElseIf (L >= 121) And (L <= 132) Then    '162
            P1 = "431-2038"
            P2 = "431-2039"
        ElseIf (L >= 133) And (L <= 144) Then    '174
            P1 = "431-2040"
            P2 = "431-2041"
        ElseIf (L >= 145) And (L <= 156) Then    '186
            P1 = "431-2042"
            P2 = "431-2043"
        ElseIf (L >= 157) And (L <= 168) Then    '198
            P1 = "431-2044"
            P2 = "431-2045"
        ElseIf (L >= 169) And (L <= 180) Then    '210
            P1 = "431-2046"
            P2 = "431-2047"
        ElseIf (L >= 181) And (L <= 192) Then    '222
            P1 = "431-2048"
            P2 = "431-2049"
        End If
    
    Case 3
     
        If (L <= 84) Then      '114
            P1 = "431-3006"
            P2 = "431-3007"
        ElseIf (L >= 85) And (L <= 96) Then          '126
            P1 = "431-3008"
            P2 = "431-3009"
        ElseIf (L >= 97) And (L <= 108) Then         '138
            P1 = "431-3010"
            P2 = "431-3011"
        ElseIf (L >= 109) And (L <= 120) Then        '150
            P1 = "431-3012"
            P2 = "431-3013"
        ElseIf (L >= 121) And (L <= 132) Then        '162
            P1 = "431-3014"
            P2 = "431-3015"
        ElseIf (L >= 133) And (L <= 144) Then        '174
            P1 = "431-3016"
            P2 = "431-3017"
        ElseIf (L >= 145) And (L <= 156) Then        '186
            P1 = "431-3018"
            P2 = "431-3019"
        ElseIf (L >= 157) And (L <= 168) Then        '198
            P1 = "431-3020"
            P2 = "431-3021"
        ElseIf (L >= 169) And (L <= 180) Then        '210
            P1 = "431-3022"
            P2 = "431-3023"
        ElseIf (L >= 181) And (L <= 192) Then        '222
            P1 = "431-3024"
            P2 = "431-3025"
        ElseIf (L >= 193) And (L <= 204) Then        '234
            P1 = "431-3026"
            P2 = "431-3027"
        ElseIf (L >= 205) And (L <= 216) Then        '246
            P1 = "431-3028"
            P2 = "431-3029"
        ElseIf (L >= 217) And (L <= 228) Then        '258
            P1 = "431-3030"
            P2 = "431-3031"
        ElseIf (L >= 229) And (L <= 240) Then        '270
            P1 = "431-3032"
            P2 = "431-3033"
        ElseIf (L >= 241) And (L <= 252) Then        '234
            P1 = "431-3034"
            P2 = "431-3035"
        ElseIf (L >= 253) And (L <= 264) Then        '246
            P1 = "431-3036"
            P2 = "431-3037"
        ElseIf (L >= 265) And (L <= 276) Then        '258
            P1 = "431-3038"
            P2 = "431-3039"
        ElseIf (L >= 277) And (L <= 288) Then        '270
            P1 = "431-3040"
            P2 = "431-3041"
        End If
     
     Case 3.25
    
        If (L <= 84) Then
            P1 = "431-3056"
            P2 = "431-3057"
        ElseIf (L >= 85) And (L <= 96) Then
            P1 = "431-3058"
            P2 = "431-3059"
        ElseIf (L >= 97) And (L <= 108) Then
            P1 = "431-3060"
            P2 = "431-3061"
        ElseIf (L >= 109) And (L <= 120) Then
            P1 = "431-3062"
            P2 = "431-3063"
        ElseIf (L >= 121) And (L <= 132) Then
            P1 = "431-3064"
            P2 = "431-3065"
        ElseIf (L >= 133) And (L <= 144) Then
            P1 = "431-3066"
            P2 = "431-3067"
        ElseIf (L >= 145) And (L <= 156) Then
            P1 = "431-3068"
            P2 = "431-3069"
        ElseIf (L >= 156) And (L <= 168) Then
            P1 = "431-3070"
            P2 = "431-3071"
        ElseIf (L >= 169) And (L <= 180) Then
            P1 = "431-3072"
            P2 = "431-3073"
        ElseIf (L >= 181) And (L <= 192) Then
            P1 = "431-3074"
            P2 = "431-3075"
        ElseIf (L >= 193) And (L <= 204) Then
            P1 = "431-3076"
            P2 = "431-3077"
        ElseIf (L >= 205) And (L <= 216) Then
            P1 = "431-3078"
            P2 = "431-3079"
        ElseIf (L >= 217) And (L <= 228) Then
            P1 = "431-3080"
            P2 = "431-3081"
        ElseIf (L >= 229) And (L <= 240) Then
            P1 = "431-3082"
            P2 = "431-3083"
        ElseIf (L >= 241) And (L <= 252) Then
            P1 = "431-3084"
            P2 = "431-3085"
        ElseIf (L >= 253) And (L <= 264) Then
            P1 = "431-3086"
            P2 = "431-3087"
        ElseIf (L >= 265) And (L <= 276) Then
            P1 = "431-3088"
            P2 = "431-3089"
        ElseIf (L >= 277) And (L <= 288) Then
            P1 = "431-3090"
            P2 = "431-3091"
        End If
    End Select
  
    If P1 <> "" And P2 <> "" Then
        FillSummaryGrid P1, 1, "Track", GetSubCategory(P1), 0
        FillSummaryGrid P2, 1, "Track", GetSubCategory(P2), 0
    Else
        MsgBox "Cannot select 1-Piece Horizontal Track. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select 1-Piece Horizontal Track."
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function selectDHTracks() As Integer

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectDHTracks()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This sub-routine will select Double Horizontal Tracks.
    
    Dim L As Integer
    Dim DH As Integer
    
    DH = CInt(DrHgt)
    L = DH - 6
    
    selectDHTracks = L
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub select_sepCurve_EuropeanTracks()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".select_sepCurve_EuropeanTracks()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim HRZTrack_PN As String
    Dim HRZTrack_Len As Integer
    Dim HRZTrack_QTY As Integer
        
    Select Case HWType
        Case 2
            Select Case LiftType
                Case "Standard Lift"
                    If HRadius = 12 Then
                        If DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 180 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1513"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 162
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 180 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1514"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 174
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1515"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 186
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1516"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 198
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 228 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1517"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 210
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1518"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 222
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                        End If
                    Else   'radius = 16
                        If DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1515"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 186
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1516"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 198
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 228 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1517"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 210
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                            
                        ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                            HRZTrack_PN = "431-1518"
                            HRZTrack_Len = 222
                            HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                        End If
                    End If
                    
                Case "High Lift"
                    If DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
                        HRZTrack_PN = "431-1514"
                        HRZTrack_Len = 174
                        HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                        HRZTrack_PN = "431-1515"
                        HRZTrack_Len = 186
                        HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 228 Then
                        HRZTrack_PN = "431-1516"
                        HRZTrack_Len = 198
                        HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                        HRZTrack_PN = "431-1517"
                        HRZTrack_Len = 210
                        HRZTrack_QTY = 2
                    End If
                End Select
        End Select
        
       FillSummaryGrid HRZTrack_PN, HRZTrack_QTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(HRZTrack_PN), 0
        ''''''''''''''''1-piece HT for LHR-rear/front schedule
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub selectHRZTracks_LHR()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectHRZTracks_LHR()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
'    'prints HRZ. tracks part no. for European ADCA in LHR - Rear/ Front
     
    Dim HRZPN As String
    Dim cutHT As String
    Dim HTLen As Integer
    
    'If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
        Select Case LiftType
            Case "Low Headroom Front Mount", "Low Headroom Rear Mount"
                If HWType = 2 Then

                        HRZPN = "431-1511"
                        HTLen = 138
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 156 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1512"
                        HTLen = 150
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 156 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1513"
                        HTLen = 162
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 180 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1514"
                        HTLen = 174
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 180 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1515"
                        HTLen = 186
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1516"
                        HTLen = 198
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1517"
                        HTLen = 210
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 228 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1518"
                        HTLen = 222
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 228 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1519"
                        HTLen = 234
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 252 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1520"
                        HTLen = 246
                        
                    End If
                
                ElseIf HWType = 3 Then
                    If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1606"
                        HTLen = 78
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 84 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1607"
                        HTLen = 90
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 108 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1608"
                        HTLen = 102
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 108 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1609"
                        HTLen = 114
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 132 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1610"
                        HTLen = 126
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 132 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1611"
                        HTLen = 138
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 156 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1612"
                        HTLen = 150
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 156 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1613"
                        HTLen = 162
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 180 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1614"
                        HTLen = 174
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 180 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1615"
                        HTLen = 186
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1616"
                        HTLen = 198
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1617"
                        HTLen = 210
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 228 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1618"
                        HTLen = 222
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 228 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1619"
                        HTLen = 234
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 252 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1620"
                        HTLen = 246
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 264 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1621"
                        HTLen = 258
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 276 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1622"
                        HTLen = 270
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 228 And DrHgt <= 288 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1623"
                        HTLen = 282
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 300 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1624"
                        HTLen = 294
                    End If
                
                ElseIf HWType = 3.25 Then
                    If DrHgt <= 84 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1706"
                        HTLen = 78
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 84 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1707"
                        HTLen = 90
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 108 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1708"
                        HTLen = 102
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 108 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1709"
                        HTLen = 114
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 132 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1710"
                        HTLen = 126
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 132 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1711"
                        HTLen = 138
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 156 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1712"
                        HTLen = 150
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 156 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1713"
                        HTLen = 162
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 180 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1714"
                        HTLen = 174
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 180 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1715"
                        HTLen = 186
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 204 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1716"
                        HTLen = 198
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1717"
                        HTLen = 210
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 228 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1718"
                        HTLen = 222
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 228 And DrHgt <= 240 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1719"
                        HTLen = 234
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 252 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1720"
                        HTLen = 246
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 204 And DrHgt <= 264 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1721"
                        HTLen = 258
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 276 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1722"
                        HTLen = 270
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 228 And DrHgt <= 288 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1723"
                        HTLen = 282
                        
                    ElseIf DrHgt > 240 And DrHgt <= 300 Then
                        HRZPN = "431-1724"
                        HTLen = 294
                    End If
                End If  'hwTYpe
        End Select  'Lift type
    'End If
    
    Dim lintQty As Integer
    Dim HTQTY As Integer
    Dim lstrPN_QTY As Variant
    Dim lstrCutVTInst As String
    Dim lReturnValue As String
    Dim lintcutValue As Integer
    
    cutHT = cut_HRZTrack_LHR(HTLen)
    
    lReturnValue = IsPNAlreadyExist(HRZPN, "HRZTrack")    'finds the same part no. in grid and returns qty 7 cut instructions
    lintcutValue = InStr(1, lReturnValue, "~")
    
    If lintcutValue > 0 Then    'cut instruction exist with the same PN
        lstrPN_QTY = Split(lReturnValue, "~")
        lintQty = Int(CStr(lstrPN_QTY(0)))
        lstrCutVTInst = CStr(lstrPN_QTY(1))
        If lintQty > 0 Then
            Call RollBack("Track", True, HRZPN)
            HTQTY = 4 + lintQty
        Else
            HTQTY = 4
        End If
    Else                        'no cut instruction exist with the same PN
        If lReturnValue > 0 Then
            Call RollBack("Track", True, HRZPN)
            HTQTY = 4 + lReturnValue
        Else
            HTQTY = 4
        End If
    End If
    
    If cutHT <> "" And lstrCutVTInst = "" Then
        FillSummaryGrid HRZPN, HTQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(HRZPN), 0, cutHT
    ElseIf cutHT <> "" And lstrCutVTInst <> "" Then
        FillSummaryGrid HRZPN, HTQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(HRZPN), 0, lstrCutVTInst & vbNewLine & cutHT
    ElseIf cutHT = "" And lstrCutVTInst <> "" Then
        FillSummaryGrid HRZPN, HTQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(HRZPN), 0, lstrCutVTInst
    Else
        FillSummaryGrid HRZPN, HTQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(HRZPN), 0
    End If
            
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler

    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'Dim vardoordata.sngshaftcenter As Single
    Dim reqAmount As Single

    varDoorData.sngShaftCenter = 0

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''

    
'    'Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType

    varDoorData.sngShaftCenter = GetFloorToShaftCenter
    
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    If varDoorData.sngShaftCenter > 0 Then
        reqAmount = (varDoorData.sngShaftCenter - 30) * 2
        If reqAmount >= 24 Then
'            totalLen = Abs(reqAmount - 312) * 2        '312 is the amount given in package
            
            'Bill L - Modified on feb 27, 04 - All we need is (shaft centre - 30) x 2 inches of chain.  The package already has 312" of chain.  We don't have to multiply the absolute value x 2, we've already multiplied to get the required amount.
            totalLen = Abs(reqAmount - 312)
            varDoorData.ExtraChainReq = totalLen    'total extra chain length req.
            Dim MetLen As Single
            ClearConObj
            aCon.Inch = totalLen
            aCon.mmcalc
            MetLen = aCon.mm
            If DrHgt > 168 Or (DrHgt + HILIFT) > 168 Then
                Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                    Case 2, 3, 4, 5
                    Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 9, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                        Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
                        If MsgBox("Do you wish to add extra handchain that may be required for this door?", vbApplicationModal + vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "Extra handchain") = vbYes Then
                            
                            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
                            Case 3, 4, 11
                                ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                                Call RollBack("Sections", True, "131-107")
                                ''''''''''END Change in .NET
                                Call FillSummaryGrid("131-107", IIf(MeasU = 1, totalLen, MetLen), "Track", GetSubCategory("131-107"), totalLen)
                            Case 2
                                If RollBack("Track", True, "131-109") = True Then Call FillSummaryGrid("131-109", IIf(MeasU = 1, totalLen + 312, MetLen + 7924.8), "Track", GetSubCategory("131-109"), totalLen + 312)
                            Case 5
                                ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                                Call RollBack("Sections", True, "131-109")
                                ''''''''''END Change in .NET
                                Call FillSummaryGrid("131-109", IIf(MeasU = 1, totalLen, MetLen), "Track", GetSubCategory("131-109"), totalLen)

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                            Case 9
                                Call RollBack("Sections", True, "131-107")
                                Call FillSummaryGrid("131-107", IIf(MeasU = 1, totalLen, MetLen), "Track", GetSubCategory("131-107"), totalLen)
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                            
                            End Select
                        End If
                    End Select
            End If
                
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub calShaftLength()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".calShaftLength()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim MinShaftLen As Single       ' min Shaft Length
    Dim ShaftLen As Single          ' Stock Shaft Length
    Dim cutShaft As Single
    Dim cutshaftLoc As String
    Dim NoSprg As Integer
    Dim WoundSprgLen As Single
    Dim cutShaft_Center As Single
    Dim NoSmallSprg As Single
    Dim NoBigSprg As Single
    Dim totalshaftlen As Single     'Total = Left Shaft length + Right Shaft length
    Dim CheckCut As Single          'no. springs divede by 2 & add 1
    Dim newMinShaftLen As Single
    Dim L1 As Double
    Dim L2 As Double
    Dim QTY_LHM As Integer
    Dim QTY_RHM As Integer
    Dim lblnModifiedL1 As Boolean
    Dim TotalShaftReq As Single
    Dim lsngFactor As Single
    
    lblnModifiedL1 = False
    
    couplerValue = IIf(GetCouplerAmt = True, 6, 8)
        
    If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D6375-164" Or _
        varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-120" Then
        If HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
            Case 1, 7, 8                        'tube shaft
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 12.5    '14.5
                If varDoorData.ShaftTube = True Then
                    If CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) <= 2 Then
                        MinShaftLen = MinShaftLen - 2.5
                    End If
                End If
                

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10              'solid shaft
            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11        'solid shaft

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 12.5    '14.5
                If CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) > 0.5 And CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) <= 2 Then
                    MinShaftLen = MinShaftLen - (CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) - 0.5)
                    lblnModifiedL1 = True
                End If
            End Select
        End If
    Else
        If HWType = 2 Then
            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
            Case 1, 7, 8
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 9
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10
            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 9
            End Select
            

        ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
            Case 1, 7, 8
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 10.5
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10
            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 10.5
            End Select
        End If
    End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'    Select Case HWType
'    Case 2
'        AvailShaftSpace = DrWidth + 3 - (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) - (IIf(CouplNum > 0, 1, 0) * couplerValue)
'    Case 3
'        AvailShaftSpace = DrWidth + 4 - (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) - (IIf(CouplNum > 0, 1, 0) * couplerValue)
'    End Select

    AvailShaftSpace = GetAvailableShaftSpace(HWType, DrWidth, varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, CouplNum, couplerValue)

''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    
    Dim lsngsqfact As Single
        'check to see coupler is selected; couplNum=1
        If CouplNum > 0 Then
            If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).blnMismatched = False Then   'match sol (Gird1)
                'calculates #springs needed for the solution
                NoSprg = UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring()) + 1
                
                'calculates wound spring length just like in mismatch springs selection for Round & Square WireType
                If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).strSpringWireType = "Square" Then
                    lsngsqfact = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize, _
                                                 varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
                
                    WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length + Winds) * _
                                    (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize * lsngsqfact) + 3
                Else
                    WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length) + (Winds * varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize) + 3
                End If
                
                ' possible problem
                If (WoundSprgLen * NoSprg) + _
                    (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
                    > (AvailShaftSpace - (2 * NoSprg)) Then
                        Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
                        MsgBox "It is recommended that you use auxiliary shaft.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Auxiliary Shaft."
                        
                        'no need to have an extra msg - Bill approved
'                        If (WoundSprgLen * NoSprg) + _
'                            (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
'                            > (AvailShaftSpace - (1 * NoSprg)) Then
'                            Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
'                                MsgBox "This solution will fit on the shaft but requires tight assembly.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Tight assembly. "
'                        End If
                End If
                
                'matched - odd # and even # of springs
                Dim arrSpringBlock() As Single
                ReDim arrSpringBlock(NoSprg)
                Dim RemainSpace As Single
                
                'find total QTY for LHM and RHM springs
                QTY_LHM = GetSprQtyPerSide(SpringIndex, False)
                QTY_RHM = GetSprQtyPerSide(SpringIndex, True)
                
                'simple solution has same wound spring length for all springs
                RemainSpace = AvailShaftSpace - (WoundSprgLen * NoSprg)
                
                'Calculate the Spring block
                Dim i As Integer
                For i = 0 To NoSprg - 1
                    arrSpringBlock(i) = (RemainSpace / NoSprg) + WoundSprgLen
                Next i
                
                If QTY_LHM >= QTY_RHM Then
                    L1 = arrSpringBlock(0) * Int(NoSprg / 2) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                Else
                    L1 = arrSpringBlock(0) * Fix(NoSprg / 2) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                End If
                                        
                If lblnModifiedL1 = True Then
                    L1 = L1 - ((DrWidth + 12.5) - MinShaftLen) / 2
                End If
                ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                'condition to re-calculate the L1
                If NoSprg = 1 And CouplNum = 1 Then
                    If L1 < 20 Then L1 = 20
                End If
                ''''''''''END Change in .NET

                L2 = MinShaftLen - L1
                
                'for operator - Chainhoist, Dyna-Hoist or jackshaft add 2"
                Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType

                Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
                    If varDoorData.OperatorLeft = True Then
                        L1 = L1 + 2
                    Else
                        L2 = L2 + 2
                    End If
                End Select
            
                If varDoorData.ShaftTube = False Then
                    '1", 1-1/4" w/coupler
                    TotalShaftReq = L1 + L2 'for SL, HL, VL, LHR-VL, LHRFM
                    
                    If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                        L1 = L1 + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) 'need to add drum width to individual length w/coupler
                        L2 = L2 + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping)
                        TotalShaftReq = L1 + L2 're-calculate the total shaft length for LHRRM
                        
                        lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(TotalShaftReq - Fix(TotalShaftReq)) 'round to the nearest 1/2"
                        TotalShaftReq = Fix(TotalShaftReq) + lsngFactor 'final shaft length for LHRRM
                    End If
                        
                    If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                        Call OneInchShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)   '# of springs > 1
                    ElseIf varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then
                        Call OneInchQuaterShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)
                    End If
                End If
                    
            Else    'mismatch sol
                
                Dim small_WoundSprgLen As Single
                Dim big_WoundSprgLen As Single
                Dim NoMisMatchSprg As Integer
                Dim QTYSmall_LHM As Integer
                Dim QTYSmall_RHM As Integer
                Dim QTYBig_LHM As Integer
                Dim QTYBig_RHM As Integer
                Dim QTYSmallSprg As Integer
                Dim QTYBigSprg As Integer
                Dim SumSB_LHM As Double
                Dim SumSB_RHM As Double
                Dim QTYSBB_RHM As Integer
                Dim QTYSBB_LHM As Integer
                Dim SumSBB_RHM As Double
                Dim SumSBB_LHM As Double
                Dim SBB As Double
                Dim SBS As Double
                                   
                'no. of LHM & RHM small mismatch springs
                For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                    If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize Then
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            QTYSmall_RHM = QTYSmall_RHM + 1
                        Else
                            QTYSmall_LHM = QTYSmall_LHM + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                Next
                                
                'no of LHM & RHM Big mismatch springs
                For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                    If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize Then
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            QTYBig_RHM = QTYBig_RHM + 1
                        Else
                            QTYBig_LHM = QTYBig_LHM + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                Next
                'total # QTY_LHM
                QTY_LHM = QTYSmall_LHM + QTYBig_LHM
                
                'total # QTY_RHM
                QTY_RHM = QTYSmall_RHM + QTYBig_RHM
                
                'total # of small mismatch springs
                QTYSmallSprg = QTYSmall_LHM + QTYSmall_RHM
                
                'total # of Big mismatch springs
                QTYBigSprg = QTYBig_LHM + QTYBig_RHM
                
                'total # of mismatch spring
                NoMisMatchSprg = QTYSmallSprg + QTYBigSprg
                        
                'calculates wound spring length for Round & Square WireType
                If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).strSpringWireType = "Square" Then
                    
                    Dim lsngsqfact_smallSprg As Single
                    Dim lsngsqfact_bigsprg As Single
                    
                    'retreives wound spring length and convert it into inches
                    lsngsqfact_smallSprg = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize, _
                                 varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
                    
                    small_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length + Winds) * _
                                         varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize * lsngsqfact_smallSprg + 3
                                         
                    lsngsqfact_bigsprg = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize, _
                                 varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
                                 
                    big_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).Length + Winds) * _
                                         varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize * lsngsqfact_bigsprg + 3
                Else
                
                    'retreives wound spring length
                    small_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length) + (Winds * varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize) + 3
                    
                    'retreives wound spring length
                    big_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).Length) + (Winds * varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize) + 3
                End If
                
                'possible problem
                If (small_WoundSprgLen * NoSmallSprg) + (big_WoundSprgLen * NoBigSprg) + _
                    (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
                    > (AvailShaftSpace - (2 * NoMisMatchSprg)) Then
                        Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
                        MsgBox "It is recommended that you use auxiliary shaft.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Auxiliary Shaft."
                        
                        'same as above
'                        If (small_WoundSprgLen * NoSmallSprg) + (big_WoundSprgLen * NoBigSprg) + _
'                            (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
'                            > (AvailShaftSpace - (1 * NoMisMatchSprg)) Then
'                                Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
'                                MsgBox "This solution will fit on the shaft but requires tight assembly.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Tight assembly."
'                        End If
                End If
                
                'calculates remaining space for mismatch springs
                RemainSpace = AvailShaftSpace - ((small_WoundSprgLen * QTYSmallSprg) + (big_WoundSprgLen * QTYBigSprg))
                                              
                'spring block of big springs - does not matter LHM or RHM for calculation only
                SBB = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + big_WoundSprgLen

                'spring block of small springs - does not matter LHM or RHM for calculation only
                SBS = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + small_WoundSprgLen
                               
                If QTY_LHM > 0 Then
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(QTY_LHM - 1)
                Else
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(0) 'UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_LHM()) + 1
                End If

                If QTY_RHM > 0 Then
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(QTY_RHM - 1)
                Else
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(0) 'UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_RHM()) + 1
                End If
                
'                ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(QTY_LHM - 1)  'commented September 12/05
'                ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(QTY_RHM - 1)
                
                Dim lindex As Integer
                Dim lCount As Integer
                lindex = 0
                lCount = 0
                
                'LHM(B&S) and RHM(B&S) - Spring Block
                For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                    If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize Then
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).RHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + small_WoundSprgLen
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize
                            lindex = lindex + 1
                        End If
                    Else
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).RHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + big_WoundSprgLen
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize
                            lindex = lindex + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                 Next
                
                 For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                     If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize Then
                         If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = False Then
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).LHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + small_WoundSprgLen
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize
                             lCount = lCount + 1
                         End If
                     Else
                         If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = False Then
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).LHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + big_WoundSprgLen
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize
                             lCount = lCount + 1
                         End If
                    End If
                 Next
                
                'Sum of spring block of LHM springs - include small and big
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_LHM())
                    SumSB_LHM = SumSB_LHM + SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(i).LHM
                Next i
                
                'Sum of spring block of RHM springs - include small and big
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_RHM())
                    SumSB_RHM = SumSB_RHM + SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(i).RHM
                Next i
                                
                'find the QTY for SBB_RHM also find the sum of SBB_RHM
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_RHM())
                   If SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(i).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize Then
                        QTYSBB_RHM = QTYSBB_RHM + 1
                        SumSBB_RHM = SumSBB_RHM + SBB
                   End If
                Next
                        
                'find the QTY for SBB_LHM also find the sum of SBB_LHM
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_LHM())
                   If SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(i).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize Then
                        QTYSBB_LHM = QTYSBB_LHM + 1
                        SumSBB_LHM = SumSBB_LHM + SBB
                   End If
                Next
                        
                'Check to see mismatch balance or Not Balance
                If Abs(QTY_LHM - QTY_RHM) <= 1 Then     'balance mismatch spring - difference of 1 spring b/w LHM and RHM
                    L1 = SumSB_LHM + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                Else                                    'NOT balance mismatch spring - difference of > 1 spring b/w LHM and RHM
                    If QTY_LHM < QTY_RHM Then
                       If QTY_RHM - Int(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) <= QTYSBB_RHM Then
                           L1 = SumSB_LHM + SBB * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_LHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                       Else
                           L1 = SumSB_LHM + SumSBB_RHM + SBS * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_LHM - QTYSBB_RHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                        End If
                    Else
                        If QTY_LHM - Int(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) <= QTYSBB_LHM Then
                            L1 = SumSB_LHM - SBB * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_RHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                        Else
                            L1 = SumSB_LHM - SumSBB_LHM - SBS * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_RHM - QTYSBB_LHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                
                 If lblnModifiedL1 = True Then
                    L1 = L1 - ((DrWidth + 12.5) - MinShaftLen) / 2
                End If
                    
                L2 = MinShaftLen - L1
                
                'for operator - Chainhoist, Dyna-Hoist or jackshaft add 2"
                Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10
                    Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                        If varDoorData.OperatorLeft = True Then
                            L1 = L1 + 2
                        Else
                            L2 = L2 + 2
                        End If
                End Select

                If varDoorData.ShaftTube = False Then
                    TotalShaftReq = L1 + L2
                    If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                        Call OneInchShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)
                    ElseIf varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then
                        Call OneInchQuaterShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            
        Else
            'couplNum=0
            Dim TubeShaftLen As Double
            
            If varDoorData.ShaftTube = True Then
            
                If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                    MinShaftLen = (DrWidth + 10) + (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) * 2)
                    lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(MinShaftLen - Fix(MinShaftLen))
                    MinShaftLen = Fix(MinShaftLen) + lsngFactor
                End If
                
                Call OneInchTubeShaft_PN(MinShaftLen, 0, 0)
            Else
                If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                    ShaftLen = MinShaftLen
                    
                    Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
                        Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
                            ShaftLen = ShaftLen + 2
                    End Select
                    
                    If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                        ShaftLen = ShaftLen + (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) * 2)
                        lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(ShaftLen - Fix(ShaftLen))
                        ShaftLen = Fix(ShaftLen) + lsngFactor
                    End If
                    
                    Call OneInchShaft_PN(ShaftLen, 0, 0)
                
                ElseIf varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then
                    ShaftLen = MinShaftLen
                    
                    Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
                        Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
                            ShaftLen = ShaftLen + 2
                    End Select
                    
                    If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                        ShaftLen = ShaftLen + (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) * 2)
                        lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(ShaftLen - Fix(ShaftLen))
                        ShaftLen = Fix(ShaftLen) + lsngFactor
                    End If
                    
                    Call OneInchQuaterShaft_PN(ShaftLen, 0, 0)
                End If
            End If
        End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub
Public Sub calShaftLength_AuxillaryShaft()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".calShaftLength()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim MinShaftLen As Single       ' min Shaft Length
    Dim ShaftLen As Single          ' Stock Shaft Length
    Dim cutShaft As Single
    Dim cutshaftLoc As String
