    Dim NoSprg As Integer
    Dim WoundSprgLen As Single
    Dim cutShaft_Center As Single
    Dim NoSmallSprg As Single
    Dim NoBigSprg As Single
    Dim totalshaftlen As Single     'Total = Left Shaft length + Right Shaft length
    Dim CheckCut As Single          'no. springs divede by 2 & add 1
    Dim newMinShaftLen As Single
    Dim L1 As Double
    Dim L2 As Double
    Dim QTY_LHM As Integer
    Dim QTY_RHM As Integer
    Dim lblnModifiedL1 As Boolean
    Dim TotalShaftReq As Single
    Dim lsngFactor As Single
    
    lblnModifiedL1 = False
    
    couplerValue = IIf(GetCouplerAmt = True, 6, 8)
        
    If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D6375-164" Or _
        varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-120" Then
        If HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
            Case 1, 7, 8                        'tube shaft
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 12.5    '14.5
                If varDoorData.ShaftTube = True Then
                    If CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) <= 2 Then
                        MinShaftLen = MinShaftLen - 2.5
                    End If
                End If
                

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10              'solid shaft
            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11        'solid shaft

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 12.5    '14.5
                If CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) > 0.5 And CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) <= 2 Then
                    MinShaftLen = MinShaftLen - (CInt(DrWidth Mod 12) - 0.5)
                    lblnModifiedL1 = True
                End If
            End Select
        End If
    Else
        If HWType = 2 Then
            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
            Case 1, 7, 8
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 9
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10
            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 9
            End Select
            

        ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
            Case 1, 7, 8
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 10.5
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10
            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                MinShaftLen = DrWidth + 10.5
            End Select
        End If
    End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'    Select Case HWType
'    Case 2
'        AvailShaftSpace = DrWidth + 3 - (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) - (IIf(CouplNum > 0, 1, 0) * couplerValue)
'    Case 3
'        AvailShaftSpace = DrWidth + 4 - (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) - (IIf(CouplNum > 0, 1, 0) * couplerValue)
'    End Select

    AvailShaftSpace = GetAvailableShaftSpace(HWType, DrWidth, varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, CouplNum, couplerValue)

''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    
    Dim lsngsqfact As Single
        'check to see coupler is selected; couplNum=1
        If CouplNum > 0 Then
            If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).blnMismatched = False Then   'match sol (Gird1)
                'calculates #springs needed for the solution
                NoSprg = UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring()) + 1
                
                'calculates wound spring length just like in mismatch springs selection for Round & Square WireType
                If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).strSpringWireType = "Square" Then
                    lsngsqfact = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize, _
                                                 varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
                
                    WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length + Winds) * _
                                    (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize * lsngsqfact) + 3
                Else
                    WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length) + (Winds * varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize) + 3
                End If
                
                ' possible problem
                If (WoundSprgLen * NoSprg) + _
                    (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
                    > (AvailShaftSpace - (2 * NoSprg)) Then
                        Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
                        MsgBox "It is recommended that you use auxiliary shaft.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Auxiliary Shaft."
                        
                        'no need to have an extra msg - Bill approved
'                        If (WoundSprgLen * NoSprg) + _
'                            (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
'                            > (AvailShaftSpace - (1 * NoSprg)) Then
'                            Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
'                                MsgBox "This solution will fit on the shaft but requires tight assembly.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Tight assembly. "
'                        End If
                End If
                
                'matched - odd # and even # of springs
                Dim arrSpringBlock() As Single
                ReDim arrSpringBlock(NoSprg)
                Dim RemainSpace As Single
                
                'find total QTY for LHM and RHM springs
                QTY_LHM = GetSprQtyPerSide(SpringIndex, False)
                QTY_RHM = GetSprQtyPerSide(SpringIndex, True)
                
                'simple solution has same wound spring length for all springs
                RemainSpace = AvailShaftSpace - (WoundSprgLen * NoSprg)
                
                'Calculate the Spring block
                Dim i As Integer
                For i = 0 To NoSprg - 1
                    arrSpringBlock(i) = (RemainSpace / NoSprg) + WoundSprgLen
                Next i
                
                If QTY_LHM >= QTY_RHM Then
                    L1 = arrSpringBlock(0) * Int(NoSprg / 2) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                Else
                    L1 = arrSpringBlock(0) * Fix(NoSprg / 2) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                End If
                                        
                If lblnModifiedL1 = True Then
                    L1 = L1 - ((DrWidth + 12.5) - MinShaftLen) / 2
                End If
                ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                'condition to re-calculate the L1
                If NoSprg = 1 And CouplNum = 1 Then
                    If L1 < 20 Then L1 = 20
                End If
                ''''''''''END Change in .NET

                L2 = MinShaftLen - L1
                
                'for operator - Chainhoist, Dyna-Hoist or jackshaft add 2"
                Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType

                Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
                    If varDoorData.OperatorLeft = True Then
                        L1 = L1 + 2
                    Else
                        L2 = L2 + 2
                    End If
                End Select
            
            
                If varDoorData.ShaftTube = False Then
                    '1", 1-1/4" w/coupler
                    TotalShaftReq = L1 + L2 'for SL, HL, VL, LHR-VL, LHRFM
                    
                    If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                        L1 = L1 + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) 'need to add drum width to individual length w/coupler
                        L2 = L2 + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping)
                        TotalShaftReq = L1 + L2 're-calculate the total shaft length for LHRRM
                        
                        lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(TotalShaftReq - Fix(TotalShaftReq)) 'round to the nearest 1/2"
                        TotalShaftReq = Fix(TotalShaftReq) + lsngFactor 'final shaft length for LHRRM
                    End If
                              
                              
                    If varDoorData.AuxillaryShaft = True Then   'add 3" to each of the shaft with coupler in match spring sol
                        L1 = L1 + 3
                        L2 = L2 + 3
                        TotalShaftReq = L1 + L2
                    End If
                    
                    If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                        Call OneInchShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)   '# of springs > 1
                    ElseIf varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then
                        Call OneInchQuaterShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)
                    End If
                End If
                    
            Else    'mismatch sol
                
                Dim small_WoundSprgLen As Single
                Dim big_WoundSprgLen As Single
                Dim NoMisMatchSprg As Integer
                Dim QTYSmall_LHM As Integer
                Dim QTYSmall_RHM As Integer
                Dim QTYBig_LHM As Integer
                Dim QTYBig_RHM As Integer
                Dim QTYSmallSprg As Integer
                Dim QTYBigSprg As Integer
                Dim SumSB_LHM As Double
                Dim SumSB_RHM As Double
                Dim QTYSBB_RHM As Integer
                Dim QTYSBB_LHM As Integer
                Dim SumSBB_RHM As Double
                Dim SumSBB_LHM As Double
                Dim SBB As Double
                Dim SBS As Double
                                   
                'no. of LHM & RHM small mismatch springs
                For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                    If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize Then
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            QTYSmall_RHM = QTYSmall_RHM + 1
                        Else
                            QTYSmall_LHM = QTYSmall_LHM + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                Next
                                
                'no of LHM & RHM Big mismatch springs
                For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                    If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize Then
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            QTYBig_RHM = QTYBig_RHM + 1
                        Else
                            QTYBig_LHM = QTYBig_LHM + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                Next
                'total # QTY_LHM
                QTY_LHM = QTYSmall_LHM + QTYBig_LHM
                
                'total # QTY_RHM
                QTY_RHM = QTYSmall_RHM + QTYBig_RHM
                
                'total # of small mismatch springs
                QTYSmallSprg = QTYSmall_LHM + QTYSmall_RHM
                
                'total # of Big mismatch springs
                QTYBigSprg = QTYBig_LHM + QTYBig_RHM
                
                'total # of mismatch spring
                NoMisMatchSprg = QTYSmallSprg + QTYBigSprg
                        
                'calculates wound spring length for Round & Square WireType
                If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).strSpringWireType = "Square" Then
                    
                    Dim lsngsqfact_smallSprg As Single
                    Dim lsngsqfact_bigsprg As Single
                    
                    'retreives wound spring length and convert it into inches
                    lsngsqfact_smallSprg = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize, _
                                 varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
                    
                    small_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length + Winds) * _
                                         varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize * lsngsqfact_smallSprg + 3
                                         
                    lsngsqfact_bigsprg = GetSquareFactor(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize, _
                                 varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).sngSpringCoilID)
                                 
                    big_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).Length + Winds) * _
                                         varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize * lsngsqfact_bigsprg + 3
                Else
                
                    'retreives wound spring length
                    small_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).Length) + (Winds * varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize) + 3
                    
                    'retreives wound spring length
                    big_WoundSprgLen = (varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).Length) + (Winds * varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize) + 3
                End If
                
                'possible problem
                If (small_WoundSprgLen * NoSmallSprg) + (big_WoundSprgLen * NoBigSprg) + _
                    (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
                    > (AvailShaftSpace - (2 * NoMisMatchSprg)) Then
                        Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
                        MsgBox "It is recommended that you use auxiliary shaft.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Auxiliary Shaft."
                        
                        'same as above
'                        If (small_WoundSprgLen * NoSmallSprg) + (big_WoundSprgLen * NoBigSprg) + _
'                            (2 * (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping))) + 8 _
'                            > (AvailShaftSpace - (1 * NoMisMatchSprg)) Then
'                                Screen.MousePointer = vbDefault
'                                MsgBox "This solution will fit on the shaft but requires tight assembly.", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Tight assembly."
'                        End If
                End If
                
                'calculates remaining space for mismatch springs
                RemainSpace = AvailShaftSpace - ((small_WoundSprgLen * QTYSmallSprg) + (big_WoundSprgLen * QTYBigSprg))
                                              
                'spring block of big springs - does not matter LHM or RHM for calculation only
                SBB = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + big_WoundSprgLen

                'spring block of small springs - does not matter LHM or RHM for calculation only
                SBS = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + small_WoundSprgLen
                               
                If QTY_LHM > 0 Then
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(QTY_LHM - 1)
                Else
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(0) 'UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_LHM()) + 1
                End If

                If QTY_RHM > 0 Then
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(QTY_RHM - 1)
                Else
                    ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(0) 'UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_RHM()) + 1
                End If
                
'                ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(QTY_LHM - 1)  'commented September 12/05
'                ReDim SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(QTY_RHM - 1)
                
                Dim lindex As Integer
                Dim lCount As Integer
                lindex = 0
                lCount = 0
                
                'LHM(B&S) and RHM(B&S) - Spring Block
                For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                    If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize Then
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).RHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + small_WoundSprgLen
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize
                            lindex = lindex + 1
                        End If
                    Else
                        If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = True Then
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).RHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + big_WoundSprgLen
                            SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(lindex).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize
                            lindex = lindex + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                 Next
                
                 For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())
                     If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).WireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize Then
                         If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = False Then
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).LHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + small_WoundSprgLen
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(0).WireSize
                             lCount = lCount + 1
                         End If
                     Else
                         If varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(i).Left = False Then
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).LHM = (RemainSpace / NoMisMatchSprg) + big_WoundSprgLen
                             SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(lCount).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize
                             lCount = lCount + 1
                         End If
                    End If
                 Next
                
                'Sum of spring block of LHM springs - include small and big
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_LHM())
                    SumSB_LHM = SumSB_LHM + SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(i).LHM
                Next i
                
                'Sum of spring block of RHM springs - include small and big
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_RHM())
                    SumSB_RHM = SumSB_RHM + SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(i).RHM
                Next i
                                
                'find the QTY for SBB_RHM also find the sum of SBB_RHM
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_RHM())
                   If SpringMountSide.SB_RHM(i).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize Then
                        QTYSBB_RHM = QTYSBB_RHM + 1
                        SumSBB_RHM = SumSBB_RHM + SBB
                   End If
                Next
                        
                'find the QTY for SBB_LHM also find the sum of SBB_LHM
                For i = 0 To UBound(SpringMountSide.SB_LHM())
                   If SpringMountSide.SB_LHM(i).Sprg_wireSize = varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring(UBound(varDoorData.arrSelectedSolution(SpringIndex).arrSpring())).WireSize Then
                        QTYSBB_LHM = QTYSBB_LHM + 1
                        SumSBB_LHM = SumSBB_LHM + SBB
                   End If
                Next
                        
                'Check to see mismatch balance or Not Balance
                If Abs(QTY_LHM - QTY_RHM) <= 1 Then     'balance mismatch spring - difference of 1 spring b/w LHM and RHM
                    L1 = SumSB_LHM + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                Else                                    'NOT balance mismatch spring - difference of > 1 spring b/w LHM and RHM
                    If QTY_LHM < QTY_RHM Then
                       If QTY_RHM - Int(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) <= QTYSBB_RHM Then
                           L1 = SumSB_LHM + SBB * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_LHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                       Else
                           L1 = SumSB_LHM + SumSBB_RHM + SBS * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_LHM - QTYSBB_RHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                        End If
                    Else
                        If QTY_LHM - Int(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) <= QTYSBB_LHM Then
                            L1 = SumSB_LHM - SBB * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_RHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                        Else
                            L1 = SumSB_LHM - SumSBB_LHM - SBS * (Fix(NoMisMatchSprg / 2) - QTY_RHM - QTYSBB_LHM) + (couplerValue / 2) + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) + (ExtShaft(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping, HWType) / 2)
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                
                 If lblnModifiedL1 = True Then
                    L1 = L1 - ((DrWidth + 12.5) - MinShaftLen) / 2
                End If
                    
                L2 = MinShaftLen - L1
                
                'for operator - Chainhoist, Dyna-Hoist or jackshaft add 2"
                Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10
                    Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                        If varDoorData.OperatorLeft = True Then
                            L1 = L1 + 2
                        Else
                            L2 = L2 + 2
                        End If
                End Select

                
                If varDoorData.AuxillaryShaft = True Then   'add 3" to each length with coupler
                    L1 = L1 + 3
                    L2 = L2 + 3
                End If
                
                
                If varDoorData.ShaftTube = False Then
                    TotalShaftReq = L1 + L2
                    If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                        Call OneInchShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)
                    ElseIf varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then
                        Call OneInchQuaterShaft_PN(TotalShaftReq, L1, L2)
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            
        Else
            'couplNum=0
            Dim TubeShaftLen As Double
            
            If varDoorData.ShaftTube = True Then
            
                If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                    MinShaftLen = (DrWidth + 10) + (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) * 2)
                    lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(MinShaftLen - Fix(MinShaftLen))
                    MinShaftLen = Fix(MinShaftLen) + lsngFactor
                End If
                
                Call OneInchTubeShaft_PN(MinShaftLen, 0, 0)
            Else
                If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                    ShaftLen = MinShaftLen
                    
                    Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
                        Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
                            ShaftLen = ShaftLen + 2
                    End Select
                    
                    If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                        ShaftLen = ShaftLen + (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) * 2)
                        lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(ShaftLen - Fix(ShaftLen))
                        ShaftLen = Fix(ShaftLen) + lsngFactor
                        
                    End If
                    
                    If varDoorData.AuxillaryShaft = True Then ShaftLen = ShaftLen + 6   'add 6" inches to the shaft when auxillary shaft is selected with no coupler
                    Call OneInchShaft_PN(ShaftLen, 0, 0)
                
                ElseIf varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then
                    ShaftLen = MinShaftLen
                    
                    Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
                        Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
                            ShaftLen = ShaftLen + 2
                    End Select
                    
                    If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                        ShaftLen = ShaftLen + (GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping) * 2)
                        lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(ShaftLen - Fix(ShaftLen))
                        ShaftLen = Fix(ShaftLen) + lsngFactor
                    End If
                    
                    If varDoorData.AuxillaryShaft = True Then ShaftLen = ShaftLen + 6   'add 6" inches to the shaft when auxillary shaft is selected with no coupler
                    Call OneInchQuaterShaft_PN(ShaftLen, 0, 0)
                End If
            End If
        End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub
Public Sub OneInchQuaterShaft_PN(ByVal MinShaftLen As Single, ByVal LenShaft1 As Single, ByVal LenShaft2 As Single)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".OneInchQuaterShaft_PN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim cutShaftLoc1 As String
    Dim cutShaftLoc2 As String
    Dim totalLen As Single
    Dim ShaftLen As Single
    Dim cutoffshaft As Single
    Dim SideDrive As String
    Dim lsngFactor As Single
    
    ReDim varDoorData.varShaft(2)
    
    Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
    Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
        If varDoorData.OperatorLeft = True Then
            SideDrive = "LH drive side"
        Else
            SideDrive = "RH drive side"
        End If
    End Select
    
    If CouplNum > 0 And (MinShaftLen > 0 And LenShaft1 > 0 And LenShaft2 > 0) Then  'odd no. of springs + mismatch springs

        Call ClearConObj
        If MeasU = 0 Then
            aCon.Inch = LenShaft1
            aCon.mmcalc
            cutoffshaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
            cutShaftLoc1 = cutoffshaft & " mm"
            
            Call ClearConObj
            cutoffshaft = 0
            
            aCon.Inch = LenShaft2
            aCon.mmcalc
            cutoffshaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
            cutShaftLoc2 = cutoffshaft & " mm"
            
        Else
            cutShaftLoc1 = GetImpString(LenShaft1)
            cutShaftLoc2 = GetImpString(LenShaft2)
        End If
        
'        If MinShaftLen <= 207 Then
            totalLen = LenShaft1 + LenShaft2
            
            'For LHR-Rear Mount : already added drum width factor into L1 & L2 - need to re-calculate the total shaft length in order to round to the nearest 1/2"
            If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(totalLen - Fix(totalLen)) 'round to the nearest 1/2"
                totalLen = Fix(totalLen) + lsngFactor
            End If
            
            If MeasU = 0 Then
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = totalLen
                aCon.mmcalc
            End If
            If totalLen <= 168 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0272"
                
            ElseIf totalLen > 168 And totalLen <= 288 Then
                               
                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0271"
            
            ElseIf totalLen > 288 Then
            
                're-calculate each shaft over 288" wider doors
            
                If LenShaft1 <= 168 Then
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0272"
                Else
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0271"
                End If
                
                If LenShaft2 <= 168 Then
                    varDoorData.varShaft(1).PN = "426-0272"
                Else
                    varDoorData.varShaft(1).PN = "426-0271"
                End If
                
                
                'save shaft part number
                If varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = varDoorData.varShaft(1).PN Then
                    If LenShaft1 <= 168 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        
                    End If
                    
                Else
                    If LenShaft1 <= 168 Then
                        'Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft "))
                    Else
                        'Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    End If
                    
                    If LenShaft2 <= 168 Then
                        'Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft "))
                    Else
                        'Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            
            'save shaft len
            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = LenShaft1
            varDoorData.varShaft(1).ShaftLen(1) = LenShaft2
            
'        Else
'            totalLen = LenShaft1 + LenShaft2
'            If MeasU = 0 Then
'                ClearConObj
'                aCon.Inch = totalLen
'                aCon.mmcalc
'            End If
'            If totalLen <= 168 Then
'                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
'                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0272"
'            Else
'                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
'                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0271"
'            End If
'            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = LenShaft1
'            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(1) = LenShaft2
            
'        End If
        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-103", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-103"), 0)
        If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("436-0097", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("436-0097"), 0)
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("436-0047", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("436-0047"), 0)
        End If
    
    ElseIf CouplNum > 0 And (MinShaftLen > 0) And (LenShaft1 = 0 And LenShaft2 = 0) Then    'even no. of springs
        Dim L1 As Single
        Dim L2 As Single
        
        ShaftLen = MinShaftLen / 2    'for even No. sprg
        
        L1 = ShaftLen
        L2 = MinShaftLen - L1
        
'        If MinShaftLen > 207 Then
            Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10
            Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11

''''''''''END Change in .NET
                If varDoorData.OperatorLeft = True Then
                    L1 = L1 + 2
                Else
                    L2 = L2 + 2
                End If
            End Select
            
            'need to re-calculate the L1 & L2 adding drum width factor into the equation
            If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                L1 = L1 + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping)
                L2 = L2 + GetDrumWidth(varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping)
            End If
            
            totalLen = L1 + L2
            
            'For LHR-Rear Mount : already added drum width factor into the L1 & L2 - need to re-calculate the total shaft length in order to round to the nearest 1/2"
            If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
                lsngFactor = RoundFractionToHalfInch(totalLen - Fix(totalLen)) 'round to the nearest 1/2"
                totalLen = Fix(totalLen) + lsngFactor
            End If
            
            Call ClearConObj
            If MeasU = 0 Then
                aCon.Inch = L1
                aCon.mmcalc
                cutoffshaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                cutShaftLoc1 = cutoffshaft & " mm"
                
                cutoffshaft = 0
                
                aCon.Inch = L2
                aCon.mmcalc
                cutoffshaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                cutShaftLoc2 = cutoffshaft & " mm"
            Else
                cutShaftLoc1 = GetImpString(L1)
                cutShaftLoc2 = GetImpString(L2)
            End If
                    
            If MeasU = 0 Then
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = totalLen
                aCon.mmcalc
            End If
            If totalLen <= 168 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0272"
            ElseIf totalLen > 168 And totalLen <= 288 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0271"
            ElseIf totalLen > 288 Then
                're-calculate each shaft over 288" wider doors
            
                If LenShaft1 <= 168 Then
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0272"
                Else
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0271"
                End If
                
                If LenShaft2 <= 168 Then
                    varDoorData.varShaft(1).PN = "426-0272"
                Else
                    varDoorData.varShaft(1).PN = "426-0271"
                End If
                
                'save the shaft part number
            
                If varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = varDoorData.varShaft(1).PN Then
                    If LenShaft1 <= 168 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    End If
                    
                Else
                    If LenShaft1 <= 168 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    End If
                    
                    If LenShaft2 <= 168 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            
            'save the shaft len
            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = L1
            varDoorData.varShaft(1).ShaftLen(1) = L2
            
            
            
'        Else
'            shaft1 = MinShaftLen / 2
'            Call ClearConObj
'            If MeasU = 0 Then
'                aCon.Inch = shaft1
'                aCon.mmcalc
'                cutoffshaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
'                cutShaftLoc1 = cutoffshaft & " mm"
'            Else
'                cutShaftLoc1 = GetImpString(shaft1)
'            End If
'            shaft2 = shaft1
'            totalLen = shaft1 + shaft2
'            If MeasU = 0 Then
'                ClearConObj
'                aCon.Inch = totalLen
'                aCon.mmcalc
'            End If
'            If totalLen <= 168 Then
'                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Cut to (2) " & cutShaftLoc1)
'                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0272"
'            Else
'                Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Cut to (2) " & cutShaftLoc1)
'                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0271"
'            End If
'            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = shaft1
'            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(1) = shaft2
'        End If
        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-103", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-103"), 0)
        If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("436-0097", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("436-0097"), 0)
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("436-0047", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("436-0047"), 0)
        End If
        
    Else    'couplnum = 0
        
        totalLen = MinShaftLen
        Call ClearConObj
        If MeasU = 0 Then
            aCon.Inch = MinShaftLen
            aCon.mmcalc
            cutoffshaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
            cutShaftLoc1 = cutoffshaft & "mm"
        Else
            cutShaftLoc1 = GetImpString(MinShaftLen)
        End If
        If MeasU = 0 Then
            ClearConObj
            aCon.Inch = totalLen
            aCon.mmcalc
        End If
        If MinShaftLen <= 168 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0272", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0272"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1)
            varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0272"
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0271", IIf(MeasU = 0, aCon.mm, totalLen), "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-0271"), totalLen, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1)
            varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "426-0271"
        End If
        varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = totalLen
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.AuxillaryShaft = True Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid("7-2350", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("7-2350"), 0)
        Call FillSummaryGrid("423-337", 4, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("423-337"), 0)

    End If
    
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub OneInchShaft_PN(ByVal MinShaftLen As Single, ByVal LenShaft1 As Single, ByVal LenShaft2 As Single)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".OneInchShaft_PN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim cutShaftLength As Single
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim cutShaftLoc1 As String
    Dim cutShaftLoc2 As String
    Dim cutoffshaft As Single
    Dim cutshaftLoc As String
    ReDim varDoorData.varShaft(2)
    Dim SideDrive As String
    Dim lblnRoll As Boolean
    Dim PN_KeyedShaftNotes As String
    
    PN_KeyedShaftNotes = "1/4" & Chr(34) & " X 3/8" & Chr(34) & " Key for drive sprocket"
    
    Select Case varDoorData.OperatorType
    Case 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 11
        If varDoorData.OperatorLeft = True Then
            SideDrive = "LH drive side"
        Else
            SideDrive = "RH drive side"
        End If
    End Select
    
    If CouplNum > 0 And (MinShaftLen > 0 And LenShaft1 > 0 And LenShaft2 > 0) Then
        
        If MinShaftLen <= 207 Then
            Call ClearConObj
            If MeasU = 0 Then
                aCon.Inch = LenShaft1
                aCon.mmcalc
                cutShaftLoc1 = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"    'LH
                
                Call ClearConObj
                 
                aCon.Inch = LenShaft2
                aCon.mmcalc
                cutShaftLoc2 = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"     'RH
                
            Else
                cutShaftLoc1 = GetImpString(LenShaft1)  'LH
                cutShaftLoc2 = GetImpString(LenShaft2)  'RH
            End If
            
            Select Case MinShaftLen
            Case Is <= 111
                If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("950-259", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-259"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-259"
                 Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("114-700", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-700"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-700"
                 End If
                 
            Case Is <= 135
                If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("950-261", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-261"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-261"
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("114-701", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-701"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-701"
                End If
                
            Case Is <= 159
                If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("950-262", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-262"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-262"
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("114-702", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-702"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-702"
                End If
                
            Case Is <= 183
                If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("950-263", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-263"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-263"
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("114-703", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-703"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-703"
                End If
                
            Case Else
                If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("950-264", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-264"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-264"
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("114-704", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-704"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutShaftLoc2 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                    varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-704"
                End If
                
            End Select
            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = LenShaft1
            varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(1) = LenShaft2
        Else
            For i = 0 To 1
                If i = 0 Then
                    cutShaftLength = LenShaft1
                Else
                    cutShaftLength = LenShaft2
                End If
                
                'convert Cut length of shafts to proper display format
                Call ClearConObj
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = cutShaftLength
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutshaftLoc = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutshaftLoc = GetImpString(cutShaftLength)
                End If
                
                If i = 0 Then cutShaftLoc1 = cutshaftLoc            'save cut instructions of L1
                Select Case cutShaftLength
                Case Is <= 111
                    
                    If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "114-700") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-700", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-700"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-700", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-700"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "114-700"
                    Else
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "950-259") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-259", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-259"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-259", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-259"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")) & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "950-259"
                    End If
                    
                Case Is <= 135
                    
                    If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "114-701") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-701", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-701"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-701", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-701"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "114-701"
                    Else
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "950-261") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-261", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-261"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-261", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-261"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")) & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "950-261"
                    End If
                    
                Case Is <= 159
                    If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "114-702") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-702", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-702"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-702", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-702"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "114-702"
                    Else
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "950-262") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-262", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-262"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-262", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-262"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")) & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "950-262"
                    End If
                    
                Case Is <= 183
                    If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "114-703") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-703", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-703"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-703", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-703"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "114-703"
                    Else
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "950-263") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-263", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-263"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-263", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-263"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")) & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "950-263"
                    End If
                    
                Case Else
                    If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "114-704") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-704", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-704"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft"))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("114-704", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-704"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "114-704"
                    Else
                        lblnRoll = RollBack("Torsion", True, "950-264") 'check duplicate part number (if exist)
                        If lblnRoll = True Then                         'true means - eliminate pn, concatinate L1 cut instructions, add the qty
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-264", 2, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-264"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaftLoc1 & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side ", "for LH shaft ") & "and " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft") & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        Else
                            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-264", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-264"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & Chr$(32) & IIf(i = 0, IIf(SideDrive = "LH drive side", "for drive side", "for LH shaft"), IIf(SideDrive = "RH drive side", "for drive side", "for RH shaft")) & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                        End If
                        varDoorData.varShaft(i).PN = "950-264"
                    End If
                    
                End Select
                varDoorData.varShaft(i).ShaftLen(0) = LenShaft1
                varDoorData.varShaft(i).ShaftLen(1) = LenShaft2
            Next i
        End If
        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-102", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("426-102"), 0)
        If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("436-0097", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("436-0097"), 0)
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("436-0047", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("436-0047"), 0)
        End If
        
    Else 'couplnum = 0 or no Cut req.
        
        Call ClearConObj
        If MeasU = 0 Then
            aCon.Inch = MinShaftLen
            aCon.mmcalc
            cutshaftLoc = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
        Else
            cutshaftLoc = GetImpString(MinShaftLen)
        End If
        
        Select Case MinShaftLen
        Case Is <= 111
            If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("114-700", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-700"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-700"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-259", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-259"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-259"
            End If
        Case Is <= 135
            If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("114-701", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-701"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-701"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-261", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-261"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-261"
            End If
        Case Is <= 159
            If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("114-702", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-702"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-702"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-262", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-262"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-262"
            End If
        Case Is <= 183
            If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("114-703", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-703"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-703"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-263", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-263"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-263"
            End If
        Case Else
            If varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = False Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("114-704", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("114-704"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "114-704"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-264", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-264"), 0, "Requires " & cutshaftLoc & IIf(varDoorData.OperatorType = 10, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-264"
            End If
        End Select
        varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = MinShaftLen

    End If
    
    If varDoorData.AuxillaryShaft = True Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid("7-2350", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("7-2350"), 0)
        Call FillSummaryGrid("423-336", 4, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("423-336"), 0)
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub OneInchTubeShaft_PN(ByVal totalshaftlen As Single, ByVal LenShaft1 As Single, ByVal LenShaft2 As Single)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".OneInchTubeShaft_PN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim cutShaft As String
    ReDim varDoorData.varShaft(1)
       
    Call ClearConObj
    If MeasU = 0 Then
        If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
            Call ClearConObj
            aCon.Inch = totalshaftlen
            aCon.mmcalc
            cutShaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
        Else
            Call ClearConObj
            aCon.Inch = DrWidth + 10
            aCon.mmcalc
            cutShaft = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
        End If
    Else
        If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
            cutShaft = GetImpString(totalshaftlen)
        Else
            cutShaft = GetImpString(DrWidth + 10)
        End If
    End If

    If CouplNum = 0 Then
        Select Case totalshaftlen
        Case Is <= 108
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-239", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-239"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-239"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-009", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-009"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-009"
            End If
            
        Case Is <= 120
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-240", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-240"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-240"

                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-010"
            End If
            
        Case Is <= 132
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-241", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-241"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-241"

            End If
            
        Case Is <= 156
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-242", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-242"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-242"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-012", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-012"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-012"
            End If
            
        Case Is <= 180
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-243", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-243"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-243"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-013", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-013"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-013"
            End If
                
        Case Is <= 204
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-244", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-244"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-244"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-014", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-014"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-014"
            End If
            
        Case Is <= 228
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-245", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-245"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-245"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-015", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-015"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-015"
            End If
            
        Case Is <= 264
            If varDoorData.GlavanizedTubeShaft = True Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-246", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-246"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-246"
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-016", 1, "Torsion", GetSubCategory("950-016"), 0, "Requires " & cutShaft)
                varDoorData.varShaft(0).PN = "950-016"
            End If
            
        End Select
    End If

''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    varDoorData.varShaft(0).ShaftLen(0) = totalshaftlen
    
    
    

    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub
