Private Sub selectUWATrackCips()

On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectUWATrackCips()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'subroutine is being used for ADCA
    Dim UWA_PN(7) As String
    Dim UWA_Qty(7) As Integer
    Dim lintUWA As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
       
    'FOR HIGH LIFT USE UWA = HL - 9
    '    VERTICAL LIFT USE UWA = DH +21
    '    VERTICAL LIFT (LHR) USE UWA = DH + 9
    Select Case LiftType
        Case "High Lift"
            lintUWA = HILIFT - 9
'        Case "Vertical Lift"
'            lintUWA = DrHgt + 21
'        Case "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
'            lintUWA = DrHgt + 9
        End Select
        
               
    If lintUWA >= 45 And lintUWA < 75 Then
        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
                
        CB = 2
        KN = 2
      
    ElseIf lintUWA >= 75 And lintUWA < 105 Then
        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
        UWA_Qty(1) = 2
               
        CB = 4
        KN = 4
      
    ElseIf lintUWA >= 105 And lintUWA < 135 Then
        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
        UWA_Qty(1) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(2) = "426-1033"
        UWA_Qty(2) = 2
        
        CB = 6
        KN = 6
      
    ElseIf lintUWA >= 135 And lintUWA < 165 Then
        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
        UWA_Qty(1) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(2) = "426-1033"
        UWA_Qty(2) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(3) = "426-1034"
        UWA_Qty(3) = 2
        
        CB = 8
        KN = 8
          
    ElseIf lintUWA >= 165 And lintUWA < 195 Then
        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
        UWA_Qty(1) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(2) = "426-1033"
        UWA_Qty(2) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(3) = "426-1034"
        UWA_Qty(3) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(4) = "426-1035"
        UWA_Qty(4) = 2
        
        CB = 10
        KN = 10
    
    ElseIf lintUWA >= 195 And lintUWA < 225 Then
        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
        UWA_Qty(1) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(2) = "426-1033"
        UWA_Qty(2) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(3) = "426-1034"
        UWA_Qty(3) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(4) = "426-1035"
        UWA_Qty(4) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(5) = "426-1036"
        UWA_Qty(5) = 2
        
        CB = 12
        KN = 12
    
    ElseIf lintUWA >= 225 And lintUWA < 255 Then
        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
        UWA_Qty(1) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(2) = "426-1033"
        UWA_Qty(2) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(3) = "426-1034"
        UWA_Qty(3) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(4) = "426-1035"
        UWA_Qty(4) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(5) = "426-1036"
        UWA_Qty(5) = 2
        
        UWA_PN(6) = "426-1037"
        UWA_Qty(6) = 2
        
        CB = 14
        KN = 14
    End If
            
    If UWA_PN(0) <> "" Then
        
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
        
        Dim i As Integer
        For i = 0 To 6
            If UWA_PN(i) <> "" And UWA_Qty(i) <> Empty Then
                FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN(i), UWA_Qty(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN(i)), 0
            Else
                Exit Sub
            End If
        Next
    Else
        If lintUWA >= 45 Then   'since HL falls under the required mim value(45"), no message box displayed
            MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Wall Track Clips. " & vbCrLf & _
            "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Wall Track Clips."
        End If
        
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Private Sub selectFullHorzAngles()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectFullHorzAngles()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim HA_PN As String
    Dim HA_Len As Integer
    Dim LenHorzAngle As Single
    Dim DHFeet As Double
    Dim HLFeet As Double
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim noSection As Single
    Dim DoorHgt As Integer
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim lbyteTag As Byte
    
    'noSection = (N21 + N24) * 24 - 6
    DoorHgt = CInt(DrHgt)
    lbyteTag = 1
    
    'Dh is rounded up to the nearest 3"
    Do While i = 0
        If DoorHgt Mod 3 = 0 Then
            i = 1
        Else
            DoorHgt = DoorHgt + 1
        End If
    Loop
    
    If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
        If varDoorData.Track.FullHorzAngle = 1 Then 'full length H/A
            'LenHorzAngle = McValue + noSection
           If LiftType = "Standard Lift" Then
               LenHorzAngle = McValue + DoorHgt - 9
           ElseIf LiftType = "High Lift" Then
               LenHorzAngle = McValue + (DoorHgt - HILIFT - 9)
            End If
            Call ClearConObj
            
            'calculate the no. of CB in North American style
            If LiftType = "Standard Lift" Then
                aCon.Inch = DoorHgt
                aCon.Feetcalc
                DHFeet = aCon.Feet
                
                CB = RoundUp(DHFeet / 2 + 1) * 2
                
            ElseIf LiftType = "High Lift" Then
            
                aCon.Inch = DoorHgt
                aCon.Feetcalc
                DHFeet = aCon.Feet
                
                Call ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = HILIFT
                aCon.Feetcalc
                HLFeet = aCon.Feet
                
                CB = RoundUp((DHFeet - HLFeet) / 2 + 1) * 2
            End If
            
            ' select North American PN
            Select Case LiftType
            Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift"
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    Select Case LenHorzAngle
                        Case Is < 108
                            HA_PN = "425-2021"
                            HA_Len = 108
                            
                        Case 108 To 132
                            HA_PN = "425-2022"
                            HA_Len = 132
                            
                        Case 132 To 156
                            HA_PN = "425-2023"
                            HA_Len = 156
                            
                        Case 156 To 180
                            HA_PN = "425-2024"
                            HA_Len = 180
                            
                        Case 180 To 204
                            HA_PN = "425-2025"
                            HA_Len = 204
                            
                        Case 204 To 228
                            HA_PN = "425-2026"
                            HA_Len = 228
                        Case Else
                           MsgBox "Cannot select Full Horizontal Angle. " & vbCrLf & _
                            "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Full Horizontal Angle. "
                            Exit Sub
                    End Select
                ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
                    Select Case LenHorzAngle
                        Case Is < 108
                            HA_PN = "425-2031"
                            HA_Len = 108
                            
                        Case 108 To 132
                            HA_PN = "425-2032"
                            HA_Len = 132
                            
                        Case 132 To 156
                            HA_PN = "425-2033"
                            HA_Len = 156
                            
                        Case 156 To 180
                            HA_PN = "425-2034"
                            HA_Len = 180
                            
                        Case 180 To 204
                            HA_PN = "425-2035"
                            HA_Len = 204
                            
                        Case 204 To 228
                            HA_PN = "425-2036"
                            HA_Len = 228
                            
                        Case 228 To 252
                            HA_PN = "425-2037"
                            HA_Len = 252
                            
                        Case 252 To 276
                            HA_PN = "425-2038"
                            HA_Len = 276
                            
                        Case 276 To 300
                            HA_PN = "425-2039"
                            HA_Len = 300
                        Case Else
                            MsgBox "Cannot select Full Horizontal Angle. " & vbCrLf & _
                            "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Full Horizontal Angle. "
                            Exit Sub
                    End Select
                End If
            End Select
        Else
            Select Case LiftType
            Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift"
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    HA_PN = "425-2010"
                    HA_Len = 72
                    CB = 8
                    
                ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
                    HA_PN = "425-2011"
                    HA_Len = 90
                    
                    CB = 10
                End If
                lbyteTag = 0    'No cut req.
            End Select
        End If
    Else    'European adca
        Select Case LiftType
            Case "Standard Lift"
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    If HRadius = 12 Then
                        LenHorzAngle = DoorHgt + 14
                    ElseIf HRadius = 16 Then
                        LenHorzAngle = DoorHgt + 17
                    End If
                    
                    If DoorHgt > 72 And DoorHgt <= 84 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2049"
                        HA_Len = 100
                        
                        CB = 3
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 85 And DoorHgt <= 96 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2050"
                        HA_Len = 112
                        
                        CB = 4
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 97 And DoorHgt <= 120 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2051"
                        HA_Len = 136
                        
                        CB = 5
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 121 And DoorHgt <= 144 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2052"
                        HA_Len = 160
                        
                        CB = 6
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 145 And DoorHgt <= 168 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2053"
                        HA_Len = 184
                        
                        CB = 7
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 169 And DoorHgt <= 192 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2054"
                        HA_Len = 208
                        
                        CB = 8
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 193 And DoorHgt <= 216 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2055"
                        HA_Len = 232
                        
                        CB = 9
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 217 And DoorHgt <= 240 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2056"
                        HA_Len = 256
                        
                        CB = 10
                    End If
                Else    'HWTYPE = 3 / 3.25
                    LenHorzAngle = DoorHgt + 17
                    
                    If DoorHgt > 72 And DoorHgt <= 84 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2059"
                        HA_Len = 100
                        
                        CB = 3
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 85 And DoorHgt <= 96 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2060"
                        HA_Len = 112
                        
                        CB = 4
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 97 And DoorHgt <= 120 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2061"
                        HA_Len = 136
                        
                        CB = 5
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 121 And DoorHgt <= 144 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2062"
                        HA_Len = 160
                        
                        CB = 6
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 145 And DoorHgt <= 168 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2063"
                        HA_Len = 184
                        
                        CB = 7
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 169 And DoorHgt <= 192 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2064"
                        HA_Len = 208
                        
                        CB = 8
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 193 And DoorHgt <= 216 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2065"
                        HA_Len = 232
                        
                        CB = 9
                        
                    ElseIf DoorHgt > 217 And DoorHgt <= 240 Then
                        HA_PN = "425-2066"
                        HA_Len = 256
                        
                        CB = 10
                    End If
                End If
        
        Case "High Lift"
            
            If varDoorData.Track.FullHorzAngle = 1 Then
                'Horizontal angle
                LenHorzAngle = McValue + (DrHgt - HILIFT - 9)
                
                'calculate no. of CB
                aCon.Inch = DrHgt
                aCon.Feetcalc
                DHFeet = aCon.Feet
                
                Call ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = HILIFT
                aCon.Feetcalc
                HLFeet = aCon.Feet
                
                CB = RoundUp((DHFeet - HLFeet) / 2 + 1)
                
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    Select Case LenHorzAngle
                        Case Is < 108
                            HA_PN = "425-2021"
                            HA_Len = 108
                            
                        Case 108 To 132
                            HA_PN = "425-2022"
                            HA_Len = 132
                            
                        Case 132 To 156
                            HA_PN = "425-2023"
                            HA_Len = 156
                            
                        Case 156 To 180
                            HA_PN = "425-2024"
                            HA_Len = 180
                            
                        Case 180 To 204
                            HA_PN = "425-2025"
                            HA_Len = 204
                            
                        Case 204 To 228
                            HA_PN = "425-2026"
                            HA_Len = 228
                            
                    End Select
                ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
                    Select Case LenHorzAngle
                        Case Is < 108
                            HA_PN = "425-2031"
                            HA_Len = 108
                            
                        Case 108 To 132
                            HA_PN = "425-2032"
                            HA_Len = 132
                            
                        Case 132 To 156
                            HA_PN = "425-2033"
                            HA_Len = 156
                            
                        Case 156 To 180
                            HA_PN = "425-2034"
                            HA_Len = 180
                            
                        Case 180 To 204
                            HA_PN = "425-2035"
                            HA_Len = 204
                            
                        Case 204 To 228
                            HA_PN = "425-2036"
                            HA_Len = 228
                            
                        Case 228 To 252
                            HA_PN = "425-2037"
                            HA_Len = 252
                            
                        Case 252 To 276
                            HA_PN = "425-2038"
                            HA_Len = 276
                        
                        Case 276 To 300
                            HA_PN = "425-2039"
                            HA_Len = 300
                            
                        Case Else
                            MsgBox "Cannot select Full Horizontal Angle. " & vbCrLf & _
                            "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Full Horizontal Angle. "
                            Exit Sub
                    End Select
                End If
            Else
                HA_PN = "425-2010"
                HA_Len = 72
                
                CB = 8
                lbyteTag = 0    'No cut req.
            End If
            
        End Select
    End If
    
    Dim cutOffHA As String
    Dim cutOff As Integer
    
    If lbyteTag > 0 Then
        cutOff = HA_Len - LenHorzAngle
        If cutOff > 0 Then
            ' calculates cut to's amount
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                cutOffHA = "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenHorzAngle)
            Else
                Call ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = LenHorzAngle
                aCon.mmcalc
                cutOffHA = "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
            End If
            FillSummaryGrid HA_PN, 2, "Track", GetSubCategory(HA_PN), 0, cutOffHA
        Else
            FillSummaryGrid HA_PN, 2, "Track", GetSubCategory(HA_PN), 0
        End If
    Else
        FillSummaryGrid HA_PN, 2, "Track", GetSubCategory(HA_PN), 0
    End If
    
    ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB

    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub selectSepCurves(ByVal LenHT As Integer)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectSepCurves()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim VerticalTrackLowerPN As String
    Dim VerticalTrackQTY As Integer
        
    If LenHT < 0 Then Exit Sub  'LenHT = DrHgt

    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift", "High Lift"
        Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                                  
               If LenHT <= 96 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1506"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                     
                ElseIf LenHT > 97 And LenHT <= 108 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1507"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 109 And LenHT <= 120 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1508"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 121 And LenHT <= 132 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1509"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 133 And LenHT <= 144 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1510"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 145 And LenHT <= 156 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1511"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 157 And LenHT <= 168 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1512"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 169 And LenHT <= 180 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1513"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 181 And LenHT <= 192 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1514"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 193 And LenHT <= 204 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1515"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 205 And LenHT <= 216 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1516"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 217 And LenHT <= 228 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1517"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 229 And LenHT <= 240 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1518"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 241 And LenHT <= 252 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1519"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                 ElseIf LenHT > 253 And LenHT <= 264 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1520"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                End If
            
            Case 3
                
                If LenHT <= 96 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1606"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                     
                ElseIf LenHT > 97 And LenHT <= 108 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1607"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 109 And LenHT <= 120 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1608"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 121 And LenHT <= 132 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1609"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 133 And LenHT <= 144 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1610"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 145 And LenHT <= 156 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1611"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 157 And LenHT <= 168 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1612"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 169 And LenHT <= 180 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1613"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 181 And LenHT <= 192 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1614"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 193 And LenHT <= 204 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1615"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 205 And LenHT <= 216 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1616"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 217 And LenHT <= 228 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1617"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 229 And LenHT <= 240 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1618"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 241 And LenHT <= 252 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1619"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 253 And LenHT <= 264 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1620"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 265 And LenHT <= 276 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1621"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 277 And LenHT <= 288 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1622"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                End If
                
            Case 3.25
                
                If LenHT <= 96 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1706"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                     
                ElseIf LenHT > 97 And LenHT <= 108 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1707"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 109 And LenHT <= 120 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1708"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 121 And LenHT <= 132 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1709"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 133 And LenHT <= 144 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1710"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 145 And LenHT <= 156 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1711"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 157 And LenHT <= 168 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1712"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 169 And LenHT <= 180 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1713"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 181 And LenHT <= 192 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1714"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 193 And LenHT <= 204 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1715"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 205 And LenHT <= 216 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1716"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 217 And LenHT <= 228 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1717"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 229 And LenHT <= 240 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1718"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 241 And LenHT <= 252 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1719"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 253 And LenHT <= 264 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1720"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 265 And LenHT <= 276 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1721"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                    
                ElseIf LenHT > 277 And LenHT <= 288 Then
                    VerticalTrackLowerPN = "431-1722"
                    VerticalTrackQTY = 2
                End If
            End Select
      End Select
            
    'for unrestricted values
    If VerticalTrackLowerPN = "" Then
        MsgBox "Cannot select Horizontal Track. " & vbCrLf & _
        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Horizontal Track. "
        Exit Sub
    Else
        'prints horizontal track part no. for sep. curve HT in SL, HL
        If LenHT > 0 Then
            FillSummaryGrid VerticalTrackLowerPN, VerticalTrackQTY, "Track", GetSubCategory(VerticalTrackLowerPN), 0
        End If
    End If
            
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Function cut_UWA(ByVal UWA_Len As Integer, ByVal UWA_Qty As Integer) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".cut_UWA()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim cutLenUWA As Single
    Dim CutTo As Single
    Dim UWALen As String
    
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "High Lift"
        If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
            cutLenUWA = HILIFT - 9
        Else                'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
            cutLenUWA = HILIFT - 18
        End If
'        cutOff = UWA_Len - cutLenUWA   'rem amount no need to worry about
                                        
        If cutLenUWA <> UWA_Len Then                                 ' only show cut to's amount - UWA
            If UWA_Qty = 1 Then
                
                If MeasU = 1 Then
'                    UWALen = "Cut (1) to (2) " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA) & " from both ends"
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA)
                Else
                    Call ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = cutLenUWA
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
'                    UWALen = "Cut (1) to (2) " & CutTo & " mm" & " from both ends"
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) " & CutTo & " mm"
                End If
            ElseIf UWA_Qty = 2 Then
                 
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA)
                Else
                    Call ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = cutLenUWA
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) to " & CutTo & " mm"
                End If
            End If
        End If
    
'    Case "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
'
'        If LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Then
'            If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
'                cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 21
'            Else                    'If varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
'                cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 12
'            End If
'
'        ElseIf LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
'            If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
'                cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 9
'            Else
'                cutLenUWA = DrHgt
'            End If
'        End If
        
        'cutOff = UWA_Len - cutLenUWA   'rem amount no need to worry about
                                        
        If cutLenUWA <> UWA_Len Then                                ' only show cut to's amount - UWA
            If UWA_Qty = 1 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
'                    UWALen = "Cut (1) to (2) " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA) & " from both ends"
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA)
                Else
                    Call ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = cutLenUWA
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
'                    UWALen = "Cut (1) to (2) " & CutTo & " mm" & " from both ends"
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) " & CutTo & " mm"
                End If
            ElseIf UWA_Qty = 2 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA)
                Else
                    Call ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = cutLenUWA
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) to " & CutTo & " mm"
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End Select
    
    cut_UWA = UWALen
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)


End Function

Public Sub selectEUJambWeatherSeal()
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectEUJambWeatherSeal()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim EU_Pn As String
    Dim CutTo As String
    Dim lsngCutOff As Integer
    Dim EU_Len As Integer
    Dim lintcutLen As Integer
    
    If DrHgt <= 84 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-339"
        EU_Len = 86
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 84 And DrHgt <= 96 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-340"
        EU_Len = 98
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 96 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-341"
        EU_Len = 122
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 120 And DrHgt <= 144 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-342"
        EU_Len = 146
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 144 And DrHgt <= 168 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-343"
        EU_Len = 170
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 168 And DrHgt <= 192 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-344"
        EU_Len = 194
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 192 And DrHgt <= 216 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-345"
        EU_Len = 218
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 216 And DrHgt <= 248 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-346"
        EU_Len = 242
        
    ElseIf DrHgt > 248 And DrHgt <= 288 Then
        EU_Pn = "152-347"
        EU_Len = 288
    End If
    
    If EU_Pn <> "" Then
    
        lintcutLen = CInt(DrHgt) + 2
        lsngCutOff = EU_Len - lintcutLen
        
        If lsngCutOff > 0 Then
            'calculates cut to's amount
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                CutTo = "Cut to " & GetImpString(lintcutLen)
            Else
                Call ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = lintcutLen
                aCon.mmcalc
                CutTo = "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
            End If
        End If
        If lsngCutOff > 0 Then
            FillSummaryGrid EU_Pn, 2, "Track", GetSubCategory(EU_Pn), 0, CutTo
        Else
            FillSummaryGrid EU_Pn, 2, "Track", GetSubCategory(EU_Pn), 0
        End If
    Else
'        MsgBox "Cannot select EU Jamb Weather Seal. " & vbCrLf & _
'        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select EU Jamb Weather Seal."
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function IsPNExits(ByVal lstrPN As String) As Boolean

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".IsPNExits()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim i As Integer
    
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = 1
    
    For i = frmGrid.Grid1.Row To frmGrid.Grid1.Rows - 1
        If frmGrid.Grid1.Text = lstrPN Then
            frmGrid.Grid1.RemoveItem
            IsPNExits = True
            Exit Function
        End If
        frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Row = frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Row + 1
    Next
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub selectUpperBrackets()
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectUpperBrackets()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim TrackBracketsPN(9) As String
    Dim TrackBracketsPN1QTY(9) As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
    Dim lintUV As Integer
    
    'FOR HIGH LIFT USE UV = HL - 9
    '    VERTICAL LIFT & LHR - VL USE UV = DH + 18
    Select Case LiftType
        Case "High Lift"
            lintUV = HILIFT - 9
'        Case "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
'            lintUV = DrHgt + 18
        End Select
        

    If lintUV >= 45 And lintUV < 75 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 2
            
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
            
        CB = 2
        KN = 4
            
    ElseIf lintUV >= 75 And lintUV < 105 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 4
        
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
            
        CB = 4
        KN = 8
            
    ElseIf lintUV >= 105 And lintUV < 135 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 6
            
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
            
        TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(3) = "426-1032"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
        
        CB = 6
        KN = 12
            
    ElseIf lintUV >= 135 And lintUV < 165 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
        
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(3) = "426-1032"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(4) = "426-1033"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(4) = 2
        
        CB = 8
        KN = 16
            
    ElseIf lintUV >= 165 And lintUV < 195 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 10
        
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(3) = "426-1032"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(4) = "426-1033"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(4) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(5) = "426-1034"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(5) = 2
        
        CB = 10
        KN = 18
        
    ElseIf lintUV >= 195 And lintUV < 225 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 12
        
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(3) = "426-1032"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(4) = "426-1033"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(4) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(5) = "426-1034"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(5) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(6) = "426-1035"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(6) = 2
        
        CB = 12
        KN = 14
        
    ElseIf lintUV >= 225 And lintUV < 255 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 14
        
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(3) = "426-1032"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(4) = "426-1033"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(4) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(5) = "426-1034"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(5) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(6) = "426-1035"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(6) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(7) = "426-1036"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(7) = 2
        
        CB = 14
        KN = 16
        
    ElseIf lintUV >= 255 And lintUV < 285 Then
        TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
        
        TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1014"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(3) = "426-1032"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(4) = "426-1033"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(4) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(5) = "426-1034"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(5) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(6) = "426-1035"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(6) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(7) = "426-1036"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(7) = 2
        
        TrackBracketsPN(8) = "426-1037"
        TrackBracketsPN1QTY(8) = 2
        
        CB = 16
        KN = 18
    End If
    
    If TrackBracketsPN(0) <> "" Then
   
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
        
        Dim lintQty As Integer
        lintQty = Int(IsPNAlreadyExist(TrackBracketsPN(0)))
        
        If lintQty > 0 Then Call RollBack("Track", True, TrackBracketsPN(0))
        
        Dim i As Integer
        For i = 0 To 8
            If TrackBracketsPN(i) <> "" And TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i) <> Empty Then
                If i = 0 Then   'first index of array carrys the qty req. for the part no which already exit previously w/ other schedule
                    FillSummaryGrid TrackBracketsPN(i), TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i) + lintQty, "Track", GetSubCategory(TrackBracketsPN(i)), 0
                Else
                    FillSummaryGrid TrackBracketsPN(i), TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(TrackBracketsPN(i)), 0
                End If
            End If
        Next
        
    Else
        If lintUV >= 45 Then    'since UV falls under the min required therefore no message box needed. Only in case of unrestricted message box displayed
             MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Brackets. " & vbCrLf & _
            "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Brackets."
        End If
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub OperatorSelection()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".OperatorSelection()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim op_PN As String
    
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift", "Low Headroom Front Mount"
        
        If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strDynaHoistType = 1 Then
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-144" Then
                If DrHgt >= 78 And DrHgt <= 144 Then Call OperatorPartnum("805", "A")
            
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-216" Then
                If DrHgt >= 78 And DrHgt <= 174 Then Call OperatorPartnum("805", "1B")
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-384" Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "2C")
            End If
        
        ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strDynaHoistType = 0 Then
        
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-144" Then
                    If DrHgt >= 114 And DrHgt <= 144 Then Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "A")
                    
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-216" Then
                If DrHgt >= 114 And DrHgt <= 216 Then Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "1B")

            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-384" Then
                Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "2C")
            End If
        End If
    
    Case "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strDynaHoistType = 1 Then
            If DrHgt <= 144 Then
                Call OperatorPartnum("805", "2C")
            ElseIf DrHgt <= 174 Then
                Call OperatorPartnum("805", "3D")
            End If
        
        ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strDynaHoistType = 0 Then
            If DrHgt <= 144 Then
                Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "2C")
            ElseIf DrHgt <= 276 Then
                Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "3D")
            End If
        End If
        
    Case "High Lift"
    
        If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strDynaHoistType = 1 Then
         
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-54" Then
                Call OperatorPartnum("805", "1B")
                    
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-54" Then
                If HILIFT <= 32 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "1B")
                   
                ElseIf HILIFT >= 33 And HILIFT <= 54 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "2C")
                End If
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D575-120" Then
                If HILIFT <= 104 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "2C")
                   
                ElseIf HILIFT >= 105 And HILIFT <= 120 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "3D")
                
                End If
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-120" Then
                If HILIFT <= 78 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "3D")
                   
                ElseIf HILIFT > 78 Then
                    '''''''NOTHING
                End If
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D6375-164" Then
                If HILIFT <= 74 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "2C")
                   
                ElseIf HILIFT >= 75 And HILIFT <= 128 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("805", "3D")
                    
                ElseIf HILIFT > 128 Then
                    '''''''NOTHING
                End If
                
            End If
        
        ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strDynaHoistType = 0 Then
            
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-54" Then
                Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "1B")
                    
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-54" Then
                If HILIFT <= 32 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "1B")
                   
                ElseIf HILIFT >= 33 And HILIFT <= 54 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "2C")
                End If
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D575-120" Then
                If HILIFT <= 104 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "2C")
                   
                ElseIf HILIFT >= 105 And HILIFT <= 120 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "3D")
                
                End If
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-120" Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "3D")
    
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D6375-164" Then
                If HILIFT <= 74 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "2C")
                   
                ElseIf HILIFT >= 75 And HILIFT <= 164 Then
                    Call OperatorPartnum("8805", "3D")
                End If
                
            End If
        End If
    End Select
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Private Sub OperatorPartnum(ByVal opType, ByVal OpLetter)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".OperatorPartnum()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim PN_Notes As String
    Dim PN_KeyedShaftNotes As String
    Dim op_PN As String
    
    PN_Notes = "Headroom required for 805/8805 = 4-1/2" & Chr(34) & " above shaft center " & vbNewLine & _
                "Sideroom required for 805/8805 = 4" & Chr(34) & " from headplate"
                
    PN_KeyedShaftNotes = "1/4" & Chr(34) & " X 3/8" & Chr(34) & " key (436-0096) is required"
    
    If opType = "805" Then
        Select Case OpLetter
        Case "A"
           If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 1 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
            
            Call FillSummaryGrid("953-094", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-094"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

           ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-090", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-090"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

           ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-092", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-092"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

           End If

        Case "1B"
           If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 1 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-017", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-017"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

           ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then

               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-021", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-021"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))
           
           ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-014", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-014"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

           End If

        Case "2C"
            If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 1 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-018", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-018"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-022", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-022"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-015", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-015"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            End If

        Case "3D"
            If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 1 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-019", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-019"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-023", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-023"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-016", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-016"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            End If

        End Select

    ElseIf opType = "8805" Then
        Select Case OpLetter
        Case "A"
            If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-086", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-086"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-088", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-088"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            End If

        Case "1B"
            If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-074", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-074"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-076", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-076"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            End If

        Case "2C"
            If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-078", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-078"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-080", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-080"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            End If

        Case "3D"
            If varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 1 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-082", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-082"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            ElseIf varDoorData.DynaHoist.strBtnSelect = 0 And varDoorData.DynaHoist.strVoltageSelect = 0 Then
               Call FillSummaryGrid("953-084", 1, "Operator", GetSubCategory("953-084"), 0, PN_Notes & IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, vbNewLine & PN_KeyedShaftNotes, ""))

            End If

        End Select
    End If
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Sub
Public Function IsPNAlreadyExist(ByVal lstrPartNum As String, Optional blnTag As String) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".IsPNAlreadyExist()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'returns qty and the cut instructions for the same part no. in the grid
    Dim lintCount As Integer
    Dim lintQty As Integer
    Dim lstrCut As String
    
    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = 1
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
    
    Do While frmGrid.Grid1.Row <= frmGrid.Grid1.Rows - 1
        If lstrPartNum = frmGrid.Grid1.Text Then
            lintQty = frmGrid.Grid1.TextMatrix(frmGrid.Grid1.Row, 1)
            If blnTag = "HRZTrack" Then lstrCut = frmGrid.Grid1.TextMatrix(frmGrid.Grid1.Row, 4)
            Exit Do
        Else
            If frmGrid.Grid1.Row < frmGrid.Grid1.Rows - 1 Then
                frmGrid.Grid1.Row = frmGrid.Grid1.Row + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
            
        End If
    Loop
       
    If lintQty > 0 And blnTag = "HRZTrack" Then
        IsPNAlreadyExist = lintQty & "~" & lstrCut
    Else
        IsPNAlreadyExist = CStr(lintQty)
    End If
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function SelectAStopStock(ByVal L As Integer, ByVal lindex As Integer) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectAStopStock()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim Partno As String
    
    'This routine select stock part for Aluminum stop according a requested length
    
    Select Case lindex
        Case 2, 3  'complete / vertical black vinyl
            If (L <= 98) Then
                Partno = "328-231"
            ElseIf (L > 98) And (L <= 122) Then
                Partno = "328-232"
            ElseIf (L > 122) And (L <= 146) Then
                Partno = "328-233"
            ElseIf (L > 146) And (L <= 170) Then
                Partno = "328-234"
            ElseIf (L > 170) And (L <= 194) Then
                Partno = "328-235"
            Else    'L > 194
                Partno = "328-236"
            End If
            
    'Carmen 5.10.99 - New Steel W.S.
        Case 4, 5  'complete / vertical steel white vinyl
            If (L <= 86) Then
                Partno = "328-238"
            ElseIf (L > 86) And (L <= 98) Then
                Partno = "328-239"
            ElseIf (L > 98) And (L <= 110) Then
                Partno = "328-240"
            ElseIf (L > 110) And (L <= 122) Then
                Partno = "328-241"
            ElseIf (L > 122) And (L <= 146) Then
                Partno = "328-242"
            ElseIf (L > 146) And (L <= 170) Then
                Partno = "328-243"
            Else
                Partno = "328-244"
            End If
        End Select
       
    SelectAStopStock = Partno
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)


End Function

Private Function cut_HRZTrack_LHR(ByVal LHR_HTLen As Integer) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".cut_HRZTrack_LHR()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim cutLenHT As Integer
    Dim CutTo As Single
    Dim HTLen As String
    
    cutLenHT = CInt(DrHgt) - 6
                                        'rem amount no need to worry about
                                        'only show cut to's amount - UWA
    If LHR_HTLen <> cutLenHT Then
        If MeasU = 1 Then

            ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            HTLen = "Cut (4) to " & GetImpString(cutLenHT)
            HTLen = "Cut (4) horizontal track to " & GetImpString(cutLenHT)
            ''''''''''END Change in .NET
        Else
            Call ClearConObj
            aCon.Inch = cutLenHT
            aCon.mmcalc
            CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)

            ''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            HTLen = "Cut (4) to " & CutTo & " mm"
            HTLen = "Cut (4) horizontal track to " & CutTo & " mm"
            ''''''''''END Change in .NET
        End If
    End If
    cut_HRZTrack_LHR = HTLen
        
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Private Function cutInstructionJWeather(ByVal JWLen As Variant) As String
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".cutInstructionJWeather()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim lsngCutOff As Single
    Dim lsngcutLen As Single
    Dim CutTo As String
    Dim cutFromTop As String
    Dim cutFromBtm As String
    Dim cutOffJW As String
    Dim lsngBtm As Single
    Dim lintTop As Integer
    Dim cutLocation As String
    Dim lsngCutTo As Single
    Dim lsngC_Off As Single
           
    lsngcutLen = DrHgt + 3
    
    If DrHgt > 223 Then         'No cut to's req.
                                'Cut-off amounts
        If JWLen(1) <> "" Then
            lsngCutOff = (JWLen(0) + JWLen(1)) - lsngcutLen
        Else
            lsngCutOff = (JWLen(0) * 2) - lsngcutLen
        End If
        
        If lsngCutOff > 0 Then
            'cut from top & cut from btm
            If lsngCutOff <= 15 Then
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 9
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(9)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"
                
                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 9
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
            
            ElseIf lsngCutOff > 15 And lsngCutOff <= 20 Then
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 14
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(14)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"

                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 14
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
            
            ElseIf lsngCutOff > 20 And lsngCutOff <= 28 Then
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 21
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(21)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"

                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 21
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
            
            ElseIf lsngCutOff > 28 Then
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 26
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(26)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"

                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 26
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Else
        lsngCutOff = JWLen(0) - lsngcutLen
        If lsngCutOff > 0 Then
            
            'calculates cut to's amount
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                lsngCutTo = RoundFraction(lsngcutLen - Fix(lsngcutLen)) + Fix(lsngcutLen)
                CutTo = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(lsngCutTo)
            Else
                Call ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = lsngcutLen
                aCon.mmcalc
                CutTo = "Cut (2) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
            End If

            'cut from top & cut from btm
                
            If lsngCutOff <= 15 Then   'Changed on Dec 29 - Approved by Bill L
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 9
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(9)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"
                
                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 9
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
            
            ElseIf lsngCutOff > 15 And lsngCutOff <= 20 Then
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 14
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(14)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"

                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 14
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
                
            ElseIf lsngCutOff > 20 And lsngCutOff <= 28 Then
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 21
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(21)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"

                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 21
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
            
            ElseIf lsngCutOff > 28 Then
                'cut from top
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = 26
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                Else
                    cutOffJW = GetImpString(26)
                End If
                cutFromTop = cutOffJW & " from top"

                'cut from btm
                lsngBtm = lsngCutOff - 26
                If lsngBtm > 0 Then
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = lsngBtm
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        cutOffJW = Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"
                    Else
                        lsngC_Off = RoundFraction(lsngBtm - Fix(lsngBtm)) + Fix(lsngBtm)
                        cutOffJW = GetImpString(lsngC_Off)
                    End If
                    cutFromBtm = cutOffJW & " from bottom"
                End If
            End If
        End If ' cutoff > 0
    End If ' DH
    
    If DrHgt > 223 Then
        If cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
            cutLocation = "BOTH" & "~" & cutFromTop & "; " & cutFromBtm
        ElseIf cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm = "" Then
            cutLocation = "TOP" & "~" & cutFromTop
        ElseIf cutFromTop = "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
            cutLocation = "BTM" & "~" & cutFromBtm
        End If
    Else
        If cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
            cutLocation = "BOTH" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutFromTop & "; " & cutFromBtm
        ElseIf cutFromTop <> "" And cutFromBtm = "" Then
            cutLocation = "TOP" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutFromTop
        ElseIf cutFromTop = "" And cutFromBtm <> "" Then
            cutLocation = "BTM" & "~" & CutTo & "~" & cutFromBtm
        End If
    End If
    
    cutInstructionJWeather = cutLocation
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub PicklagPkg(QTY As Integer)

    'This routine will try to find a combination of packages of size
    '6, 8, 10 and 12 to make up a selection for given quantity Qty.
    
    Dim D1 As Integer, T1 As Integer, M As Integer
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Dim i As Integer
    
    For i = 0 To 3
        ResultLagQTY(i) = 0
    Next i
    
    If QTY < 18 Then
        QTY = 18
    ElseIf QTY = 22 Then
        QTY = 24
    ElseIf QTY = 30 Or QTY = 32 Or QTY = 34 Then
        QTY = 36
    ElseIf QTY = 50 Then
        QTY = 52
    End If
        
    T1 = QTY \ 2
    D1 = 14
    
    Flag = False
    Do While Not Flag
        M = T1 Mod D1
        Select Case M
        Case 0, 9, 10, 12
            If M = 0 Then
                If D1 = 14 Then
                    ResultLagQTY(3) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 12) Then
                    ResultLagQTY(2) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 10) Then
                    ResultLagQTY(1) = T1 \ D1
                Else
                    ResultLagQTY(0) = T1 \ D1
                End If
            Else
                If D1 = 14 Then
                    ResultLagQTY(3) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 12) Then
                    ResultLagQTY(2) = T1 \ D1
                ElseIf (D1 = 10) Then
                    ResultLagQTY(1) = T1 \ D1
                Else
                    ResultLagQTY(0) = T1 \ D1
                End If
                    
                If (M = 9) Then
                    ResultLagQTY(0) = (T1 Mod D1) \ M
                ElseIf (M = 10) Then
                    ResultLagQTY(1) = (T1 Mod D1) \ M
                Else
                    ResultLagQTY(2) = (T1 Mod D1) \ M
                End If
            End If
            Flag = True
        Case Else
            D1 = D1 - 1
            If D1 < 9 Then
                If (T1 - 8) > 0 Then
                    T1 = T1 - 14
                    ResultLagQTY(3) = ResultLagQTY(3) + 1
                ElseIf (T1 - 7) > 0 Then
                    T1 = T1 - 12
                    ResultLagQTY(2) = ResultLagQTY(2) + 1
                ElseIf (T1 - 6) > 0 Then
                    T1 = T1 - 10
                    ResultLagQTY(1) = ResultLagQTY(1) + 1
                Else
                    ResultLagQTY(0) = ResultLagQTY(0) + 1
                    Flag = True
                End If
                If Not Flag Then
                    D1 = 14
                End If
            End If
        End Select
    Loop
End Sub
Public Sub SetTrackOptionsGrid(PN As String, QTY As Integer, Group As String, SubGroup As String, Optional Var)
                                                                                                                                                                              
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SetTrackOptionsGrid()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    If frmTrackEntry.Grid2.TextMatrix(1, 0) = "" And frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Rows = 2 Then
'        gintRow = 1
    Else
        frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Rows = frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Rows + 1
    End If
    
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Row = frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Rows - 1
       
    'frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Row = gintRow
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Col = 0
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Text = PN
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Col = 1
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Text = QTY
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Col = 2
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Text = Group
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Col = 3
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Text = SubGroup
                                                     
    frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Col = 4
        
'    gintRow = frmTrackEntry.Grid2.Row

    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
  End Sub
