'Private Function ExtShaft() As Single    'shaft outside the headplate
Public Function ExtShaft(ByVal pstrStamping As String, ByVal HW As Single) As Single    'shaft outside the headplate

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".ExtShaft()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

'    If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D6375-164" Or _
'        varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-120" Then
'        If HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
'            ExtShaft = 8.5
'        End If
'    Else
'        If HWType = 2 Then
'            ExtShaft = 6
'        ElseIf HWType = 3 Or HWType = 3.25 Then
'            ExtShaft = 6.5
'        End If
'    End If
    If pstrStamping = "D6375-164" Or _
        pstrStamping = "D800-120" Then
        If HW = 3 Or HW = 3.25 Then
            ExtShaft = 8.5
        End If
    Else
        If HW = 2 Then
            ExtShaft = 6
        ElseIf HW = 3 Or HW = 3.25 Then
            ExtShaft = 6.5
        End If
    End If

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
Public Function GetFloorToShaftCenter()
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetFloorToShaftCenter()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Select Case LiftType
    Case "Standard Lift"
    
        Select Case varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping
        Case "D400-96", "D400-144"
        
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                
                Select Case HRadius
                Case 12
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 10.4375
                Case 16
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 13.4375
                End Select
                
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 14.5
            End Select
        
        Case "D525-216" '
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                
                Select Case HRadius
                Case 12
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 11
                Case 16
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 14
                End Select
                
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 15.5
            End Select
        
        Case "D800-384" '
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                
                Select Case HRadius
                Case 12
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 11.375
                Case 16
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 14.375
                End Select
                
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 16.125
            End Select
        End Select
        
    Case "High Lift"
        
        Select Case varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping
        Case "D400-54"
        
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + HILIFT + 4.4375
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + HILIFT + 5.5
            End Select
            
        Case "D525-54" '
            
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + HILIFT + 5
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + HILIFT + 6.5
            End Select
            
        Case "D575-120" '
            
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + HILIFT + 5.375
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + HILIFT + 7.125
            End Select
            
        Case "D6375-164", "D800-120"
            
            Select Case HWType
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + HILIFT + 12.125
            End Select
        End Select
         
    Case "Vertical Lift"
        
        Select Case varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping
            Case "D850-132", "D1100-216", "D1150-236"
                
                Select Case HWType
                Case 2, 3, 3.25
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = 2 * DrHgt + 16
                End Select
                
            Case "D1350-336"
                Select Case HWType
                Case 2, 3, 3.25
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = 2 * DrHgt + 20.5
                End Select
        End Select
        
    Case "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
                
        Select Case varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping
            Case "D850-132", "D1100-216", "D1150-236"
                
                Select Case HWType
                Case 2, 3, 3.25
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = 2 * DrHgt + 4
                End Select
                
            Case "D1350-336"
                Select Case HWType
                Case 2, 3, 3.25
                    GetFloorToShaftCenter = 2 * DrHgt + 5.5
                End Select
                
        End Select
    
    Case "Low Headroom Front Mount"
        
        Select Case varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping
        Case "D400-96", "D400-144"
        
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 8.4375
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 9.625
            End Select
            
        Case "D525-216"
            
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 9
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 9.625
            End Select
        
        Case "D800-384" '
            
            Select Case HWType
            Case 2
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 9.375
            Case 3, 3.25
                GetFloorToShaftCenter = DrHgt + 12.5625
            End Select
        End Select
       
    End Select
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function
''''''''''END Change in .NET


Public Function GetAvailableShaftSpace(ByVal HW As Single, ByVal DRW As Single, ByVal pstrStamping As String, ByVal pintCoupl As Integer, ByVal psngCoupl As Single) As Single
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetAvailableShaftSpace()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Select Case HW
    Case 2
        GetAvailableShaftSpace = DRW + 3 - (2 * (GetDrumWidth(pstrStamping))) - (IIf(pintCoupl > 0, 1, 0) * psngCoupl)
    Case 3, 3.25
        GetAvailableShaftSpace = DRW + 4 - (2 * (GetDrumWidth(pstrStamping))) - (IIf(pintCoupl > 0, 1, 0) * psngCoupl)
    End Select

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

    Dim llngErrNo As Long

    '    VERTICAL LIFT USE UWA = DH +12
    '    VERTICAL LIFT (LHR) USE UWA = DH

            If HILIFT >= 54 And HILIFT < 84 Then
                UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"

        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Wall Track Clips."

    End If

    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub
Public Function RoundFractionToHalfInch(ByVal psngPart As Single) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".RoundFractionToHalfInch()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Select Case psngPart
    Case 0
    RoundFractionToHalfInch = 0
       
    Case Is <= 0.5
    RoundFractionToHalfInch = 0.5
        
    Case Is <= 1
    RoundFractionToHalfInch = 1
    
    End Select

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetMinCeilingHeight() As Single

On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetMinCeilingHeight()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim ReqHRAmt As Single      ' calculation purposes
    Dim ReqHRCal As Single      ' display format
        
    If LiftType = "Standard Lift" Then
        If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-144" Then
            If HWType = 2 Then
                If HRadius = 12 Then
                    ReqHRCal = 13
                Else
                    ReqHRCal = 16.5
                End If
            Else
                ReqHRCal = 18
            End If
        ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-216" Then
            If HWType = 2 Then
                If HRadius = 12 Then
                    ReqHRCal = 14.5
                Else
                    ReqHRCal = 18.5
                End If
            Else
                ReqHRCal = 19
            End If
    
        ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-384" Then
             If HWType = 2 Then
                If HRadius = 12 Then
                    ReqHRCal = 16.5
                Else
                    ReqHRCal = 19.5
                End If
             Else
                ReqHRCal = 21
             End If
        End If
        
        GetMinCeilingHeight = DrHgt + ReqHRCal
    Else
        If LiftType = "High Lift" Then
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-54" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 7.5
                Else
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 9.5
                End If
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-54" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 9
                Else
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 10.5
                    
                End If
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D575-120" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 10
                Else
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 12
                End If
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D6375-164" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 16
                Else
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 18
                End If
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-120" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 17
                Else
                    ReqHRAmt = HILIFT + 18.5
                End If
            End If
        
            GetMinCeilingHeight = DrHgt + ReqHRAmt
    
        ElseIf LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Then
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D850-132" Or _
                varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1100-216" Or _
                varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1150-236" Then
                
                    ReqHRAmt = DrHgt + 22
            
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "1350-336" Then
                    
                    ReqHRAmt = DrHgt + 24   'need to verify
                    
            End If
            
            GetMinCeilingHeight = DrHgt + ReqHRAmt
    
        ElseIf LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
             If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D850-132" Or _
                varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1100-216" Or _
                varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1150-236" Then
                
                    ReqHRAmt = DrHgt + 10
            
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "1350-336" Then
                    
                    ReqHRAmt = DrHgt + 9   'need to verify
                    
            End If
            
            GetMinCeilingHeight = DrHgt + ReqHRAmt
    
        ElseIf LiftType = "Low Headroom Front Mount" Then
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-144" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRCal = 10.5
                Else
                    ReqHRCal = 13
                End If
            
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-216" Or varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-384" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRCal = 11.25
                Else
                    ReqHRCal = 15.5
                End If
            End If
            
            GetMinCeilingHeight = DrHgt + ReqHRCal
    
        ElseIf LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
            If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D400-144" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRCal = 4.5
                Else
                    ReqHRCal = 7.5
                End If
        
            ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D525-216" Or varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D800-384" Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    ReqHRCal = 5
                Else
                    ReqHRCal = 7.5
                End If
            End If
            
            GetMinCeilingHeight = DrHgt + ReqHRCal
        End If
    End If
    
    
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
End Function


Function cut_UWA_VL_VLLHR(Optional ByVal UWA_Len As Integer, Optional ByVal UWA_Qty As Integer) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".cut_UWA_VL_VLLHR()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim cutLenUWA As Single
    Dim CutTo As Single
    Dim UWALen As String
    
    Select Case LiftType
        
    Case "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
        
        If LiftType = "Vertical Lift" Then
            If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                
                If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1150-236" Then
                    
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 16
                    
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 14
                
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D850-132" Or varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1100-216" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 21
                    
                End If
                
            Else
            
               If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1150-236" Then
                    
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 7
                    
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 5
                
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D850-132" Or varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1100-216" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 12
                    
                End If
            End If
        
        ElseIf LiftType = "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
            If Not varDoorData.Track.strMountType = "EU ADCA" Then
                
                If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1150-236" Then
                    
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 4
                    
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 2
                    
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D850-132" Or varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1100-216" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt + 9
                    
                End If
            Else
            
                If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1150-236" Then
                    
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt - 5
                    
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt - 7
                    
                ElseIf varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D850-132" Or varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1100-216" Then
                
                    cutLenUWA = DrHgt
                    
                End If
            End If
        End If
        
        If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1.25 Then cutLenUWA = cutLenUWA - 3
        
                                        
        If cutLenUWA <> UWA_Len Then                                ' only show cut to's amount - UWA
            If UWA_Qty = 1 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then

                    UWALen = "Cut (2) " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA)
                        
                Else
                    Call ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = cutLenUWA
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                    UWALen = "Cut (2) " & CutTo & " mm"
                End If
                
            ElseIf UWA_Qty = 2 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    
                   UWALen = "Cut (2) to " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA)
                                      
                Else
                    Call ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = cutLenUWA
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                    
                   UWALen = "Cut (2) to " & CutTo & " mm"
                End If
            
            ElseIf UWA_Qty = 4 Then
            
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    
                    UWALen = "Cut to Total Length of " & GetImpString(cutLenUWA)
                    
                Else
                    Call ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = cutLenUWA
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    CutTo = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                    
                    UWALen = "Cut to Total Length of " & CutTo & " mm"
                    
                End If
            
            End If
        End If
    End Select
    
    cut_UWA_VL_VLLHR = UWALen
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)


End Function
Public Function GetBracketMountC_VL_VLLHR() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBracketMountC()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim BracketMountC As Single
    
    Select Case LiftType
                   
        Case "Vertical Lift"
        
            If Not varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
                BracketMountC = (DrHgt + 17) * 0.1051 + bValue
    
            Else
                BracketMountC = (DrHgt + 17) * 0.05255 + bValue
            End If
        
            Select Case HWType
                Case 2
                        If BracketMountC < 12 Then BracketMountC = 12
                    
                Case 3, 4
                        If BracketMountC < 11.0625 Then BracketMountC = 11.0625
            End Select
            
        Case "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
            
            If Not varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
                 BracketMountC = (DrHgt + 5) * 0.1051 + bValue
    
            Else
                 BracketMountC = (DrHgt + 5) * 0.05255 + bValue
            End If
            
            Select Case HWType
                Case 2
                    If BracketMountC < 12.125 Then BracketMountC = 12.125
                    
                Case 3, 3.25
                    If BracketMountC < 11.21875 Then BracketMountC = 11.21875
            End Select
    End Select
    
    GetBracketMountC_VL_VLLHR = BracketMountC

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

Public Sub selectUWATrackCips_VL_LHRVL()

            KN = 6
          
        ElseIf lintUWA >= 135 And lintUWA < 165 Then
            UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
            UWA_Qty(0) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
            UWA_Qty(1) = 2

            UWA_Qty(2) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(3) = "426-1034"
            UWA_Qty(3) = 2
            
            CB = 8
            KN = 8
              
        ElseIf lintUWA >= 165 And lintUWA < 195 Then
            UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
            UWA_Qty(0) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
            UWA_Qty(1) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(2) = "426-1033"
            UWA_Qty(2) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(3) = "426-1034"
            UWA_Qty(3) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(4) = "426-1035"
            UWA_Qty(4) = 2
            
            CB = 10
            KN = 10
        
        ElseIf lintUWA >= 195 And lintUWA < 225 Then
            UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
            UWA_Qty(0) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
            UWA_Qty(1) = 2

            UWA_Qty(5) = 2
            
            CB = 12
            KN = 12
        
        ElseIf lintUWA >= 225 And lintUWA < 255 Then

            UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
            UWA_Qty(1) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(2) = "426-1033"
            UWA_Qty(2) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(3) = "426-1034"
            UWA_Qty(3) = 2
            
            UWA_PN(4) = "426-1035"

            UWA_PN(6) = "426-1037"
            UWA_Qty(6) = 2
            
            CB = 14
            KN = 14

    End If
    
   
            
    If UWA_PN(0) <> "" Then
        
        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
        
        Dim i As Integer
        For i = 0 To 6
            If UWA_PN(i) <> "" And UWA_Qty(i) <> Empty Then
                FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN(i), UWA_Qty(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN(i)), 0
            Else
                Exit Sub
            End If
        Next
    Else
        If lintUWA >= 45 Then   'since HL falls under the required mim value(45"), no message box displayed
            MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Wall Track Clips. " & vbCrLf & _
            "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Wall Track Clips."
        End If
        
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)

    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub
Public Sub selectUpperBrackets_VL_VLHRVL()
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectUpperBrackets_VL_VLHRVL()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim TrackBracketsPN(9) As String
    Dim TrackBracketsPN1QTY(9) As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
    Dim lintUV As Integer
    
    'FOR HIGH LIFT USE UV = HL - 9
    '    VERTICAL LIFT & LHR - VL USE UV = DH + 18
    Select Case LiftType
'        Case "High Lift"
'            lintUV = HILIFT - 9
        Case "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
            lintUV = DrHgt + 18
        End Select
        
    If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
    
        If lintUV >= 45 And lintUV < 75 Then
            TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 2
                
            TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1031"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
                
            CB = 2
            KN = 4
                
        ElseIf lintUV >= 75 And lintUV < 105 Then
            TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 4
            
             TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1031"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
            
            TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
                
            CB = 4
            KN = 6
                
        ElseIf lintUV >= 105 And lintUV < 135 Then
            TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 6
                
            TrackBracketsPN(1) = "426-1031"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(1) = 2
                
            TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1032"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 4
            
            
            CB = 6
            KN = 8
                
        ElseIf lintUV >= 135 And lintUV < 165 Then
            TrackBracketsPN(0) = "111-1470"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(0) = 8
            
            TrackBracketsPN(2) = "426-1031"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(2) = 2
            
            TrackBracketsPN(3) = "426-1032"
            TrackBracketsPN1QTY(3) = 4

        ReqQty(0) = ReqQty(0) + CB
        ReqQty(1) = ReqQty(1) + KN
        
        Dim lintQty As Integer
        lintQty = Int(IsPNAlreadyExist(TrackBracketsPN(0)))
        
        If lintQty > 0 Then Call RollBack("Track", True, TrackBracketsPN(0))
        
        Dim i As Integer
        For i = 0 To 8
            If TrackBracketsPN(i) <> "" And TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i) <> Empty Then
                If i = 0 Then   'first index of array carrys the qty req. for the part no which already exit previously w/ other schedule
                    FillSummaryGrid TrackBracketsPN(i), TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i) + lintQty, "Track", GetSubCategory(TrackBracketsPN(i)), 0
                Else
                    FillSummaryGrid TrackBracketsPN(i), TrackBracketsPN1QTY(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(TrackBracketsPN(i)), 0
                End If
            End If
        Next
        
    Else
        If lintUV >= 45 Then    'since UV falls under the min required therefore no message box needed. Only in case of unrestricted message box displayed
             MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Brackets. " & vbCrLf & _
            "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Brackets."
        End If
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub selectUpperWallAngle_VL_LHRVL()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectUpperWallAngle()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim UWA_PN(1) As String
    Dim UWA_Qty(1) As Integer
    Dim UWA_Len(1) As Integer

    UWA_Len(1) = 0

    Select Case LiftType
        
    Case "Vertical Lift"

        If HWType = 2 Then
           
            If DrHgt <= 10.5 Then
                UWA_PN(0) = "425-0511"

            ElseIf DrHgt > 286 And DrHgt <= 304 Then
                UWA_PN(0) = "425-0554"
                UWA_Len(0) = 141
                UWA_Qty(0) = 2
                
                UWA_PN(1) = "425-0555"
                UWA_Len(1) = 165
                UWA_Qty(1) = 2
            
            ElseIf DrHgt > 304 And DrHgt <= 328 Then
                UWA_PN(0) = "425-0554"
                UWA_Len(0) = 141
                UWA_Qty(0) = 2
                
                UWA_PN(1) = "425-0556"
                UWA_Len(1) = 189
                UWA_Qty(1) = 2
                
            ElseIf DrHgt > 328 And DrHgt <= 336 Then
                UWA_PN(0) = "425-0554"
                UWA_Len(0) = 141
                UWA_Qty(0) = 2
                
                UWA_PN(1) = "425-0557"
                UWA_Len(1) = 213
                UWA_Qty(1) = 2
                
            End If
        End If
    End Select
    
'    If UWA_PN <> "" Then
'        cutUWA = cut_UWA_VL_VLLHR(UWA_Len, UWA_Qty)
'        If cutUWA <> "" Then
'            FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN, UWA_Qty, "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN), 0, cutUWA
'        Else
'            FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN, UWA_Qty, "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN), 0
'        End If
'    Else
'        MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Wall Angle. " & vbCrLf & _
'        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Wall Angle. "
'    End If
    
    UWA_Len_VL = UWA_Len(0) + UWA_Len(1)
    UWA_Qty_VL = UWA_Qty(0) + UWA_Qty(1)
    
    
    cutUWA = cut_UWA_VL_VLLHR(UWA_Len_VL, UWA_Qty_VL)
    
    If UWA_PN(0) <> "" Then
                
        Dim i As Integer
        For i = 0 To 1
            If UWA_PN(i) <> "" And UWA_Qty(i) <> Empty Then
                
                If i = 0 Then
                    If cutUWA <> "" Then
                    
                        FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN(i), UWA_Qty(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN(i)), 0, cutUWA
                    Else
                    
                        FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN(i), UWA_Qty(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN(i)), 0
                    
                    End If
                    
                    
                End If
                
                If i = 1 Then
                
                    If cutUWA <> "" Then
                    
                        FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN(i), UWA_Qty(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN(i)), 0
                    Else
                    
                        FillSummaryGrid UWA_PN(i), UWA_Qty(i), "Track", GetSubCategory(UWA_PN(i)), 0
                    
                    End If
                    
                End If
                
            Else
                Exit Sub
            End If
        Next
    Else
'         MsgBox "Cannot select Upper Wall Angle. " & vbCrLf & _
'        "Please consult manufacturer for this door size!", vbApplicationModal + vbInformation, "Cannot Select Upper Wall Angle. "
        
    End If
        
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub selectEU_UWATrackClips_VL_VLLHR()
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".selectEU_UWATrackClips()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'subroutine is being used for EU ADCA
    Dim UWA_PN(7) As String
    Dim UWA_Qty(7) As Integer
    Dim lintUWA As Integer
    Dim CB As Integer
    Dim KN As Integer
       
    'FOR HIGH LIFT USE UWA = HL - 18
    '    VERTICAL LIFT USE UWA = DH +12
    '    VERTICAL LIFT (LHR) USE UWA = DH
    
    
 
        Select Case LiftType
                        
            Case "Vertical Lift", "Low Headroom Vertical Lift"
            
                If varDoorData.aDrum.strStamping = "D1350-336" Then
                
                    If DrHgt >= 24 And DrHgt < 54 Then
                        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
                        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
                                
                        CB = IIf(HWType = 2, 2, 4)
                        KN = 2
                  
                    ElseIf DrHgt >= 54 And DrHgt < 84 Then
                        UWA_PN(0) = "426-1031"
                        UWA_Qty(0) = 2
                        
                        UWA_PN(1) = "426-1032"
                        UWA_Qty(1) = 2

                        KN = 6

                    End If
                    
                End If

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub
